- **Now**: cards.bounty `draft` = that table (105 cards: 56 next-battle incl. 源石虫·特训, 42 two-battle, 7 multi-round), drawn uniformly, no multi-round cap — **superseded by §21.2** (the official draft structure of each round); bounty cards carry `descRaw`; the effects column says 还剩 N 场作战 (`counterText`). 教鞭 draws 3 战术特训 cards and takes one at random (it used to draw the band bounties, so 战术特训 was unreachable); “神秘顾客” (granted by nothing in act2) keeps the band bounties [ASSUMED]. Not modelled: the one official draft screenshot hints at one series per draft.
- **Settled by the user** — "我不记得有过多轮悬赏": until confirmed otherwise every official multi-round card (山海众头目·多轮悬赏, 多轮悬赏·假想敌 ×6, and 法术大师A2·多轮战术特训 from 教鞭) **lasts two battles** exactly like the "接下来两场作战" cards (no draft offers one since §21.2; 教鞭's card still lasts two battles): `MULTI_ROUND_BOUNTY_BATTLES = 2` (`bountyBattles`; `bountyText` rewrites "之后的 / 后续<@ba.vdown>每场</>作战" to "接下来<@ba.vup>两场作战</>" on the card and in the effects column, 还剩 N 场作战); choices.json keeps the official `rounds` 99 / `multiRound`. Flip: `MULTI_ROUND_BOUNTY_BATTLES = null` restores the official red 每场 everywhere (no data rebuild).
- **#7 seen**: the leaker's number never fell during 联防 and seemed capped at 10. **Official**: PRTS 帮助 "防卫失败的玩家可通过上方信息栏确认自身所属敌人的剩余数量"; the capsule shows an orange runner tag ×N (research 09 `tag_miss`, drawn with ui/battle bg_miss_enemy [ASSUMED]); the 10-LP cap per round is unchanged. **Now** (revising §17.5): `uniteLeft` per leaker = not spawned yet + alive + counted leaks — it falls on kills and rises on splits / summons (children inherit `sourcePlayerId`); the server takes it from `b.progress left`, the headless timeline `[gt, killed, total, left]` or the exact `uniteSurvivors`, clamps live values to `uniteBillBounds`; m.public players[] `uniteLeft` is uncapped and `pendingLp` = min(cap, uniteLeft). ×N in the capsule and in the leakers' team rows; a client with the 联防 field on screen shows its local replica's counts there; a teammate's row tooltip names the teammate.
- **#19**: PRTS 帮助 "临时刷新3名高于调度中心等级1阶（最多6阶）的干员"; the official 玩法介绍 image shows three different operators at price 0. v2.4.1 rolled three times independently (14–21 % of offers had a duplicate). **Now** three different chess, copy-weighted; a tier short of different chess tops up from the tier below [ASSUMED]; the offer reserves no copies. The integration QA found the same repeat in the other free pick-one chess offers — 信标 (same tier), 寻呼模块 (same bond ≤ shop level, content/items/meta.js) and the engine default `builtinMeta use_equip_reward_special_goods_char_chess` — they now draw different operators too (信标 tops up from the tier below, 寻呼模块 shows fewer cards when the pool has no other one), and `pushRewardOffer` drops a repeated id from any list it is given (`test/content/items.test.js`).
- Tests: `test/match/playtest6-matchflow.test.js` (the draft, two battles for a multi-round card end to end, 联防 counts, rewards), `test/match/followups.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest6-unite.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest6-bounty.test.js`, `test/content/items.test.js` (教鞭).

### 20.7 机变 card text; the shop's confirming tap on phones (#6, #10) — `ui/choiceOverlay.js`, `css/screens/game-panels.css`, `ui/shopBar.js`, `css/screens/game-shop.css`, `css/devices.css`

- **#6 seen** (screenshot 2, Android 756×366 CSS px, 终极 co-op): the 道具补给 cards showed only icon, name and tier. **Cause**: the card was a centred column sized for 16:9; with the root font clamped at 40 px a 3×2 card was 105 px tall and showed 0.2 lines of text (217 of 223 texts cut), in all four families; at 1920×1080 nine long texts were cut at 4 lines. **Official** (research 06 §11.5; screenshots Mb2mtd9 / Wrkhtxv / SEbySVl): a framed icon at the top-left with the bold name beside it, the description below, numbers in blue, the tier chip on the icon's corner, the taker's round avatar alone at the top-right. **Now**: that layout; the text from `cardText` (the card's own `descRaw`, else the data `descRaw` while its plain form equals the server's `desc`, else the server text), clamped to 7 lines (8 at line height 1.3 on phones under 700 px wide: with Noto Sans SC — the page's web font, metrics of Android's CJK font — 海沟实验体 wraps to 8 lines at 640×360, residual QA); short screens get a tighter overlay header (no CONTINGENCY micro line) and .19rem text; the taker badge is the avatar alone (name in its tooltip and the card's aria-label) and the taken card's head reserves its width. All 223 texts (51 items, 129 bounty, 43 tactic) fit in full, taken or not, from 640×360 to 2560×1080, measured with Noto Sans SC.
- **#10 seen**: on the phone the second tap on the 确认购买 strip did nothing; only the middle of the card worked. **Cause**: the card's invisible ⓘ corner had the generic 44 px touch area (its 30 px override lost on specificity: `:is()` scores 0,3,1 against 0,2,1), covering the bottom-right quarter of the card and the right half of the strip (120 / 144 grid points); below 768 px the left-docked detail card opened by the first tap also reached over the bar's first cards. **Official** (research 09 §5): first tap = detail + 确认购买, second tap buys; the card has no info corner. **Now**: the ⓘ is removed (a tap anywhere on a card is the card's tap), `.shopbar.has-armed { z-index: 10 }` keeps the armed bar above the detail card (z 9; below `.uframe` 12 and `.spov` 20), and a card that cannot be bought right now opens its detail (not aria-disabled, help cursor). 144 / 144 points on touch at 640×360 … 932×430 and with the mouse at 1920×1080; the strip centre, its right end and the bottom-right corner buy.
- [ASSUMED]: phone text 7.6 px; the avatar inside the card's corner; the gold strip may cover the last line of the longest texts while a card is selected; the micro line hidden on phones; the armed bar hides the detail card's corner on < 768 px phones; solo 3-card sizes.
- Tests: `test/ui/playtest6-ui.test.js`; opt-in `test/ui/playtest6-ui.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`: every text at 9 viewports, each card taken once, the hit grid by tap target and real taps); mock.e2e (detail + Esc through a card tap).

### 20.8 Element gauge on screen (#11) — `sim/damage.js elementView`, `render/units.js`, `render/textures.js`

- **Seen**: correcting playtest #5, the user said the 海嗣's element damage is right after all, but the gauge "fills at the wrong rate: far from used up when the burst already hit". **Measured** (Node sim → interp → UnitView, and headless Chrome on the real view; 14 element enemies vs 古米, the reviewer with 5 operators against a mixed 海嗣 wave): the drawn gauge follows the sim within one frame. Steady attackers burst off a 1–5 % sliver; big hits legitimately burst far up the bar (掠海漂移体 does 50 % of its ATK × the round's ATK per hit — 25 % of the gauge at base, ≈ 44 % at 绝境 R11; 萨卡兹王庭军精锐术师 死亡之眼; 昆图斯's skills).
- **Display faults, all fixed**: `elementView` rounded the fill to the nearest 1 % (a gauge 1–4 元素值 short showed empty); the v2.3 ring right of the bars sat on the next operator's tier chip (read as the neighbour's gauge, and a shrinking ring reads like a filling one); during the 爆发冷却 the refill was the same white as the 元素值 left while the burst's stun / damage hit the unit.
- **Official** (PRTS 元素): "当单位存在一项不为最大值的元素时，模型下部会显示对应的元素图标，并以白条显示剩余的元素值"; enemies get a small colour-only icon; during the 爆发冷却 "单位的元素条显示缓慢恢复至上限（动画效果）" (no colour given).
- **Now**: fill rounded DOWN to 1 %, kept at 0.01–0.99 outside a burst (1 during one) — the bar only runs out when the gauge bursts; the gauge is a row under the unit's own HP / SP bars inside their span: the element disc (0.15 tile, 8–15 px; enemies ×0.8, plain colour; light rim) and a white bar of the remaining 元素值 (SP-bar height, dark track); during the 爆发冷却 the bar refills in the element's colour (`ELEMENT_RING[el].tint`) with the disc pulsing, white again when it ends.
- [ASSUMED]: the refill colour and pulse; the geometry; placement under the above-head bars (official: under the model); the disc rim. The render-demo recordings carry fills made with the old rounding (display only).
- Tests: `test/sim/playtest6_elemgauge.test.js`, `test/render/playtest6_elembar.test.js`, `test/render/downring.test.js`; opt-in `test/render/elembar.browser.test.js`, `downelem.browser.test.js`.

### 20.9 Settled decisions and what stays [ASSUMED]

The user's answers of 2026-10-01 (playtest #6 follow-up):

| Decision | Settled rule | One-line flip |
|---|---|---|
| A placed skill summon at the battle start (§20.1) | deploys once, free, then with each cast (PRTS §作战阶段) | `shared/constants.js SKILL_SUMMON_START_DEPLOY = false` |
| 凯瑟琳's 爬行号·防护单元 (§20.1) | a manually placed hand card | drop `token_10041_cathy_catsld` from `placeable` (`tools/build-data.mjs`) — and restore the v2.4 kit placement, or the device never appears |
| Multi-round bounty cards (§20.6) | last two battles like "接下来两场作战", blue text — 教鞭's card; no draft offers one (§21.2) | `server/match/choices.js MULTI_ROUND_BOUNTY_BATTLES = null` (official 每场) |
| 萨卡兹枯朽战车's reach (§20.4) | data range 2.2 + the operator's 0.25 collider for every ranged enemy; scales per data (100 % ranged, 200 % blocked melee, skill 100 %) | `server/sim/constants.js ALLY_COLLIDER_RADIUS = 0` |
| Blocking (§20.3) | every blocker hits the enemies it blocks, first, whatever its range or facing | gate `Battle.blockedTargets` / `sortEnemyTargets` blocked-first on a melee position again |
| A merge's elite (§20.11) | takes the consumed deployed copy's tile and facing, else the hand (PRTS 帮助 "若消耗已部署至作战区的干员，则发送至作战区对应位置"; the user confirmed it, 2026-10-01) | none — official; restoring the v2.5 hand-first rule means reverting `PlayerState._mergeChess` (merge `5f3842f`) |

Official since the follow-up — read from the client binary (research 11, §20.12) or the official texts (§20.10, §20.13):

| Rule | Now | One-line flip |
|---|---|---|
| Bond layer cap (§20.12) | 999 per bond (`MAX_GARRISON_STACK`; `AddBondCount` stores min(L + n, 999)) — no longer [ASSUMED] | `shared/constants.js BOND_LAYER_CAP = 0` |
| 限伤 (§20.12) | a leader hit with ceil(d) ≥ 300000 in boss / hidden battles is cancelled (`MAX_BATTLE_DAMAGE`), not an overflow | `shared/constants.js BOSS_HIT_LIMIT = 0` |
| 直接乘算 (§20.10) | bond / strategy / item / 机变 / 特质 "+X%" are summed with each other and the skills' | `server/sim/constants.js DIRECT_BONUS_STACKING = 'multiply'` |
| Ground routes (§21.15) | the official flow field unless the playtest #2 route crosses strictly fewer floor tiles (a corner touch is no crossing) | `server/sim/grid.js _buildField` always `use = o` (reverses 战场#01's col-8 road) |
| Leader parts (§20.10, §20.13) | every damage a 剑 / 锤 / 碎铳之簧 takes is lost by its leader 1:1, 无来源 | `server/sim/content/bosses.js PART_TRANSFER = 0.5` (v2.5's share; v2.5 also passed a 剑 / 锤's damage only while grounded) |

Settled by the user ("保持固定血量"): the co-op leader pool does not shrink when players are eliminated — `config bossHpScale.aliveScaling` is **false** (pool = bloodPoint whatever the number of alive players; one community note, 巴哈姆特 12294, said otherwise) (§20.10); flip: `aliveScaling: true` (× alive / 4).

Settled by the user (2026-10-02, after v2.5.2: "共享阵地按你说的规则就行"): on a shared field (联防, Final Assault / Hidden Core) the bond strip follows the player of the half the ‹ › pill points at; on 全景 or an empty half it shows your own bonds when you fight there, else the teammate picked with 前往查看 or the field's first player — never your own (§20.15); rule: `public/js/ui/watchBonds.js bondOwnerId`.

Still [ASSUMED] (each in its section): the class rows covering every MANUAL skill (§20.2); skill-summon redeploys free and never while the owner is away (§20.1); the 1-HP pool floor and the server-order verdict (§20.5); a short promotion tier topping up from below and “神秘顾客” keeping the band bounties (§20.6); the 弹道 column for unclassified pushes and the other displacement details (§20.3); 枯朽战车's skill firing on its blocker at 100 % and content enemy skills keeping a point radius (§20.4); the gauge's refill colour and placement (§20.8); the 机变 card's phone sizes (§20.7); the elite's tile among several deployed copies (the first in deploy order) and the elite's fresh summon stack (§20.11; a transformed carrier's tile no longer counts, §21.1); "strongest" rather than "latest" for same-named debuffs, which "最大生命值" the 胄 drone link reads (the pool), the 剑 / 锤 dive hits passing on, the springs' split and the solo pool × 0.25 (§20.10); the scene server clamping prep gains like the client, a cancelled hit showing nothing, a passed-on loss being one hit and the order blocks → 限伤 → HP shields (§20.12); 胄's unread `max_hp` 0.5, the untargetable 初始模式 and a replacement copy inheriting only the HP (§20.13); talent debuffs keyed per source unit (two copies of one operator each apply theirs, §20.14).

### 20.10 Final Assault leader HP and damage (user report after playtest #6, 2026-10-01) — `server/sim/constants.js DIRECT_BONUS_STACKING`, `content/support directMods`, `sim/Battle.js applyStrongest / loseHp sourceless`, content bonds / bands / items / choices / garrisons / kits tier4, `content/bosses.js PART_TRANSFER / DRONE_LINK_BASE`, `match/gamedata.js bossPoolHp / bossPoolShare`, `match/finalAssault.js`, `data/config.json bossHpScale`

- **Seen**: co-op 终极 / 绝境 leaders died within seconds; with the user's layer counts the official leader never dies that fast.
- **HP and pool — official, unchanged**: `bloodPoint[difficulty]` = activity_table act2autochess bossInfoDict `bloodPoint` / `bloodPointNormal` / `bloodPointHard` / `bloodPointAbyss` of the current data (the cache equals upstream ArknightsGameData master as of 2026-10-01; `test/match/bosshp.test.js` pins all 40 values — e.g. 胄 247 500 / 675 000 / 1 800 000 / 3 600 000, hidden 胄 750 000 / 937 500 / 3 600 000 / 7 200 000). PRTS 盟约记录's leader table is an older (11月18日) revision: 铳 险境 and 胄 / 铳 / 萨米 绝境 differ, no 终极 column, no 卢西恩 row. The data has no per-player or per-field factor; leader HP ignores `enemyScale` ("领袖单位于服务器的生命值加成不受上述加成影响"). One pool for every boss field ("最终攻势中，所有人将一起对敌方领袖造成伤害"); notice 5114's "敌方领袖的总生命值不变" means the two mirrored copies of a pair field share it.
- **Pool size**: co-op = bloodPoint whatever the number of alive players — `bossHpScale.aliveScaling` (× alive / 4, one community note: 巴哈姆特 12294) stays **off** — the user chose the fixed pool (§20.9); solo × 0.25 [ASSUMED].
- **Fixed — why leaders fell too fast**:
  1. **直接乘算**: "+X%" ATK / DEF / max HP from bonds, strategies, equipment, 机变 cards and per-layer 特质 are summed with each other and with the skills' "+X%" (PRTS 盟约记录 "…属性加成均为直接乘算"; 游戏数据基础 D_t = Σtᵢ, A = (A₀ + D_p)(1 + D_t)). v2.5 compounded them (×74 instead of ×12 at 600 精准 layers with four more bonuses). `DIRECT_BONUS_STACKING 'add'`, content/support `directMods`; 炎佑 ×1.5 and the char_attribute_mul 特质 stay multipliers (§5.2).
  2. **Same-named enemy debuffs**: one instance per target, the strongest (PRTS 作战机制 "同名buff…只能表现出一个"; 巴哈姆特 12316 "共享型buff會跟對面搶") — 奥术, 灵知 坚冰, the 莱恩哈特 / 缄默德克萨斯 RES cuts (`Battle.applyStrongest`, §5.3). v2.5 kept one 奥术 per player, so a pair field multiplied it (×40 arts damage taken at +400 layers; ×7.6 now). "Strongest" rather than "latest" is [ASSUMED].
  3. **Leader parts**: 剑 / 锤 / 碎铳之簧 pass every damage they take 1:1 as 无来源 HP loss, credited to the attacker (PRTS "受到等量的无来源生命流失"; `PART_TRANSFER` 1, `loseHp … sourceless`); v2.5 passed ½, a 剑 / 锤 only while grounded. The dive hits counting (PRTS talent "受到伤害时" vs the handbook "被击落时") and the springs' split to the other springs are [ASSUMED] (§20.13 for the 剑 / 锤 kit).
  4. **坚守 thorns**: dealt 无来源 by the member who was hit and credited to that member (statistics only; up to 19 % of a co-op pool was nobody's).
  5. **Bond layers**: uncapped in v2.5; now 999 per bond — official, §20.12 (this workstream first added the cap as [ASSUMED] from the community; the merge kept the limits workstream's single implementation).
- **Kept**: a 胄 drone costs 2 % of the leader's shown max HP = the pool (`DRONE_LINK_BASE 'pool'`, = v2.5; which "最大生命值" the official client reads is [ASSUMED] — the review tried the unit's data 600 000, which made drones 19–78 % of a solo 标准 fight, and went back). Under 限伤 it is one hit: 144 000 at most today (hidden 胄 终极), cancelled only above a 14 999 950 pool (§20.12).
- **Ruled out by measurement**: double crediting (client / server / replica), multi-hits through the 5×3 hit area, element bursts, %-max-HP damage on leaders (only the drone link).
- **Official, not a bug**: per-layer bonuses have no cap below 999 (谢拉格 vs cold ×(1.35 + 0.01 L) = ×11.34, 奥术 at low HP ×15.7, 精准 +1208.8 % ATK at 999 layers), so very high layers still kill fast — 绝境 with +400 on every active bond: median 8.6 s, fastest 3.6 s; 终极 under 20 s needs ≈ +400 on every bond (≈ 2 800 active layers per player; median 16.8 s); with two bonds per player at 999 (≈ 2 000 layers) 绝境 kills median 10 s, 7 of 28 under 5 s; with every active bond at 999 终极 median 8.3 s, 绝境 3.7 s — and there 限伤 (§20.12) cancels 6–77 hits per 12–28 fights. The community reports the same (巴哈姆特 12522, 999 谢拉格 "boss一秒死").
- **Measured** (real seconds — the fights run at 2×, so half the game seconds of the QA tables). Integrated build (QA 6b on `cca11e6`, after the elite-to-board merge): the R14 boards of real 4-AI co-op matches, seeds 7 / 8 / 9 / 12 × boss_1…7 = 28 Final Assaults per row, both pair fields on one pool, the same boards replayed on v2.5 → now: 终极 own layers median 206 → 258 s, +200 on every active bond 24.5 → 42.1 s (fastest 8.2 → 18.9 s), +400 11.3 → 16.8 s; 绝境 own 79 → 99 s, +200 11.6 → 14.8 s (fastest 1.9 → 7.3 s), +400 6.1 → 8.6 s; each player's top bond requested at 1500 (now held at 999): 终极 53 → 95 s, 绝境 17.5 → 33 s. On each version's own bot boards the medians moved only ×1.0–1.2 (终极 own 256 → 258 s, +200 47 → 54 s; 绝境 own 81 → 99 s, +200 13 → 15 s): the elite-to-board rule (§20.11) gives the bots slightly stronger R14 boards (终极 2.12 → 2.26 elites per player, 绝境 2.14 → 2.51; 绝境 6.0 → 6.9 active bonds), so a player whose board did not change feels the same-board numbers. No landed leader hit reached 300 000 (v2.5 landed up to 18.6 M); the largest at +400 is 144 000 (a hidden 胄 drone; 114 000 from an operator), at 999 layers 299 600. The boss-HP workstream's own measurement (42 pre-merge boards, seeds 7–9: 终极 +200 30.9 → 56.0 s, …) is kept in BALANCE §3, labelled as such; BALANCE §3 has both tables.
- Tests: `test/match/bosshp.test.js` (real Final Assault / Hidden Core matches, the 40 bloodPoint values; the 奥术 pair test runs seed 12 since the elite-to-board merge changed the bots' boards), `test/sim/bosshp_stacking.test.js`, `test/match/layercap.test.js`, `test/sim/battle.test.js` (Battle.addLayers cap), `test/content/bonds_addon.test.js`, `kits_t4.test.js`, `kits_alt_t4.test.js`, `enemies_bosses.test.js`, `items.test.js`, `garrisons_battle.test.js`, `choices.test.js`, `test/match/unitStats.test.js`, `finalAssault.test.js`, `balance.test.js`, `playtest6-bosspool.test.js`; opt-in `SIM_E2E=1 node --test test/sim/bossfield.browser.test.js` (the real boss / hidden specs of a 999-layer bot match give the same digest, pool and fx in headless Chrome as in Node; covers §20.12 and §20.13 too).

### 20.11 A merge's elite takes the deployed copy's tile (user-confirmed) — `match/PlayerState.js _mergeChess / transformChess`, `match/board.js mergeTile`, `match/audit.js`, `ui/gameLogic.js mergeProgress / mergeTarget`, `ui/shopBar.js armedSlotOf / onArm`, `screens/game.js MERGE_HL`, `render/promote.js`, `render/fx.js promote`, `render/app.js setPrep`

- **Seen** (the user, first-hand, after playtest #6): "官方就是合成精锐时，如果消耗了场上的干员，精锐会出现在场上那个位置".
- **Cause**: research 01 A1 row 7 read PRTS 帮助 as "1 elite goes to the hand, not to a board tile" (marked VERIFIED, repeated in `01-core-data.json` promotion.eliteGoesTo), so `_mergeChess` put every elite in the hand and used a consumed copy's tile only when the hand and temp were both full.
- **Official** (PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助): §干员的获得与精锐化 "…发送1名【精锐】状态的该干员至手牌区（若消耗已部署至作战区的干员，则发送至作战区对应位置）"; tip "干员晋级后已配发装备会回收至整备区，需要重新配置"; §战斗部署 "如果部署的干员拥有可手动部署的附属召唤物，则该召唤物会立刻加入手牌区", deploy order "从上到下>从左到右". The text does not say which tile wins among several deployed copies.
- **Now (server)**: when a consumed copy stood on the board the elite takes that tile and its facing, otherwise it goes to the hand, overflowing into temp (outside PREP too). Several deployed copies: the one that deploys first — col asc, then row desc since 0.1.3 (§23.f4; row desc, then col asc before), the mirrored right boss half included — `board.js mergeTile(tiles, legal)` [ASSUMED]. A 突变细胞 transformation destroys its carrier before the gain, so the carrier's freed tile is no copy's (§21.1 — official footage; the 0.1.1 WA rule that counted it, `transformChess` `fromKey` / `fromDir` [ASSUMED], is withdrawn). A tile the elite may not use is skipped; with the hand and temp also full the elite stays on the first deployed copy's tile rather than being lost (as `_evictIllegal`). The deploy count never grows (no BOARD_FULL path). Equipment returns to the hand, overflow temp; with both full it stays on the elite up to its `equipPerChess` (2) slots, any further item destroyed with a log warning (unchanged). The consumed copies' summons are removed; an elite on the board gets its own fresh stack (`grantTokensFor`, after the items) following the §16 loadout [ASSUMED: placed summons are not carried over]. Every path (g.buy, g.reward, `ctx.grantChess`, 画卷, transformations), every phase (a SETTLE / Final Assault merge places the elite at once, its reward offer waits for the next prep) and the boss-field prep (legality on the player's half, §19.7). Reward offer, ticker and `stats.merges` unchanged; `onMerge` carries `area` ('board' | 'hand' | 'temp'). Bots plan the elite like any owned unit.
- **Now (client)**: `gameLogic.mergeTarget(priv, chessId)` mirrors `mergeTile`; a shop or reward card armed for a merge-completing buy lights the target tile in gold (`ShopBar onArm` → `screens/game.js` highlight group `mergeTile`); the 可晋升 tag says 精锐干员将出现在作战区原位置 or 精锐干员将进入整备区; an elite card never merges (`completesChessMerge` refuses `isGolden`, `mergeProgress` reports 0 copies: no pips, tag or tile); `render/promote.js promotionsOf` recognises a merge in setPrep and the elite plays `fx.promote` (gold pillar, shockwave, hex ring, streaks from the other copies) instead of `fx.deploy` (`view.debug.promotions`).
- **Checks**: `audit.js` checks every merge — the tile is `mergeTile` of the deployed copies legal for the elite, with that copy's facing; the deploy count did not grow; otherwise the elite is not on the board, except the no-room fallback. `tools/matchrun --check` sweeps are clean.
- Normative lines rewritten: §2 (`promote.js`), §6.2 (the economy line and the temp-overflow paragraph), §9 (the promotion cue).
- Tests: `test/match/playtest6-elite-to-board.test.js`, `test/match/merge.test.js`, `hardening.test.js`, `facing.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest6-elite.test.js`, `test/render/playtest6-promote.test.js`, `test/docs-consistency.test.js`; opt-in `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/playtest6-elite.e2e.test.js` (real server and prep: the armed card lights the deployed copy's tile, the elite stands there after the buy, 剩余可放置角色 unchanged).

### 20.12 Official limits: 999 layers per bond, 限伤 300000 on leaders — `shared/constants.js BOND_LAYER_CAP / BOSS_HIT_LIMIT / layerGainRoom`, `match/PlayerState.addLayers`, `Match` SETTLE, `sim/Battle.addLayers / loseHp`, `sim/damage.js leaderHitCancelled`, `match/fields.js validateClientResult`, `match/invariants.js`, `render/fx.js`, `tools/matchrun.mjs`, `tools/balance.mjs`

- **Asked**: the co-op leaders as hard to kill as in the official game. Research 11 (`docs/research/11-limits-official.md`) found two official caps in the client (2.7.71 il2cpp) that the remake lacked.
- **Official** [DATA]: `AutoChessBattleConst.MAX_GARRISON_STACK = 999` — `AddBondCount` stores `min(L + n, 999)`, per bond. `MAX_BATTLE_DAMAGE = 300000` — in step mode (every boss battle outside training) `AutoChessStepModeManager._OnBossEnemyTakeDamage` runs `modifier.Cancel()` when `ceil(value) ≥ 300000` on an `IsBossEnemy` unit; `IsBossEnemy` (disassembled, research 11 §2.3) = enemyId ∈ `autoChessData.bossInfoDict[*].enemyId` = data/bosses.json `enemyKey`. [COMM] bonds stay at 999 while fed; 「一次超过30w会被卡掉」: players avoid extra multipliers at 999 layers. 限伤 is a deliberate constant, **not** an engine fixed-point overflow — the boss-HP workstream's earlier reading (2³¹, "not modelled", §20.10) is superseded.
- **Now — layers**: the single implementation is `shared/constants.js BOND_LAYER_CAP` + `layerGainRoom(before, n)` = min(n, 999 − before), never negative (the boss-HP workstream's duplicate `sim/constants.js BOND_LAYER_CAP / layerRoom` was removed in the merge). Every writer uses it: `PlayerState.addLayers` (特质, items, bands, 机变 cards, bonds), the SETTLE of `BattleResult.layerGains`, `Battle.addLayers` (the live copy, clamped after the `layerGain` hook, like the client), and the dev tools' direct writes (`matchrun --layers N`, `balance.mjs applyBoard`). A gain at the cap adds 0: no onLayers, hook or 'layer' event, so the per-N milestones (远见, 奇迹, 维多利亚 …) stop with the count. Client results: per-bond `layerGains ≤ min(60 + 4·round, 999 − starting layers)` (+ the lineup's layer 特质, §21.26). `invariants.js` flags a bond above 999; the UI shows the server's capped count.
- **Now — 限伤**: kinds `'boss'` / `'hidden'` only; a leader is `isBoss` (the tag-'boss' units: those 10 keys and their mirrored copies — parts, 刺胄之弹, drones and escorts never count). A hit with `ceil(final) ≥ 300000` is cancelled: checked in `dealDamage` after DEF/RES and every multiplier, before HP shields, and in `loseHp` (a part's 传递, a drone's death). A cancelled hit causes no HP or pool loss, no credit or stats, no damage number, no `damaged` / `fatal` / kill; the attack, its SP, the `hit` hook and separate element 损伤 hits stay. Element bursts and DoT ticks are checked, gauge fills are not. The sim emits fx `hitCap {id, n}`, drawn as nothing (deterministic in Node and browsers; SP_VERIFY digests agree).
- **The line vs the pool**: 【死亡集群】 drone deaths cost 0.02 × the pool max (§20.10, §20.13) — at most 144 000 today (boss_8 终极 7.2M), so it lands; a pool above 14 999 950 would cancel every drone death. An alive-scaled pool (§20.9, open) can only shrink, but any larger pool must re-check it; a real-kit test pins the line.
- **[ASSUMED]**: the scene server clamps prep gains the same way; a cancelled hit shows nothing; a loss passed on to a leader is one hit; the order hit-step blocks → 限伤 → HP shields — 阿利斯泰尔's 莫非王土 vest (`boss:vest`, 5000): a hit of 300000–304999 is cancelled and the barrier stays; 假想敌：再生's blocks on 假想敌：管 (hidden h08_03) negate a hit past the line before the limit sees it.
- **Measured**: the competent and strong balance boards never reach the line (biggest leader hits 28k–72k), so BALANCE §3's model numbers stand (BALANCE §7); the co-op replays up to +400 per bond and a top bond at 999 cancel nothing either. Two bonds per player at 999 or every active bond at 999 do reach it: 6–18 cancelled hits per 28 Final Assaults and 14–77 per 12 Hidden Cores (QA 6b), and those kills take longer than with v2.5's uncapped hits (§20.10).
- **Flip**: `BOND_LAYER_CAP = 0` and / or `BOSS_HIT_LIMIT = 0` (or Infinity).
- Tests: `test/sim/playtest6_limits.test.js` (including the real-kit vest, 再生 and 【死亡集群】 cases), `test/match/playtest6-limits.test.js` (a 999-layer board through a real Final Assault + Hidden Core, both tools at the cap), `test/render/playtest6_hitcap.test.js`, `test/sim/playtest6_bosspool.test.js` (adapted), and the boss-HP workstream's `test/match/layercap.test.js` / `test/sim/battle.test.js` cap tests on the single API.

### 20.13 假想敌：胄 kit audit (PRTS) — `content/bosses.js kitHelm / kitBlade`

- **Audit** (a separate session, 2026-10-01), against the PRTS 技能 / 天赋 / 能力修正 texts and the skill blackboards:
  - 灭顶之灾 fires **one** 刺胄之弹 below 20 % too (PRTS 能力修正 原因 6 "描述与游戏实际表现不符合": no extra shell; no blackboard holds a second one), at the highest-ATK operator **within attack range** for both copies ("触发索敌和普通攻击相同"; boss_1 used the whole field).
  - 死亡集群: **any** drone death costs the leader 2 % max HP (= the pool, §20.10), credited to the killing operator if one killed it; a leak costs nothing (能力修正 "该无人机单位死亡时"). Drone HP: `summon.hp_ratio` (隐秘核心) scales it; skill 2's `max_hp` 0.5 is not read [ASSUMED] — no source defines it.
  - 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤: 初始模式 hovers invulnerable (untargetable [ASSUMED]) and attacks everything in range (锤 150 % ATK, unblockable). 掷 → 出击模式: blink to the start of the level's `<hand>_blink` route, fly at the lowest-ATK operator, no normal attack. 15 hits → 【瘫痪】: stun 10 s (its end ends 瘫痪, not the 20 s), ground unit, damage taken ×1.3. Landing or 瘫痪 ending: a new 初始模式 copy with the old HP is summoned on the `<hand>_origin` route and the old one is killed (a part: uncounted, no bounty) [ASSUMED: only the HP is inherited, so 掷 comes back after its initial 25 / 55 s instead of the 80 s cooldown].
  - Every damage a 剑 / 锤 takes passes 100 % to 胄 (PRTS "等量", also during the dive) — merged with §20.10 into the one `PART_TRANSFER` 1 (`BLADE_TRANSFER` is its alias), 无来源 and credited to the attacker.
- **Measured** (same-seed `balance.mjs --boss-only`, n = 3–5 per cell): boss_1 unchanged (≤ 0.5 s); boss_8 独立 绝境 44.7 → 45.2 s, 独立 终极 win 2/3 → 3/3, 同盟 绝境 23.3 → 23.7 s, 同盟 终极 72.0 → 64.2 s (BALANCE §3).
- Tests: `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (shell count and range, drone deaths and leaks, the pool-based link, the sortie / 瘫痪 / replacement cycle with the 1:1 无来源 transfer and its credit).

### 20.14 QA residuals of the integrated build (2026-10-02) — `match/Match.js _onResult / heldResult`, `render/app.js promoBase`, `ui/shopBar.js mergeHint`, `ui/detailPanel.js`, `screens/game.js dSide`

Two QA verifiers ran the integrated `playtest6b` (real 4-AI and 2-human co-op matches for every leader, Node vs headless Chrome digests, the merge UI on the user's phone profile and on desktop). Every check passed; the residual issues and what was done:

- **Fast boss kills handed over (fixed).** Under client-side combat a pair that empties the pool faster than the plausibility budget (one field credits ≤ the pool per `BOSS_MIN_CLEAR_GS` 5 game s, §14) had its `'cleared'` `b.result` rejected as implausible, the client demoted and the field handed to the partner or the server — seen at 绝境 with every bond at 999 (kills at 2.0–4.0 game s; official 999-layer play reports "boss一秒死"). Now a `'cleared'` result whose cumulative report covers what the pool holds (1 HP slack, the pool floor) is **held** (`f.heldResult`): the boss clock credits the report at the budget's rate, the silence watchdog and handovers skip the field, a re-sent copy is ignored, and the pool's end completes the field with it (`_endFinal`); a team-LP defeat first still credits nothing more. The budget itself is unchanged, so a forged report gains nothing a `b.progress` could not already get. The verdict waits at most until the budget covers the report (≤ 5 game s from the field's start). Re-run of the QA cases (绝境 seed 7 boss_1 / boss_6 with every bond pushed to 999, seed 21 with 990 early layers, 3 more at 999 / 终极): 0 rejections, 0 takeovers, every boss field kept by its client, Σ credited boss damage = the cleared pools; in headless Chrome (2 humans + 2 AI, a scratch server with a 12× slower budget so the path runs) both boss rounds held the clearing client's result and went on to the Hidden Core and the result with no takeover and no console error.
- **No promotion cue for a merge outside the prep (fixed).** A merge completed between two preps (a band / 机变 grant at ROUND_START, a SETTLE merge) placed the elite silently: `enterBattle` emptied the prep state the cue compares with. `render/app.js` keeps the last prep pieces across a battle or scouting board (`promoBase`), so the next `setPrep` plays `fx.promote` on the elite (streaks from the consumed copies' slots). Re-run of the QA case (松桐 grants the third 古米 at R4 ROUND_START): `view.debug.promotions` records the elite on the copy's tile, copies 2.
- **The 可晋升 destination on touch (fixed).** The tag's text was a native `title`, never shown on a phone. The detail card the first tap opens now says it under the header — 「可晋升：精锐干员将出现在作战区原位置」 / 「…将进入整备区」 (`shopBar.js mergeHint`, gold `.dhint--merge`; shop and reward cards).
- **The detail card covered the gold tile (fixed).** At 1920×1080 the left card covered half of a target tile in the leftmost legal column; the card now docks on the side away from the armed merge tile (`screens/game.js dSide`, the underframe's `panelSide` rule), on phones too.
- **Measurements re-taken (docs).** §20.10 / §20.12 and BALANCE §3 / §7 now give the integrated build's numbers (real seconds; the earlier boss-HP numbers are labelled as pre-merge boards) and the 999-layer profiles where 限伤 cancels hits.
- **Not changed — per-source talent debuffs [ASSUMED].** Some talents key their debuff with the source unit (`etlchi:dot:<id>`, `ines:atkSteal:<id>`, `ines:sentry:<id>`, and similar slows / DoTs in other kits), so two copies of one operator (a pair field where both players own it) each apply theirs. PRTS 作战机制 says only that same-named buffs show one instance under the *default* overlap strategy; whether these talents use it across copies is not in the data the project has, and the effect on leader kill times is small (an arts DoT, an ATK steal, a slow). The rule stays applied to the debuffs that move leader kills (奥术, 灵知 坚冰, the RES cuts — §20.10).
- **Not changed — temp items behind a full hand.** With the hand full, items overflowing into the 临时整备区 (row 8) stand behind the bench row's operator models and are hard to see; the row's red frame, the 「临时整备区 N 件道具待处理」 notice and the refused Ready still show them. A layout change of the temp row is left for a later pass.
- Tests: `test/match/clientCombat.test.js` (the held result; a 'cleared' result not covering the pool is still handed over), `test/render/playtest6-promote.test.js`, opt-in `RENDER_E2E=1 node --test test/render/browser.test.js` (a merge between two preps is cued), `test/ui/playtest6-elite.test.js` (the detail hint), opt-in `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/playtest6-elite.e2e.test.js` (the hint in the real detail card).

### 20.15 The bond strip follows the watched player (user report after playtest #6, 2026-10-02) — `ui/watchBonds.js`, `ui/bondStrip.js`, `ui/combatHud.js`, `screens/game.js` (`watchWho`, `bondOpen`), `battle/runner.js bondLayers / ownerOps`, `battle/observe.js resumedWatch`, `match/bondsMeta.js bondsWithGains`, `PlayerState.pendingLayerGains / bondsView`, `Match.publicView / _finishCombat / settle`

User: 「观看队友时（不仅是休整时候，还有自己战斗结束时，联防时，最终boss战）盟约栏应该变成当前队友的盟约以及他的层数」.

**Before this change:**
- Only 前往查看 of a teammate's normal field switched the strip, and it showed the start-of-round layers.
- An eliminated player's auto-observed field, the 联防 field and the Final Assault pair field kept the viewer's own bonds.
- A teammate's popup listed the viewer's pieces as its members.

**Whose bonds** (`watchBonds.screenFieldId` / `bondOwnerId` / `screenStrip`): the player whose field or half the camera shows.

The field on screen is, in order of preference:
- the watched field (`g.watch`);
- else the battle the view shows. Under client-side combat this is the runner's battle on screen, which is also an eliminated player's auto-observed field; under server-run combat it is the field the view entered;
- else home.

In SETTLE it is the field the last battle left on screen. In prep without watching, it is the own board.

- **The own board / own battle** → your bonds (`m.private bonds`).
- **A normal field `'n:<pid>'`** → its owner's bonds (`m.public players[].bonds`). This covers:
  - a teammate's board in prep (前往查看). Research 09 §3.1: "their board, read-only, including their pen and bonds". Official clients receive every board's bonds (`ChangePositionDn.boardStatus.updatedBonds`);
  - a teammate's battle watched after the own one;
  - the field an eliminated player auto-observes;
  - a field picked in the legacy view switcher.
- **A shared field** (联防 `'u'`, boss / hidden `'b1'` / `'b2'`) — **settled by the user** (2026-10-02: "共享阵地按你说的规则就行"; no official source shows the strip there):
  1. If the ‹ › pill points at a half that a player holds → that player. The pill's "你自己" means yours; "👁 name" means theirs.
  2. Else (全景, an empty half "无人在家", or no pill), if you fight on that field → yours.
  3. Else (you are not on the field: a 联防 leaker, an eliminated spectator) → never your own. The strip shows the teammate you picked with 前往查看 if they are on that field, else the field's first listed player (helper 1 / the pair's first seat).

  Basis: official observing targets a player (`ObserveUp { obIndex }`, `DeadAutoObDn { obIndex }`, `m_leftBattleObIdx / m_rightBattleObIdx`), and the official non-helper pill reads "‹ 👁 helper# ›" (research 09 §3.1).
- **The picked teammate:** the game screen remembers `watchWho = { fieldId, playerId }` when a team row is tapped (`requestWatch(fid, playerId)`). The `g.watch` request still names only the field. `watchWho` counts only while that field is the watched one (`watchedPlayer`). It is cleared by 返回战场, the own row and a phase change, and restored if the server refuses the watch.
- **Camera:** picking a teammate on a two-half shared field moves the camera to their half (`playerLayer`), once per pick and per field entered. The ‹ › pill is also offered on a shared field watched with 前往查看, so the pill and the strip tag always name the same player.
  - On such a field the ‹ › pill replaces the observing pill and carries 返回战场 (absent for an eliminated player).
  - On a normal field the observing pill "👁 name" takes the strip owner's name.
  - The default camera on entering a field is still 全景.
- **返回战场 without an own field** (client-side combat: a 联防 leaker, an eliminated player): no `g.watch` is sent, because it would be refused with `BAD_TARGET 'no such field'` (an error toast). The screen keeps the field it shows, and the strip falls back to the rules above.
- **A reload / reconnect while watching** a teammate's battle after the own one (client-side combat): the server already resends the watched field (`Match._resendBattle`, `b.start watch: true`). But a fresh screen starts with no watched field, so before this the HUD showed 「作战结束，等待队友完成作战」 with no observing pill and no 返回战场, and the own row did nothing.
  - The screen now adopts that field as the watched one, once per battle (`observe.resumedWatch`, decided at the first sight of its battleId).
  - It does so only for a teammate's normal battle in COMBAT, shown to a living player who watches nothing.
  - A battle first seen while a watch is already set is only marked as seen. So a 返回战场 tapped while the own `b.start` is still on its way never brings the teammate back.
  - A loading battle waits until it runs (its state carries no `watch` yet).
  - 联防 leakers and eliminated spectators keep their own rules.

**Labelling:**
- A teammate's strip leads with an amber "👁 name" pill (the observing pill's spelling) and gets amber disc rings, `aria-label` "name 的盟约" and `data-owner`; slots carry `data-bond`.
- The pill is horizontal at every width, with a pixel floor: 10 px on desktop and tablet, 9 px below 768 CSS px (1rem is clamped at 40 px there).
- An empty teammate strip reads "name 尚未激活盟约".

**Detail card chips and the popup (whose bond a popup shows):**
- A unit card's bond chips use the bonds of `watchBonds.detailBondOwner`:
  - a unit on the field → its owner, when that owner is a known player (yours or a teammate's: a shared field shows both halves' units, whoever the strip follows);
  - your own piece (board / bench) → yours;
  - a bond-member card opened from a popup → that popup's player;
  - anything else (shop / reward / drawer cards, a unit without a known owner) → the strip's player.
- A bond popup carries the player it was opened for: `bondOpen = { id, ownerId, from: 'strip' | 'detail' }` (`toggleBond`). From the strip, that is the strip's player; from a chip, it is the same player the chips show. The same bond of the same player closes it.
- `popupView` builds the popup from that player, never simply from the strip:
  - the entry with the live layers (as the strip and the chips show it);
  - "👁 name 的盟约" for a teammate;
  - the member list: your pieces, or that teammate's operators on the field on screen (`ownerBoard`: the prep scouting board's or a server-run battle field's units). Under client-side combat it also uses the battle on screen's operators (`battleRunner.ownerOps(ownerId, fieldId)`), because the field meta the runner publishes is taken before they deploy.
  - Their hand is never sent, so a teammate's member counts as owned only when it is on the field.
- The strip marks a disc as open only for a popup of its own player.
- A popup opened from the strip closes when the strip changes hands: another teammate, a ‹ › half, or back to your own. A popup opened from a chip stays open, labelled with its own player.

**Layers:**
- **In battle:** the battle runner reports `state().bondLayers = { [playerId]: { [bondId]: n } }`. These are absolute live counts of every bond that grew in a battle it simulates (the own battle, a teammate's display replica), capped at `BOND_LAYER_CAP` (999). They are published when one grows and kept through the round.
- The client lays them over the view's counts with `withLiveLayers`: the higher value wins (a count only grows) and never shows above 999. They apply in the battle phases and SETTLE only.
- 联防 and boss battles disable gains, so they report none.
- **After COMBAT** (`_finishCombat`): until SETTLE, both `m.private bonds` and `m.public players[].bonds` add the finished normal battle's IN_BATTLE gains (`PlayerState.pendingLayerGains` = the result's `layerGains`; `bondsMeta.bondsWithGains`: floored, at most up to the cap, like `settle()`). The strip of a player, and of a teammate watching him in the 联防, therefore keeps the layers his battle reached.
- `ps.bonds` / `ps.layers` are untouched. SETTLE clears the pending gains as it adds them (once), and the next round start clears them too. Since 0.1.3 the 联防 field fights with the same counts: the helpers' battle input takes its bonds from `bondsView()` (`battleInput({ reached: true })`, PRTS §联防阶段 "将以其阵地当前的状态" [ASSUMED] — it used to carry the round-start layers while the strip showed the reached ones); no other rule reads them.

**Data:**
- `m.public players[].bonds = ps.alive ? bondList(gd, ps.bondsView(), { off: offBondCounts(gd, ps) }) : []`: every bond with members, layers or an active tier (since 0.1.3 also the mode-off bonds with members, `off: true`, last). It is the same list and order as the player's own `m.private bonds`, minus `thresholds` / `countsHand` (the client reads those from bonds.json).
- An eliminated player's list is `[]`, for these reasons:
  - nobody can watch them: `g.watch` refuses them and they have no field;
  - the result screen reads `m.result`'s own bonds;
  - their remaining layers would otherwise cost every `m.public` for the rest of the match.
- Measured in a 4-AI NORMAL harness match (seed 11, round 13, 3 players eliminated): `players[].bonds` is 771 B, against 2424 B without this rule. The review measured 705 B on v2.5.1; the difference is the living player's layers-only entries, which the strip needs.
- There is no new message and no new field. `m.public` stays throttled and deduplicated, so the extra traffic is the living players' layers-only entries plus one change at the end of COMBAT.
- Nothing is exposed that scouting (research 09 §3.1) or watching the battle does not already show.

## 21. Player feedback after 0.1.0 (v0.1.1)

Thirty-two reports after the public 0.1.0 release (2026-10-02), relayed by the user in six batches; most were not tested by the user. Verdicts: **real** (fixed), **partly** (the rule was right, what a player sees was wrong or a related part was wrong — fixed), **not real** (not reproduced; tests lock the rule). Where each is handled: batch 1 — #1 拉普兰德's "获得该干员后该回合的首次刷新" should stack 叙拉古 → §21.1 (real); #2 late bounty enemies offered in early bounty drafts → §21.2 (real); #3 the water pool map: operators deployable in the water, 突袭 operators land in it → §21.3 (real); #4 昆图斯's 突变细胞 gone after one use → §21.1 (real: not consumed; its new operator joins the bench, not the carrier's tile — corrected after the merge, pointed out in PR #2); #5 转译基底 does not transform → §21.4 (real); #6 凯瑟琳's three upgrade items shown wrong → §21.2 (real); #7 鸭爵's 圆仔 blockable, audit the other 鸭爵 enemies → §21.5 (real); #8 深池逐火战士's 怨恨的余烬 cannot be killed → §21.4 (real); #9 tacticians (伺夜) can place the tactical point outside their attack range → §21.3 (real); #10 bots too dumb, smarter without more CPU → §21.6 (real). Batch 2 — B1 奥术 not working → §21.7 (partly: the bond works, 标准 switches it off); B2 源石溶剂 white + white → gold, then an extra white → §21.7 (not real); B3 维娜·维多利亚 S3 summons one 黄金盟誓 → §21.8 (real); B4 玛恩纳's skill deals no damage → §21.8 (real, on flying waves); B5 薄绿 can pull flying enemies → §21.9 (real). Batch 3 — C1 the hidden leader's "造成50%伤害" broadcast before the fight → §21.10 (real); C2 变形同构体 + class item: the bond member count excludes the wearer → §21.11 (partly: display only). Batch 4 — D1 信仰搅拌机: 源石溶剂's HP drain should trigger S3 → §21.12 (real); D2 野鬃's skill sometimes pushes the wrong way → §21.13 (real); D3 special 源石虫 all drawn as the plain one → §21.14 (partly); D4 暴鸰's bombs do not drop properly → §21.14 (partly); D5 the originium-floor map's lower route should cut diagonally from row 4 to row 3 → §21.15 (real). Batch 5 — E1 烛煌 S3's attack range does not change → §21.16 (partly: the card); E2 medics that block a roaming leader stop healing and attack it → §21.17 (not real); E3 卡涅利安's attack is not a true AoE → §21.18 (real). Batch 6 — F1 坚固维式重锤's lock does not work → §21.21 (partly: the lock works; it was once per battle, now once per deployment as the user settled, and an M3茧甲 equipped first took the first lethal hit); F2 a free 谢拉格 uses up 休露丝's discount → §21.21 (not real); F3 蒂比 still hit after taking off → §21.22 (real); F4 深巡 on a fenced tile does not attack → §21.23 (not real; a fence blocking fault found on the way is fixed); F5 突袭 operators land on a knocked-out operator's body → §21.24 (real). Hardening credited to pull requests (PR #2, PR #7, PR #10, PR #14) → §21.25. Closing additions from the public GitHub issues and pull requests: the gaps found while triaging issues #1 / #8 → §21.26; the client fixes for issue #5 (the folded shop's camera) and #8 item 5 (operators left as placeholders) → §21.27; the strategy draft's match info (#8 item 1) → §21.28; six 重装 skills cast with an enemy in range, the owner's deliberate deviation (issue #4, PR #12) → §21.29.

Eighteen workstreams (WA–WR, each on its own branch from master 7f13473) were merged into `feedback1` in the order WB, WA, WE, WD, WC, WI, WM, WN, WO, WL, WG, WH, WJ, WK, WP, WR, WQ, WF, then WB's follow-up from 66 screenshots of 22 matches (bounty lists, the 机密商店 and 战术决策 with repeats) after QA, then batch 6's four in the order WS, WU, WT (on WI's branch), WV (on WC's), then the four closing branches (the gaps, the 突变细胞 bench rule, the issue #5 / #8 client fixes, the draft's match info — each adding its own subsection, numbered §21.26–§21.28 in that merge order) and GitHub's master (PR #10, PR #12); data/*.json was rebuilt by `tools/build-data.mjs --offline` after every builder merge, never hand-merged. The integration fixed a bug already in 0.1.0 (a 联防 battle timed out during 隐德来希 S3 reported its 心烛 as leaks, so the server refused an honest client result — candles carry `mem.noLeak`), the last review of the transform workstream (form fx were still dropped by the render engine), every cross-workstream note worth doing with a primary source, and reconciled the bot with the merged rules — §21.19. §21.20 lists the settled decisions and what stays [ASSUMED]; batch 6 and the pull-request fixes follow it (§21.21–§21.25), then the closing additions (§21.26–§21.29), with what QA of that integration found and the integration fixed: a lent 坚固维式重锤 and in-place revives against the once-per-deployment lock (§21.21), 卢西恩 / 锏 casting for an airborne 蒂比 alone (§21.22), rule 3 for a summon that is gone (§21.24) and when the dispatcher takes its item snapshot (§21.25). Normative lines rewritten with it: §2 (`grid.js`), §3 (the effective `buildable`, the 深水区, range-bound summons, 攻击距离 vs skill ranges), §5.2 (`liveRangeGrid`, `form`), §5.3 (stealth vs area damage, the 奥术 refresh), §5.4 (`kill` / `death`, `foesInRadius`, `loseHp` vs damage, 静态刚体), §5.5 (healers that block, `rangeAoe`, armed enemies, results), §5.6 (TAKE_DAMAGE, `noRangeExtend` / `showOwnRange`, the profession splash radii), §6.2 (rewardOffer, piece `meta.round`, summons in range, returned equipment), §6.3 (变形同构体), §6.6 (the bot), §7 (bounty drafts, 鸭爵, knock-out forms), §8.2 / §8.3 (fx `form`, `m.ticker`, `range`, UnitInfo `items` / `form`, the stealth bit, rewardOffer), §9 (forms, alias tint), §10 (offers, ticker, 本局禁用), §13 (`spine/enemy`), §14 (ground pathing, boss tickers, the runner's form fx), §16, §17.3, §18.5, §18.6 #10, §19.1, §19.7, §20.1, §20.3, §20.6, §20.9; with batch 6: §3 (fenced tiles), §5.3 (`liftoff`), §5.4 (`fatal`, `ignoreSelect`, `redeploy`), §5.5 (bodies and redeploys, blocking, enemy AI), §6.4 (`onBuy`, the item snapshot), §8.2 (`down` row, col), §18.3, §19.7; with the closing additions: §5.6 (TAKE_DAMAGE), §6.2 (the 突变细胞 gain), §6.3 (`harmony`), §6.6 (the band pick, the gained operator), §8.2 / §8.3 (`harmony?`), §9 (`setCamera` `shop`, the model fallback), §10 (the draft's match info), §11 (the boss-field speed bar), §14 / §20.12 (the layer bound), §17.1, §19.2, §19.8, §20.2, §20.9, §20.11.

### 21.1 拉普兰德's first refresh; 突变细胞 is not consumed and its new operator joins the bench (#1, #4) — `sim/content/garrisons/meta.js SERVER_GAIN_BOND_LAYER_BY_REFRESH_CNT`, `match/PlayerState.js pieceRoundCount / bumpPieceRoundCount / _mergeChess / transformChess / acquireChess`, `match/effectsMeta.js ctx.pieceCounter / incPieceCounter / transform`, `match/builtinMeta.js char_chess_transformation_equip`, `match/audit.js`, `match/bot.js cellTarget / makeHandRoom`, `tools/build-data.mjs ITEM_RULES`, `ui/detailPanel.js ItemDetail`

- **#1 seen**: with 普罗旺斯 + 德克萨斯 deployed — refresh, buy 拉普兰德, deploy her (3 叙拉古), refresh again: no 叙拉古 layers. Players: officially "获得该干员后该回合的首次刷新" also stacks. **Cause**: the handler compared the PLAYER's round refresh count with `refresh_cnt` 1.
- **#1 official**: garrison_123_a/_b = SERVER_REFRESH_SHOP / SERVER_GAIN_BOND_LAYER_BY_REFRESH_CNT, bb `refresh_cnt` 1, layer 4 / 8, "若为本回合首次主动刷新…此干员在整备区时也有效" (activity_table act2autochess). Prep effects run on the scene server (research 11 §1.2); PRTS has no timing note. Text and report agree when the count belongs to the trait instance; the "本回合每刷新过1次" multipliers (阿罗玛 122, 安洁莉娜 147) fire on other events and keep the player's count.
- **#1 now**: each copy counts the manual refreshes it sees this round, on the board or in the hand (`piece.meta.round`, module-prefixed key `garrison:SERVER_GAIN_BOND_LAYER_BY_REFRESH_CNT:refreshes`), and fires on the `refresh_cnt`-th. A new piece starts at 0; a move or an in-place promotion keeps the count; a re-triggered trait neither fires nor counts; temp pieces do not count.
- **#4 seen**: "昆图斯策略给的突变细胞用完就没了". **Cause**: research 04's [ASSUMED] "item consumed"; the built-in and a content override both destroyed the cell.
- **#4 official**: the text "战斗结束后，装备者替换为高一阶的随机干员" never says 销毁 (every consumable's text does; data EQUIP, upgradeNum 100); PRTS 下半/PRTS盟约记录 备注 "生效时，原干员销毁，获得一名高一阶的随机初始干员（最高六阶）"; PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 "佩戴的装备无法手动卸除，在失去该干员（干员出售、销毁、合并等）或装备合并为进阶品质时自动卸除"; community guides re-inject it every round (bilibili cv47000418, cv48003106; cg.163.com "这个道具可以无限使用").
- **#4 where the new operator goes** (corrected after the WA merge; pointed out in the closed PR #2, whose playtest claim the footage confirms): official gameplay footage — bilibili BV1vzyVBuEN9 (上半, ≈ 8:24) and BV1Qkw1zMEoR (下半, ≈ 7:25) — shows the next prep with the carrier's tile **empty**, 剩余可放置角色 one higher and the 整备区 holding the returned equipment and the new tier+1 operator, which the player then deploys by hand. It reads the PRTS 备注 as written — a destroy followed by a gain ("原干员销毁，获得一名…") — and PRTS 帮助 sends whatever a player recruits or gains to the 手牌区 (= 整备区; "被发送至手牌区的物资优先从右到左填充空位"). WA had kept the new operator on the carrier's tile when legal [ASSUMED] and counted that tile for a merge the transformation completed [ASSUMED]; both are withdrawn (PR #2 itself still let the merge use the carrier's tile — not adopted).
- **#4 now**: after every battle the carrier is destroyed wherever it stands (board, hand or temp — a board tile is freed and the deploy count drops; its summons are removed, its copies return to the pool). Its equipment, the cell included, returns to the hand **first** (overflow temp; auto-merging like any gain). Then a random NORMAL operator one tier higher (max 6; an elite carrier too) is gained like any gained operator (`PlayerState.transformChess` → `acquireChess`, onGain source `'transform'`): the hand (right → left), overflow temp, and with both full the no-room rule of every gain (back to the pool, toast 整备区已满，获得的干员已返还; an item that found no slot takes one the gain freed, else stays on the gained operator up to 2 slots, else it is destroyed with a log warning, as in a merge). Like any operator on the bench it brings no summon card until it is deployed. A gain that completes a merge merges like any gained copy (§20.11): the elite on a consumed **deployed copy's** tile, else the hand — the carrier's freed tile is no copy's (`_mergeChess` lost its `fromKey` / `fromDir` options, `audit.js` its exception for them); the returned cell is stowed before the elite's summon card. Per-piece state stays with the returned items (商业包装方案's sell count, keyed by the item). One implementation (the built-in); the item card states the rule (items.json `note`). Bots inject their least valuable single normal operator below 6阶 (`cellTarget`: never an elite, a merge pair or a carrier of a cell), make a hand slot for a cell left in temp (`makeHandRoom`), and — AI 托管 too — deploy the gained operator in the next prep's lineup step like any owned unit (a real co-op round in the tests: SETTLE → the next prep). The buy loop's pre-emptive bench shed, which sells the weakest bench single by piece value while the bench is crowded, no longer sells a piece gained since the bot's last prep ended (`bot.js rememberOwned`): on the new rule it had sold 44 of 222 gained operators in 20 solo 绝境 matches on 昆图斯 before any placement step saw them (now none; a gained operator stays off the board only when the board is full). Measured (`tools/botbench.mjs`, decisions only): strategy 昆图斯 (`--band band_quintus`, seeds 201–220) 绝境 8 / 20 wins on the WA build → 7 with the bench rule alone → 11 with the shed fix (LP left 16.8 → 13.3 → 17.1), 险境 15 → 16 → 17 (16.3 → 16.2 → 17.6); the bots' own strategies, bench rule alone → with the fix: solo 绝境 (seeds 1–12) 8 → 8 / 12, solo 险境 (seeds 13–36) 19 → 19 / 24, co-op 绝境 4 AI (seeds 1–36) 9 → 8 / 36 (LP left within 0.6) — noise; the shed's cost is a set of the owned uids per prep. The client needs no special case: `render/app.js setPrep` drops the carrier's view and draws the new piece on its bench slot (checked in headless Chrome: the tile empty, the operator and the cell on the bench, no promotion cue, no console error).
- [ASSUMED]: an elite merged this round keeps its copies' highest count (no second firing that round); a copy re-bought after a sale fires on its own first refresh ("获得该干员后"; each extra +4 costs 3 + 1 − 1 = 3 funds); a bench carrier (hand or temp) transforms too (the footage shows deployed carriers); a 6阶 carrier becomes another 6阶; with the hand and temp both full the gained operator and an item without a slot follow the rules of every gain (no source shows that case).
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js` (a deployed and a bench carrier, the hand full → temp, the hand and temp full, a merge with and without a deployed copy, 商业包装方案's count, bots and AI 托管 redeploying, a real solo 绝境 昆图斯 bot match: no gained operator shed in its first prep, none waiting on the bench beside a free deployment), `test/ui/feedback1-cell.test.js`; `items.test.js`, `merge.test.js`, `facing.test.js`, `playtest6-elite-to-board.test.js` updated.

### 21.2 Bounty drafts by the official round structures; special offers shown as what they hold (#2, #6) — `tools/build-data.mjs BOUNTY_INITIAL_SETS / BOUNTY_BOSS_GROUPS / BOUNTY_HUNTER_GROUPS / SHOP_DRAFT / TACTIC_DRAFT`, `match/choices.js bountyDraftCards / shopDraftCards / tacticDraftCards`, choices.json `bountyDrafts` / `shopDraft` / `tacticDraft` / `draftPool` / schedule `bountyDraft`, `test/fixtures/official-bounty-drafts.json`; `match/PlayerState.js pushRewardOffer / pushItemOffer`, `match/effectsMeta.js offerLabel`, `ui/gameLogic.js offerHeader`, `ui/shopBar.js RewardCards`, `ui/rewardOverlay.js`

- **#2 seen**: "本来应该后期出的悬赏的怪物在前期的悬赏就出现了，导致选了打不过". **Cause**: every draft drew uniformly from 105 cards (§20.6), so R3 could offer the R9 bosses and the R11 giants.
- **#2 official** (66 screenshots of 22 co-op 绝境 / 终极 matches of this season at R3 / R9 / R11, collected by the user 2026-10-03; readings in the fixture, no player names): every card is an act2autochess (下半) effect whose enemy is the effect's `enemy_id` (the title only names category and tier: 悬赏·损伤I = 底海滑动者 e12_4 / 临时收音师 e18_1 / 心虚设计师 e16_11); each 悬赏决策 event is a fixed card list and a draft shows 6 different cards of it in varying positions.
  - **R3 (22 / 22)**: six 接下来两场作战 cards, I I I II II III (10 coins), one of 10 fixed sets (enemy_initial_1..10), all six shown; 9 sets seen — series 17 / 18 / 19 whole, and hand-made mixed sets (one holds two series-20 cards, one holds 自制投石机 e17_6), so no simple rule describes them.
  - **R9 (22 / 22)**: boss bounties X·悬赏 + 源石虫·特训, 下场作战 — one of 6 groups (one per bossInitial event) of 9, 9, 9, 9, 8 and 6 cards; 庞贝 and 鼠王 never share a draft; named bosses do not always come together (match 19 = 自在 + 杰斯顿 + 陷落雪祀, match 12 = 杰斯顿 alone). The 鼠王 group came in 14 of 22 matches, every dark-grey-board match among them.
  - **R11**: 悬赏决策 14, 机密商店 4, 战术决策 4, 道具补给 0 of 22. A bounty draft is a list of 7 下场战斗 cards — one 特异III giant, one card per faction series — of which the draft leaves out one (match 15 leaves out the giant; matches 1 / 6 / 21 / 22 leave out four different cards of one list); 7 lists seen.
  - **机密商店** (matches 2, 4, 5, 8): six free items with repeats (盟约之币 ×2, 变形同构体 ×2); each shop exactly two tier VI, at least one V and one 盟约之币, no tier I / II. **战术决策** (matches 7, 9, 18, 20): six ally cards each (no 排斥 / 责罚 / 裁决, no 模拟战场演变), 补给 twice in match 7.
  - Never in a draft: the 7 multi-round cards (the 假想敌 question is answered), enemyeffect_3_*, 战术特训, the 鸭爵 set.
- **#2 now**: cards.bounty `draftPool` initial 42, boss 20, hunter 24 (86 drafted); `draftExcluded` 'unseen' (19: multi-round, e3_*, 凋零骑士 / 遗弃者 / 锏 / 扎罗 / 迷路的巨像). A draft = one group, then 6 different cards of it, each drawn with weight 1 + the official drafts of its group it showed in. R3 = one of 10 slots: a seen set, or for the unseen 10th a rule set (tiers I I I II II III, ≤ 2 per series, the III 法术大师A1 e10_6 or 鼎沸 e20_6 — the two-battle cards no draft showed); R9 = a seen group, picked by the matches it came in; R11 = one of the 7 seen lists, uniform (the 4 lists seen with 6 cards completed by the list shape, so 12_8 / 16_2 / 16_6 come in about 1 R11 bounty draft in 15); 绝境 / 终极 R11 families: bounty 14 / shop 4 / tactic 4 (the official counts; was supply 50 / shop 50). The R11 机密商店 (`shopDraft`): six slots drawn with replacement — VI, VI, V, 盟约之币 and twice V / IV / III / 盟约之币 — items weighted by the official cards they showed on; the earlier 标准 / 险境 shops keep any tier I–VI per card, also with replacement. The 战术决策 (`tacticDraft`): every card drawn on its own with replacement; at R11 only the ally cards, weighted 1 + the official cards each showed on; other rounds every card, uniform. Identical cards are separate cards (picks go by index; two picks of a team card stack). `MULTI_ROUND_BOUNTY_BATTLES = 2` stays (it now only affects 教鞭's 法术大师A2·多轮战术特训). The mode's inactive enemy list no longer removes bounty cards (PRTS 11/18 note "不影响悬赏决策出场"). Flip: add a newly seen set, group or list to the build-data constants and rebuild. On the 22 official bounty drafts the generator reaches every one (60 000 seeds per round, `mode_multi_hard` and `mode_multi_abyss`).
- **#6 seen**: 凯瑟琳's three items appeared as blank operator cards with raw ids under 晋升奖励. **Now**: a special-refresh offer is drawn as what it holds — free item cards (icon, name, tier, description, FREE) or chess cards under its source's label (凯瑟琳 定向投放, 娜仁图亚 见者有份, 寻呼模块, 信标, 松果) + SPECIAL + 免费选择 1 件 / 名; m.private `shop.rewardOffer = { tier, source: 'merge'|'special', label, queued, slots }`; offers waiting behind the shown one show as "之后还有 N 项" and a +N on the reminder pill; a special offer plays the refresh sound.
- [ASSUMED]: the card weights inside a group; the unseen 10th R3 set (rule) and 险境 R6 drafting like R3; R9 groups picked by the matches they came in (what of the match decides — map, leader or difficulty — is open) and the three single-draft groups completed only with the base cards they lack; R11 = one of the 7 seen lists (which of the 15 events R11 fires is open: by the blocks of effectChoiceInfoDict it would be bounty_hunter_8..15, and with 8 events an unseen list would come in about 1 R11 bounty draft in 8); the R11 family weights = the official counts; the 机密商店 slot split and item weights, and the earlier 标准 / 险境 shops; the 战术决策 weights, R11 without terrain cards, independent draws (open: all 4 official drafts hold a 驰援, a 盟誓 and two of 列装 / 财富 / 补给 / 整备 / 升华), twin battle passives stacking; solo 绝境 / 终极 R11 = the co-op weights (no solo screenshot; the data has 15 solo `bounty_hunter_*_s` events); solo shows 3 of 6.
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-bounty.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-secret-shop.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-tactic.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-offers.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-secret-shop.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-tactic.test.js`, `test/match/playtest6-matchflow.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest6-bounty.test.js`, `test/content/choices.test.js`; opt-in `test/ui/feedback1-offers.e2e.test.js`, `feedback1-secret-shop.e2e.test.js`, `feedback1-tactic.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`).

### 21.3 No deployment in the water; tactical points inside the tactician's range (#3, #9) — `sim/grid.js DEPLOY_REFUSED_TILES / canStand`, `tools/build-data.mjs effectiveBuildable / ownerRange`, `match/board.js ownerRangeKeys`, `match/PlayerState.js _legal / summonRange / _liftOutOfRange / _reorient`, `ui/gameLogic.js summonRange`, `match/bot.js`, `match/invariants.js`, `content/kits/tier3.js tacticalPoint`

- **#3 seen**: "有水池的那张图干员可以错误的被部署到水里，突袭分队干员也会自动落到水里" — the 深水区 (`tile_deepsea`) of 战场#08(下半) 涨潮控制 (act2 m04): board (10–12, 6), partner (10–12, 14), boss (3–5, 6) / (3–5, 14). **Cause**: the level gives it buildableType ALL and the legend copied it; the deploy maps (server, client, bots) and `grid.canStand` (the 突袭 landing tile, tactical points, summon tiles) treated it as road, and a tactical point even preferred it (it lies on the enemy path).
- **#3 official**: PRTS 深水区, 地形信息 tile_deepsea: 部署类型 全部位, 地形机制 "拒绝部署（待补充）"; PRTS 作战机制: the 地形标记 AdvancedBuildableMask (values include 深水) "限制玩家仅能部署匹配的单位于其上"; `trap_040_canoe` 特制水上平台 "在水上建立可以部署任意单位的平台" (season 1's 战场#05 puts one on each water tile). There is no PRTS page "地形".
- **#3 now**: `DEPLOY_REFUSED_TILES` makes the effective buildable NONE: stages.json `tiles.d.buildable` NONE (`buildableType` ALL kept), `deployTiles` without the water, `canStand` refuses it (and melee units on a hard-blocked 射击台 / mound); every automatic placement and the bots follow. An active 特制水上平台 makes its tile deployable in the prep deploy map only (act1 m05, weight 0; the sim models no canoe, its Grid keeps those tiles NONE [ASSUMED]). Gate routes are unchanged; the water counts as non-blockable in the path tie-break, so a few off-route smoothed steps of the m04 boss field (rows 4–5) go around it. 维娜·维多利亚's lions skip it too (§21.8).
- **#9 seen**: "干员伺夜这样的战术家的战术点能被错误的被放到攻击范围之外". **Official**: 狼群 / 流形 token text "只能部署在召唤者攻击范围内" (character_table; PRTS 狼群 特性); the tactician trait "可以在攻击范围内选择一次战术点来召唤援军"; PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 "移动干员时，其所属召唤物全部退场并重置至手牌区".
- **#9 now**: tokens.json `ownerRange`; such a piece stands only on `ownerRangeKeys(loadout attack grid, owner tile, owner dir)` — enforced by the server (`_legal` / `summonRange`), the client (drag highlights; toast "只能部署在召唤者攻击范围内"), the bot and the invariants. Moving the owner sends its summons back (unchanged); in swaps each piece is checked from the tile it ends on; an in-place re-orientation or promotion that leaves the summon outside sends it back to its stack with a toast — one still inside stays (user-confirmed, §21.20); with nowhere to go (hand and temp full) the re-orientation is refused with `HAND_FULL`, on the other paths the summon leaves the board and comes back at the next round start (`grantTokensFor`). No out-of-range placement reaches the battle.
- [ASSUMED]: the range is the loadout's grid (`attackRangeGrid`, §21.16; items and bonds do not widen it); automatic placements never put a melee unit on a 射击台; the canoe in the deploy map only.
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-placement.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-water.test.js`; board, playtest5-deploy, playtest6_summons, ui/gameLogic, pathing-blockable / -crosscheck updated; opt-in `test/ui/feedback1-placement.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`; fastServer `SP_STAGE`).

### 21.4 Enemies that change form when knocked out (#5 转译基底·α, #8 深池逐火) — `content/enemies.js` (husk, kitTranslator, reborn, statue, setForm, rebirthCleanse), `sim/ai.js enemyAttack`, `sim/snapshot.js`, `shared/protocol.js fxForm`, `battle/runner.js keepsState / hold / compactHeld`, `screens/game.js keepEarly`, `render/interp.js isCosmeticEvent`, `render/app.js renderInfo`, `render/units.js FORMS`, `render/spine.js`

- **#8 seen**: the 怨恨的余烬 could not be killed. **Cause**: the ember stood still, 隐匿 AND unblockable, so no operator could ever target it (a real R3 round on 战场#01: 0 of 7 killed, 7 leaked).
- **#8 official** (PRTS 深池逐火战士 / 护卫 天赋; `Revive[Trigger]` 5 / 10, `interval` 10; PRTS 特殊机制 §重生 / §特殊生命值机制; 作战机制 §隐匿): every knock-out → a 1 s 重生 (immobile, 无敌 + 无法阻挡 + 失衡免疫); then the ember — max HP 5 (护卫's 火灰 10), 1 per damage instance, no attack, 隐匿 + 缴械; not killed within 10 s → the warrior with full HP. A blocked enemy loses 隐匿.
- **#8 now**: the ember walks on; blocked, it is targetable and drawn solid (the stealth bit only while its 隐匿 is on). The same round: 7 / 7. An operator's radius area damage reaches it only while blocked or revealed (§21.19) — and, since 0.1.2, for 3 s after a block ends (§22.8).
- **#5 seen**: 转译基底 played a "transform" clip and vanished. **Causes**: no clip sets for its forms (it stayed on A_* and died on B_Die, the 寻仇者's death clip); its first form took damage; and the client dropped the change fx in catch-up frames, hidden tabs, the pre-entry buffer and the render engine's stale-event drop (b.snap carries no form).
- **#5 official** (PRTS 转译基底·α 天赋; talents Passive / Mode_*_Passive / Mode_Fuchou_Anger): no attack; every damage instance cancelled; 失衡免疫 + control immunities; the 4th physical hit → 寻仇者, the 4th arts hit → 特战术师 (2 targets), a block → 幽灵 (unblockable, no attack — PRTS 幽灵 "不进行普通攻击，无法被阻挡"); once, 2 s.
- **#5 now**: A_Die_B / _C / _D, then B_* / D_* / C_*; no HP lost before the change (an HP loss stops at 1); the armed forms attack through `ai.js enemyAttack` (`e.profile`).
- **Kill accounting**: a survived knock-out gives no kill, credit, bounty, die event or death hook; wave completion and 联防 counters see the enemy standing; a bounty coin is paid once, at the final death.
- **重生** (PRTS §重生 "清空自身身上除白名单外所有Buff"): `rebirthCleanse` removes ally-sourced buffs and source-less statuses, keeping `persist` talents, enemy-given buffs (锏's 抵抗) and source-less field state; its skills restart from their initial cooldown when it ends (§21.19).
- **Forms on the client**: `setForm` keeps `e.form` (UnitInfo `form`) and puts `form` on its fx (`fxForm`, the 'phase' fx too, so barrier / charge phases are no forms). No client stage drops a form fx: runner catch-up frames (`keepsState`), the hidden-tab backlog of the battle on screen (`hold`; past `HELD_MAX` superseded status / skill toggles are compacted away, `compactHeld`, and only a backlog still past it re-enters the view from the field meta), a battle that ended while hidden (delivered when the tab is shown; every batch keeps the game time it was drained at, `EV_SLICE`), the game screen's pre-entry buffer (`keepEarly`, also while a re-sent field meta re-enters the field on screen) and the render engine's queue (`render/interp.js isCosmeticEvent`: a form fx is never dropped as stale nor shed; one handed out late switches the model without its telegraph or a change clip that would already have ended, its closing clip shortened — the last three since the integration, §21.19). Late views: `renderInfo` keeps `form`. Closing clips are timed from the fx `dur` and keep the current form's death clip until the next form's fx.
- **Audit**: 锏 / 扎罗 had #5's look (fixed: their 重生 clips); 杰斯顿 → C2_*; 守墓石像 per PRTS (statue, then flight, §21.9); 再生: 1 s 重生, then shields within 1.8. No unit stays untargetable. “余音” stays unmapped (no source says which form is A).
- [ASSUMED]: the translator is immobile during its change and stays damage-cancelled; the HP-loss floor 1; form letters B / C / D; the 特战术师 form also hits flyers (the standalone PRTS 特战术师 "不会攻击飞行单位" may carry over — one line, `canTarget: (u) => !u.isFlying`); the ember's 10 s counts from the end of the 重生; 再生's 傀儡 walks; the statue does not attack (clip Sleep); the 重生 whitelist. (隐匿 returned at once after a block until 0.1.2 — now 3 s, the knock-out's own release included: §22.8.)
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1-transform.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-runner-forms.test.js` (stalls, hidden tabs, a battle ending while hidden, compaction, the runner feeding the render buffer), `test/render/interp.test.js`, `test/render/forms.test.js`, `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js`; opt-in `test/render/feedback1-forms.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`; blocking 1 s long tasks).

### 21.5 The 鸭爵 strategy's enemies (#7) — `content/enemies.js` (runWhenHit, 圆仔 kits), `content/bands/meta.js duckReplace / duckGlobal`, `Match.startFinalAssault` (event `routes` / `side`), `tools/build-data.mjs bandSwapEnemyKeys`

- **Seen**: "鸭爵策略带来的怪物圆仔错误的能被阻挡"; the user asked for an audit of the other three. **Cause**: 圆仔's kit had only the front guard (its unblockability is in PRTS's talent text, not in the handbook ability list). The audit found more: 鸭爵's run multiplied the speed by `run.attack@move_speed` instead of adding it; the swap ran only in normal battles and only on NORMAL-rank enemies; the four kept the database's roguelike `lifePointReduce` 0; on a pair boss field a player's swaps came from both halves.
- **Official**: PRTS 圆仔 天赋 "无法攻击/被阻挡；受到来源于正面的物理和法术伤害-80%；自身始终朝向我方干员数量最多的方向"; PRTS 鸭爵 天赋 "受到伤害时开始奔跑，移动速度+400%"; PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约 下半/PRTS盟约记录 §策略 鸭爵 备注: "每回合将有0~2名敌人被替换为上述敌人之一，且上述敌人为特殊版本，相较于原版更弱" (popup: 鸭爵 6000 HP, +300 % speed when hurt; 高普尼克 11000 / 1600 ATK; 流泪小子 4500, 3.5 s stun; 圆仔 13000), "但进入保护目标点将减少1点目标生命值，且在最终回合和隐秘核心回合中仍然生效". Data: the band's enemylist names the `_2` versions (blackboard min 0, max 2, round 5, minweight 0.6, maxweight 0.99). How the swap is drawn is in no source.
- **Now**: 圆仔 / 圆仔_2 unblockable + front guard; 鸭爵 / 鸭爵_2 run ×(1 + v) once (+400 % / +300 %). The swap runs in normal, Final Assault and Hidden Core battles (联防 adds none); candidates: any ground wave enemy, normal or elite, except the leader, its parts, bounties, earlier swaps and uncounted units — each swap takes the replaced unit's time and route; on a pair boss field each player's 0–2 come from the enemies whose route ends on that player's half (`ev.side` + `ev.routes`, the bounties' half rule), the 60–99 % share taken over that half. The four `_2` enemies have `lpr` 1 (builder): the Final Assault / Hidden Core LP and the card's 目标价值 read it.
- [ASSUMED]: the uniform 0–2 count and the uniform pick; minweight / maxweight = the 60–99 % part of the player's enemies in time order; ground only; elites eligible (about two thirds of the leader rounds' escorts in that share are elites; NORMAL-only would leave ≈ 80 % of R14 pair halves without a candidate vs ≈ 36 %); per-half swaps; decided at battle start (not in the prep preview); 圆仔's backward pose not rendered.
- Tests: `test/match/feedback1-ducklord.test.js` (a real R6 wave; solo Final Assault + LP; co-op Final Assault → Hidden Core pair fields on the real match path), `test/content/bands.test.js`, `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js`, `test/data.test.js`.

### 21.6 AI players (#10 "人机有点太笨了") — `server/match/bot.js`, `tools/botbench.mjs`

- **Measured on the 0.1.0 bot** (`tools/botbench.mjs`, same seeds on two builds): 0–2 refreshes a prep and side-grade singles sold again for 1 (≈ 2 merges a match, ≈ 2 elites at R13); a level-up could spend the funds a third copy needed; the bounty score ignored the enemy (co-op 绝境: 155 of ≈ 310 bounty enemies leaked); 信标 (destroys its carrier) went on the best operator; DPS ignored DEF / RES; the focus bond flip-flopped.
- **Now** (decisions only, no rule or balance change): refresh vs buy by the shop odds of completing held pairs (a full board buys only merge progress or a lineup upgrade worth more than the refreshes its price would pay for; an elite's value includes the merge's free pick); ≤ 4 pairs on the bench, merges before level-ups, a freeze for an unaffordable third copy; one committed focus core bond + a second bond, a teammate's main core bond read from its public bond strip (humans too) counting against it; dealers valued by DPS against the round's DEF / RES; 坎诺特 keeps its interest capital (5) back; bounty picks by expected payout − expected leaks × (2 + 20 / LP) with the kill chance from the exposure model — an expected-value pick (co-op leaks are bound by the drafts, §21.2); items by effect (信标 on a bench single, 拟态物质 on a pair, both 博士投影 on the strongest normal operator, 突变细胞 per §21.1, bond items on a focus member, survival items on blockers); 教鞭 / “神秘顾客” used after a perfect battle and kept otherwise — a bot drops a kept one only for a hand slot, a human's seat under AI 托管 never; a tactician's 援军 inside `PlayerState.summonRange`.
- **Result** (seeds 1–40, rehearsal 3, on the WF branch): 独立 绝境 19 → 26 / 40 wins (LP left 14.3 → 16.6), 同盟 绝境 (4 AI) 5 → 12 / 40 (5.1 → 12.7), 标准 unchanged; merges per bot ≈ 2 → 3–4; forced strategies (solo 绝境, seeds 201–220): 坎诺特 9 → 14, 昆图斯 9 → 11, 杜宾 8 → 9. **Decision time unchanged**: heuristics CPU p50 / p95 31 / 74 → 28 / 70 ms solo and 17 / 39 → 17 / 38 ms co-op; the whole prep with its rehearsal 89 / 205 and 83 / 162 ms wall.
- **Reconciled with the merged rules** (§21.19): the server's legality (deploy map without the 深水区, `summonRange`), WA's cell rule (and its destroy-then-gain correction: the gained operator is deployed by the lineup step, the bench shed spares a piece gained since the last prep — §21.1), WB's draft structures (it scores whatever is offered), 本局禁用 bonds (never planned), the range a unit is deployed with, and the AoE of §21.18 in its DPS value. On the merged build, seeds 1–40: solo 绝境 27 → 27 wins, co-op 绝境 9 → 11 (within the noise); decision time stays (heuristics CPU 24 / 57 ms solo, 16 / 36 ms co-op; the whole prep 78 / 153 and 73 / 151 ms wall; BALANCE §5). After the QA: WB's 22-match bounty lists (a giant in every R11 bounty list, 14 of 22 R11s a 悬赏决策) take the same bot to 22 / 40 and 9 / 40 — a rule change; the bench-shed fix (never sell a piece of this prep, §21.19) then 24 / 40 and 10 / 40. **The 0.1.1 build against 0.1.0 (19 / 40, 5 / 40): 24 / 40 solo 绝境, 10 / 40 co-op 绝境 (4 AI)** — part of it is the rules: 0.1.0's bot on the 0.1.1 rules wins 23 / 40 and 7 / 40 (LP left 14.6 / 5.2 against 17.4 / 13.1, merges per bot 2.2 / 2.0 against 3.8 / 3.1); same-prep buy → sell in co-op 绝境 (2 AI 托管 + 2 bots, seeds 21–26) 29 → 3; decision time unchanged (one thread, seeds 1–3: the whole prep 81 / 187 → 81 / 184 ms wall solo, 71 / 153 → 71 / 129 ms co-op; heuristics CPU 36 / 88 → 35 / 86 and 16 / 40 → 16 / 40 ms).
- [ASSUMED] (tuned; the official AI is undocumented): MERGE_HIT 36, MAX_PAIRS 4, BENCH_BUDGET 6, FOCUS_MEMBER 4, ARMOR_WEIGHT 10, BOUNTY_KILL 2, the LP value 2 + 20 / LP, the teammate penalty 6, the 坎诺特 reserve = the band's interest capital, `CROWD` (×2 / ×1.3 / ×1.4).
- Tests: `test/match/bot-smarter.test.js` (bounty picks, item carriers, Arts kept / AI 托管 safe, tactician range, pair via buy or freeze, 坎诺特, the bond strip, the merged rules, a CPU cost guard < 300 ms), `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js` (AI 托管 never destroys an item to clear temp).

### 21.7 奥术 works; 本局禁用 bonds; the 源石溶剂 merge (B1, B2) — `ui/gameLogic.js modeOffBonds`, `ui/detailPanel.js BondChips`, `ui/shopBar.js ChessCard`, `ui/bondStrip.js BondPopup`, `screens/game.js`, `match/PlayerState.js _mergeChess`, `match/effectsMeta.js destroyPiece`, `content/bonds/addon/battle.js`

- **B1 measured — the bond works** (a real 险境 battle through `Match._normalOpts`, and the same battle from its BattleSpec, the browser path — identical): each member's arts hit debuffs its target ×(1.2 + 0.01·L) arts damage taken for 3 s and later arts hits land multiplied (深靛 + 洛洛, R5, seed 7: 41 / 54 arts hits debuffed at L0, 0 without the bond); 3 different members: below 50 % HP ×1.4·(…) (seed 7: 9 hits at ×1.68 and, after 海霓's live +3 layers, 19 at ×1.722); all 10 members × S1–S3 × normal / elite apply it, summons do not (not 干员); leaders too (Final Assault 绝境 R14, 999 谢拉格: 329 819 → 426 911 leader damage in 700 s at L0).
- **What a player saw — fixed**: 标准模拟 switches 奥术 off (official modeDataDict inactiveBondIdList: 拉特兰 阿戈尔 卡西米尔 灵巧 奥术 奇迹 投资人 突袭 独行 绝技), while 深靛 洛洛 阿罗玛 夕 圣聆初雪 stay in the pool through their other bond; their cards showed 奥术 normally, the chip read 「0/2 未激活」 with three deployed and the popup 「在场 0/2 未激活」. **Now** such a bond is struck through on shop / reward cards, the chip reads 本局禁用, the popup reads 本局禁用 with a note and no 在场 count or 当前效果 block (its numbers would promise an effect) [ASSUMED look].
- **Official, unchanged**: 限伤 cancels a leader hit ≥ 300 000 in boss / hidden battles (§20.12), so very large multiplier stacks can make huge hits vanish (巴哈姆特 12316); our measured boards stay far below it (largest 24 813).
- **The stacking rule, reconciled — §20.10 stands**: PRTS 盟约记录's current note 「※同一单位仅可对同一目标同时施加1个该盟约法术伤害提升效果」, read with the engine default (PRTS 常见同名状态) and 巴哈姆特 12316, gives one effective instance per target (applyStrongest). Known deviation [ASSUMED]: an earlier revision (oldid 386936) said a unit cannot re-apply while its own instance lasts; an equal re-hit refreshes it here (slightly stronger). Strongest rather than earliest stays [ASSUMED].
