- **B2 — not reproduced**: every PlayerState path ends with exactly one golden and no white (buy next to a white in the hand or equipped, two buys, equip on another or a full carrier, a reward pick, 道具补给, 列装, 整备 + buy, 画卷, the carrier's sale); random intents over all 50 mergeable items conserve W + 2·G and never leave a pair (upgradeNum 2 → _b; PRTS 帮助 「已拥有2件同一初始装备时…自动合并」). A latent gap closed: equipment returned by a promotion (`_mergeChess`) or by `ctx.destroyPiece` (突变细胞, the round-start promotion item) now runs `checkItemMerges` (unreachable by a player before).
- [ASSUMED]: 整备 bought next to an owned white merges the two first and keeps the effect for the next purchase (the official text may mean the bought item turns golden); the 标准 popup lists members the mode keeps out of the pool dimmed as not owned.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1b-arcane.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1b-bonds.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1b-items.test.js`.

### 21.8 维娜·维多利亚's lions; 玛恩纳 S3 hits air units (B3, B4) — `kits/tier6.js siege2`, `tokens.js SKILL_SUMMON_UNCAPPED`, `kits/tier5.js` 玛恩纳

- **B3 seen**: her S3 (default, defaultSkillIndex 2) summoned one 黄金盟誓. **Real**: on the real match path (战场#05 下半, R8, (10,5)) every cast placed 1 lion beside 3 free melee tiles (the reviewer's sweep of m01–m04: 74 casts, all 1 lion). The kit picked one tile, and the per-owner deploy limit (the token's phase maxDeployCount 1, a hand count) would have withdrawn a second.
- **B3 official**: skill_table skchr_siege2_3 "立即在天赋一生效范围内可部署地面召唤“黄金盟誓”"; EN client "Summons Golden Vows on deployable tiles within Talent 1's range"; wiki.gg "only be spawned on open low ground tiles"; PRTS 备注 "于技能结束时/维娜退场时自动退场".
- **B3 now**: one lion on every free tile of her 3×3 talent area where a melee piece could deploy (LOW, ALL / MELEE, unoccupied, not a reserved home tile; `grid.canStand`, so never the 深水区), at most 8, for the skill's duration; `SKILL_SUMMON_UNCAPPED` keeps the deploy limit off them; each counts for 诸王的叹息 (546 × (1 + 1.45 + 0.30) with 6 lions). 1 → 3 lions at (10,5) on 战场#05, 6 at (11,4) on 战场#01.
- **B4 seen**: 玛恩纳 "开技能没伤害". **Real, on flying waves**: his default S3 fires (CUSTOM_RANGE 3-18) on any enemy, flyers included, but his attacks were ground-only melee — a flyer-only cast hit nothing for 26 s, then the trait ramp reset (4 of 19 real casts; seed 21 R2 dealt 0).
- **B4 official** (PRTS S3 备注): "※可对空"; "每击倒一名符合条件的敌人，特性提供的攻击力加成-10%，特性最低降低至+0%" — only "自身普通攻击（与该次攻击附带的伤害）" kills count, "于当次攻击后统一结算", not kills by 第二天赋 (reflection), by a mark another unit's attack set off, or sourceless ones; S2's "不重置" follows the same rule.
- **B4 now**: S3 `targeting.canHitFly` (flyer-only casts 0–10k → 14k–93k damage); bonus = max(0, ramp × trait_up − 0.1 × own kills), settled after the attack; an own kill = an enemy his current attack (or the mark it set off) damaged — the set is cleared after each attack, so carried damage counts and reflection / teammates' marks do not. His trait sets block 0 only while the skill is off: during S2 / S3 he blocks up to 3, and the 卡西米尔 bond's true damage rides his attacks only while he blocks nothing.
- [ASSUMED]: fence tiles (low, deployable, not walkable) count as 可部署地面; "−10 %" = absolute points of the bonus.
- Tests: `test/content/feedback1b_kits.test.js`, `kits_t6` (every lion off the reserved tile), `test/sim/feedback1-integrate.test.js` (no lion in 战场#08's 深水区).

### 21.9 Air units are 静态刚体 (B5) — `tools/build-data.mjs STATIC_BODIES`, enemies.json `staticBody`, `sim/Battle.js _displaceable`, `content/bosses.js` (胄 parts), `content/enemies.js` (statue, 喷气人)

- **Seen** (relayed: "飞机可以被薄绿的技能拉走（难道不能拉动吗？…你去复核下）"). **Real**: `_displaceable` checked only 失衡免疫 and leaders, so every flyer moved by the 力度 − 重量 tables — 薄绿 S2 dragged 妖怪 1.33 tiles per hit and an act2 m01 drone reached the goal 6.6 s late; with client combat drones were dragged 43–55 times per match; 崖心 S1 moved a 刺胄之弹 0.87 tiles; 守墓石像 moved 0.03–0.44 tiles (1.4–1.7 under 失重).
- **Official**: PRTS 特殊机制 静态刚体 "可以进入失衡状态…但物理层面上无法产生任何速度或移动…※与失衡免疫不同…※是否为静态刚体与单位的行动方式无关"; PRTS 失衡位移机制 (air units count, "但会因下述的判断而被取消位移"); every air unit of the mode except “炎佑” (weight 10, 我方单位) lists {{特殊机制|静态刚体}} in its PRTS 天赋, and so does 盐风主教昆图斯 — 28 keys (all 249 enemy pages checked). 失衡免疫 also: 刺胄之弹 / 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤 (their talent also lists 不可阻挡, so a grounded 剑 / 锤 stays unblockable); 守墓石像 转换模式 "无法被阻挡，自缚，失衡免疫，免疫浮空" and 飞行模式 "变为飞行单位，失衡免疫"; 喷气人 飞行模式 "近地悬浮，不可阻挡，失衡免疫，移动速度+50%", −90 % for 1.5 s after take-off and 1.333 s unblockable at −90 % after landing.
- **Now**: a static body moves 0 tiles from every push or pull; skills keep their reach and still hit it (薄绿 S2, 锏 S3, the hookmasters, 莫斯提马 S3 …; distance effects come to nothing). 守墓石像's statue is unblockable, rooted, `noDisplace` and refuses 浮空, its flight keeps a persistent `noDisplace`; 喷气人's 7 s flight is an air unit with `noDisplace`, then the brake and landing windows. Ground enemies keep §20.3's tables; 吉兆飞鳞 / 掠海漂移体 are 失衡免疫 while hovering and movable once grounded.
- [ASSUMED]: a static body's 0.1 s 失衡硬直 is not modelled; a 浮空 that 守墓石像 carries at its knock-out runs out; 见行者 S2 / 歌蕾蒂娅 S3 reach air units, 乌尔比安 S1 does not.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1b-displacement.test.js` (the data guard, 薄绿 vs drones, the act2 m01 flight, the 16 × 5 audit, coins / 流形 / links, the 胄 shell, 喷气人, 守墓石像).

### 21.10 Damage broadcasts in the Hidden Core; the strip by priority (C1) — `match/Match.js startFinalAssault / tickerText FLOW_TICKER_PRIORITY`, `match/finalAssault.js BOSS_HIT_STEPS / SharedBossPool.byPlayer`, `ui/ticker.js`, `main.js` (m.ticker)

- **Seen**: "隐藏boss还没打就出了造成50%伤害播报". **Reproduced** in real bot matches into the 隐秘核心: solo 绝境 seeds 3–5, the hidden leader's first hit (seed 3: 2034 of 900000) announced "超过50%"; co-op 2 seats 险境 seed 3: "超过80%" at 36 % dealt.
- **Cause 1 (server)**: share = `stats.bossDamage / pool.maxHp`, and `stats.bossDamage` (the result's 领袖伤害) sums both boss rounds. **Cause 2 (client)**: the strip played its queue first in, first out, 5.2 s a line; a Final Assault line played 2–8 s into the Hidden Core over its fresh leader.
- **Official** [DATA]: activity_table `autoChessData.broadcastList` comment_boss_hit_1..3 "{0}博士对敌方领袖造成的伤害超过20% / 50% / 80%!" (BOSS_HIT, paramList 0.2 / 0.5 / 0.8, priority 30); constData `broadcastBeginDelay` 1; client 2.7.71 keeps `bossDamage[localPlayer]` per boss battle (research 11 §2.1); research 06 §9.2 "The highest priority wins": BOSS_HIT 30 > CHAR_DAMAGE 20 > SHOP_LEVEL 11 > (bond 10) > GOLDEN_CHAR 2 > CHAR_GIFT 1.
- **Now (server)**: share = the player's damage to that round's `SharedBossPool` (`byPlayer`, fed by every credit path) ÷ its `maxHp`; each threshold once per player per round, a hit passing several announces the highest; `stats.bossDamage` unchanged (both rounds).
- **Now (client)**: lines keep `type`, `playerId`, `priority` and the round they arrived in; a BOSS_HIT line from another round leaves the queue and the strip; a player's newer BOSS_HIT line of the round replaces their older one, queued or on screen; and (integration, §21.19) the queue is ordered by priority, first in first out among equals, the line on screen never cut short, the lowest priority's oldest dropped past 4 queued — the remake's match-flow notices (隐秘核心已解锁, 联防阶段, a player out or gone) at `FLOW_TICKER_PRIORITY` 25 [ASSUMED], content `ticker()` lines 0.
- [ASSUMED]: the threshold test ≥ ("超过"); the per-battle reset of `bossDamage` (inferred); a dropped line is not replayed; the 1 s begin delay is not modelled.
- Tests: `test/match/bosshit-ticker.test.js` (solo 绝境 server-run, 2 and 4 seats client combat, SP_COMBAT=server, a takeover), `test/ui/bosshit-ticker.test.js` (the queue helpers across a 14 → 15 round change, the replacement rule, the priority order, timeline replays).

### 21.11 变形同构体 wearers in the member list and on the card (C2) — `ui/gameLogic.js grantedBonds / pieceBondIds / bondMembers`, `ui/bondStrip.js BondPopup`, `ui/detailPanel.js BondChips / ChessDetail / resolveDetail`, `ui/watchBonds.js ownerBoard`, `battle/runner.js ownerOps`, `sim/snapshot.js unitInfo`, `match/Match.js prepFieldMeta`

- **Seen**: with 变形同构体 (缪尔赛思's 特质 garrison_76 / band 变形者集群) and the matching item the wearer gets the bond's effect, but "那个盟约的人口数量没有把转职之后的干员算进去". **Partly real**: the count that activates the bond already included the wearer everywhere (`bondsMeta.pieceBonds` for the prep count, m.private / m.public, the battle input; `support.unitBonds` in the battle — checked on real matches for all 36 pairings). The client did not: the popup's member list and the wearer's card read only the records' bonds (在场 3/6 next to 成员 2/10, no chip).
- **Official**: band 变形者集群 "与特定装备一同装备时装备者将视为特定盟约成员"; eff_acarm073 "携带者获得额外盟约（由另一件携带装备而定）"; the item's talent lists the 14 pairings; a member counts on the board only (BOARD) and once per operator.
- **Now**: `gameLogic.grantedBonds` = the server's rule (a test pins the three together); the popup lists wearers tagged 同构, the card shows the granted bond as a dashed 同构 chip; a 同构 row opens the wearer's card with its item ids; a card without an own piece lists the carried items read-only under 装备. UnitInfo `items?` (sim `unitInfo`, `Match.prepFieldMeta`) carries an ally operator's item ids, so teammates' popups and cards show wearers too.
- [ASSUMED], unchanged: operator talents that test "【拉特兰】/【卡西米尔】/【叙拉古】… 干员" read the record's bonds (kits tier1 内线 ammo pick, tier5 inFaction, tier6 hasBond, tokens.js 卡西米尔 sword) — no source says whether a converted operator counts for them; 调和's +1 raises 在场 for core bonds without a member row (explained since §21.26: 含调和 +1 and a 调和 row).
- Tests: `test/ui/morph-bonds.test.js`, `test/ui/watch-bonds.test.js`, server-side locks in the match suite.

### 21.12 源石溶剂 is damage; 信仰搅拌机 S3 counters any damage (D1) — `sim/damage.js periodicDamage / isHpLoss / applyHpLoss`, `items/battle.js periodic_damage`, kits tier1 / 2 / 4 / 5, `content/enemies.js`, `bonds/addon/battle.js`, `choices.js`

- **Seen** (community): officially the 源石溶剂 drain sets off 信仰搅拌机 S3, and next to 莫斯提马 it stacks 拉特兰 on its own; the remake never cast S3. **Reproduced** on the real match path (R3; default S3 + 源石溶剂, 莫斯提马 above her, 隐现): v0.1.0 never cast S3 (SP full at 23 s); without enemies she drained to death with 0 layers.
- **Cause**: the drain was `loseHp` — a 流失: no 受击回复 SP, no TAKE_DAMAGE, ignored by shields — and the counter needed an enemy attack and a target.
- **Official**: PRTS 盟约记录 源石溶剂 "受到60真实伤害" (修正 "并非流失"; 备注 "造成无来源真实持续环境伤害" and "携带后，全场范围内的所有敌人类我方单位也会获得此装备的…效果（该效果的付与为我方阵营索敌，可对空，无视目标可选性）"); PRTS 作战机制 (生命流失 "不会触发反伤、受击回复"; "同名buff的默认叠加策略buff只能表现出一个"); PRTS 伤害分类 (BUFF damage includes "自残类型", "弧光锋卫失衡状态下的自残伤害", "深水区/涨潮水蚀"); PRTS 帮助 重装 "受到伤害时释放技能"; PRTS 信仰搅拌机 S3 备注 "反击受到任何伤害后均可触发，无需目标，视为普通攻击"; garrison_22 (莫斯提马): every 6 bullets spent on her 4 tiles → 【拉特兰】 +1 / +2.
- **Now**: `periodicDamage(n)` = 无来源 true damage (canDodge false, tags dot / periodic) with the old credit — shields, 脆弱, `hit` effects, 受击回复 SP and TAKE_DAMAGE all see it, the kill credit is unchanged. S3 counters every damage but a 流失 or an element 损伤, spending a bullet even with no enemy in range (measured without enemies: +5 拉特兰 per cast, elite 2 casts +22; with the R3 wave +7; the browser sim gives the same digests). The drain also ticks on every 敌人类我方单位 of the field (炎佑, a partner's too), credited to nobody, once per second however many carriers, while a carrier is on the field. Real damage instead of 流失 also for 狂暴宿主组长 (悬赏·特异III), 码头水手's drowning ("每秒受到1000点无来源真实伤害"), 弧光锋卫's 失衡 bleed and the 孽罪奇美拉 aura (now radius 1.2, a tick every 0.5 s, one tick per unit — "同类效果取最高"). One rule both ways (`isHpLoss`): 雷蛇 战术防御 and 蒂比 S2 react to real damage; 乌尔比安, 伪装服, 远牙, 录武官's guard, 坚守 thorns and 机变 自愈 skip a 流失; operator 流失 (宴, 风丸, 水月, 华法琳, 烛煌, 史尔特尔, 号角 …), 传递 and the drone link stay 流失. Damage dealt (unit `dmg`, the results' 造成伤害) never counts the source's own side.
- [ASSUMED]: the drain gives 受击回复 SP; the chimera aura is 无来源; a target-less counter is one bullet with no hit; the 炎佑 tick ends with the carriers; drowning keeps its 0.25 s cadence and the 失衡 bleed stays per distance moved (the amounts are official); 心烛 and 扎罗's transfers stay 流失.
- Tests: `test/content/feedback1d-solvent.test.js`, `items.test.js`, `kits_t1t2.test.js` (蒂比).

### 21.13 野鬃's push direction (D2) — `kits/tier1.js` (野鬃), `kits/tier3.js` (琳琅诗怀雅 S3), `content/generic.js`, `sim/Battle.js push`, `constants.js`

- **Seen** (community): "干员野鬃的技能有时会把怪物往攻击方向相反方向推". **Real**: S2 夹枪冲锋 pushed radially at full force (§20.3's table), so a target behind her centre — an enemy that walked through her tile while her single block was taken, grabbed by the §19.2 hand-over — was thrown 1.7–2.14 tiles towards the objective. Measured on act2 m01–m04 (R3 / R5 / R7 official waves, every melee tile × 4 facings): 2689 of 15915 pushes (16.9 %) went more than 0.5 tile against her facing (LEFT 47 %).
- **Official** (client data): her S2 attack ability (charpack char_496_wildmn, anim Skill_2) carries buff `wildmn_s_2[force]` of template `knockback[dir]` = Knockback {`_useSourceDirection` true, `_decreaseForceLevelWhenNotInDirection` 2} (battle/buff_template_data.json): a 方向力 along her deploy direction with the PRTS 推与拉 特殊修正 (> 45° off or < 0.25 tile → radial at 受力等级 −2). Radial = `knockback[relative]`: 琳琅诗怀雅 S3 (`swire2_s_3[knockback]`; PRTS 备注 "推开效果为径向推动"), 山 S3, 莫斯提马 S3. 圣聆初雪 S1 = KnockBackWithCharacterDirection (no correction). Cross-check: 莱伊 S1 `ray_s_1` decreases by 1, matching its PRTS 备注.
- **Now**: 野鬃 S2 = `Battle.push` with `dir` = `unit.fwd` (within 45° the full 中力 table along it: w0 2.14, w1 1.7, w2 0.44, w3 0.12; behind, beside or < 0.25 tile radial at −2: w0 0.44, w1 0.12, ≥ w2 0) — 0 pushes over 0.5 tile against her facing in the same sample; 琳琅诗怀雅 S3's coins are radial from her centre at full force (they went along her facing). The generic kit's text keywords (now also 往攻击方向) are a fallback only. §20.3's table is rewritten accordingly; nothing goes into [ASSUMED].
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1d-push.test.js` (4 facings × ahead / ≤ 45° / behind / beside / < 0.25 tile, the mirrored boss half, a real act2 m01 R5 hand-over, 琳琅 S3).

### 21.14 Special Originium slugs and 暴鸰's bomb (D3, D4) — `tools/local-extract/extract.py ENEMY_SPINES`, `tools/assets/local-enemy-spines.json`, `plan.mjs localEnemySpines`, `fetch-assets --local-spines`, `assets.js spineEntry`, `render/units.js ALIAS_TINT / FORMS`, `content/enemies.js kitBombd`, `snapshot.js form`, `style.js PROJ.droneBomb`

- **D3** ("所有特殊源石虫的模型全表现为普通源石虫"), partly real: 高能 / 冰爆 / 简饲源石虫 and “庞贝” always had their own models; 灼热 / 炽焰源石虫 (enemy_1305_mhslim / _2, the ELEMENT slugs, up to 10 a round) were the plain 源石虫 — no web dump has their models (the asset plan aliases them); the official skeletons exist only in the client (refs/arts/enm_art_12.ab). Root cause located 2026-10-04: Ark-Models *indexes* `1305_mhslim` / `1305_mhslim_2` with an **empty `assetList`** (registered, never uploaded), so the alias chain falls to `enemy_1007_slime` — a different enemy rather than a variant of it. The *mobile* build ships their own model (`enemy_spine/<enemyId>/<enemyId>.{skel,atlas,png}`, straight-alpha pages), but its only public mirror is a community wiki and not a GitHub dump, so it is deliberately **not** an asset source (docs/ASSETS.md "Enemy aliases"): the tinted alias stays the web model until a GitHub dump carries these two.
- **D3 now**: an optional overlay — the manifest keeps the alias as the web model and `enemies[id].spineLocal` names the files of the data/local-assets.json group `spine/enemy/<id>` with the metadata of the committed `tools/assets/local-enemy-spines.json` (data/assets.json is byte-identical with or without the extraction). The client draws the official model when the local manifest lists every file, else (or on a load failure) the web alias tinted toward the slug's own lava colours (`ALIAS_TINT`: 灼热 orange, 炽焰 red-orange, research 07 §5.6) [ASSUMED look]. The 0.1.1 release bundle carries the models (extracted with `node tools/setup.mjs --local`, the `spine/enemy` job). Audit (249 enemies vs the client's prefabs): no other enemy is drawn with another's model; 伊利昂的木驮兽 starts on skin `Full` in the game and is drawn with `default` (unchanged). `fetch-assets --prune` never deletes `public/assets/local` any more.
- **D4** ("暴鸰的炸弹无法正常投放"), partly real: the bomb hit in the trigger tick, with no projectile, and stayed under the drone. **Official** (prefab enemy_1040_bombd / projectile_bombd; PRTS 暴鸰): Boomb plays Attack, the bomb leaves on OnAttack (frame 8) and flies homing at 5 tiles/s; the cast ends when the bomb is gone, ≥ 0.667 s after the release; buff bomb_s: the bomb-less mode S1 (*_2 clips) + speed ×2 ("技能结束后"); `_immuneStunWhenAffecting` 0.
- **D4 now**: the drone holds still at the trigger; 8 ticks later (8/30 s) the release ('atk' droneBomb + the form 'bombed' — UnitInfo `form`); on landing the target 100 % ATK + the 8 tiles around 100 % splash (a target gone mid-flight: the bomb lands where it was; the drone's death after the release does not stop it); at max(landing, release + 0.667) ×2 speed and it flies on. A stun / freeze / sleep before the release interrupts the cast (no drop; the skill re-arms with its 1 s cooldown).
- [ASSUMED]: no drop by a dead drone; the ATK at the release; holding still through the cast; the bomb-less look from the release; an interrupted cast keeps its one trigger; a stun after the release does not stop the cast end; the trigger radius 2 (§20.4).
- Tests: `test/feedback1d-models.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1d-bombd.test.js` (a real wave), `test/render/feedback1d-bombd.test.js`, `test/static-local-art.test.js`.

### 21.15 Enemy ground routes: 战场#04's official diagonal (D5) — `server/sim/grid.js` (`_buildField`, `_spfa`, `smoothChains`, `crossTiles` / `segmentTiles`), `data/stages.json groundPaths*`, test/sim/pathing-official / -blockable / -crosscheck / pathing

- **Seen (real)**: on 战场#04 活性源石 (act1autochess_m04, the only stage with tile_infection) the lower-gate enemies walked (9,10) → (9,8) along row 9, then straight up into (10,8).
- **Official**: every autochess route has `allowDiagonalMove` true and `visitEveryTileCenter` / `visitEveryNodeCenter` false; normal WALK routes have no checkpoints; the client SPFA flow field + Bresenham smoothing (research 08 §3.1; PRTS 作战机制 "所有敌人始终选择最短路径行动…") gives (9,10) → (10,7) → (10,4) → (9,4) → E — a diagonal that touches the floor (10,9) only at a corner.
- **Cause**: playtest #2's road-over-floor smoothing (§14 corrections) counted a diagonal step's corner tiles as floor and bent official diagonals into L shapes even where they cross no floor.
- **Now**: each flow field keeps two pointers per tile with the official distances — the official one, and 0.1.0's playtest #2 preference one restored exactly; `next` is the preference pointer only if its route crosses strictly fewer non-blockable tiles (exact geometry: a segment crosses the tiles whose interior it passes through, a corner touch is not a crossing), ties keep the official pointer (unless the preference one only skips a collinear official waypoint). Kept (playtest #2): 战场#01's lower-gate enemies take the col-8 road; on 战场#02's Final Assault both halves leave the exit via (2,9) / (2,11), so a blocker beside the exit holds them.
- **Audit** (11 stages × 14 gate / field legs = 154 routes): 140 official as merged by the workstream (0.1.0: 109); with the 深水区 refusal (§21.3) 7 routes of the inactive 战场#05(上半), whose canoe water now counts as non-blockable, differ too — 21 of 154 (pathing-official `DEVIATIONS`), each crossing strictly less floor. **Visible**: the boss exits of 战场#04, #05(下半) and #07(下半) are official diagonals — at #07(下半) the enemy passes (2,9) / (2,11) at √0.5 ≈ 0.70711 > the block radius 0.70709997 (§19.2), so an operator there no longer blocks them, as with the official route.
- Unchanged: movement tile centre to tile centre along `next[]` [ASSUMED simplification]; `findPath` / `groundPathTiles` return Bresenham tiles. One-line flip: always `use = o` in `_buildField` (pure official routes; reverses 战场#01's col-8 road).

### 21.16 The live attack range on the card; 烛煌 S3 (E1) — `sim/Battle.js _refreshRange`, `sim/skills.js changesRange`, `shared/loadoutRecord.js attackRangeGrid / traitRangeExtend / extendedGrid`, `shared/protocol.js unitStatsEntry`, `ui/detailPanel.js`, kits tier4 (信仰搅拌机) / tier5 (烛煌) / tier6 (荒芜拉普兰德)

- **Seen**: "干员烛煌开启3技能时候攻击范围不会变". **Partly real**: the sim was right (real 绝境 matches: 80 of 152 S3 hits on tiles only 4-11 reaches, none on 4-11 missed; the cast keeps the initial 3-1 per §20.2), but the battle card's 攻击范围 mini-map stayed 3-1 while the live ATK / interval beside it changed — the battle board has no range overlay.
- **Official**: skill_table skchr_blaze2_3 rangeId 4-11; PRTS 烛煌 S3 备注 "攻击溅射半径1.7；于攻击造成伤害前判定元素爆发并造成元素伤害", "补充后的弹药数量无法超过上限"; PRTS 信仰搅拌机 S3 备注 "此技能的攻击范围不受“攻击距离”属性影响".
- **Now (card)**: `unit.liveRangeGrid` (the running skill's grid, else the unit's own, plus `rangeExtend`) → `unitStatsEntry.range` (allies only) → drawn in battle 4× a second and in the prep preview; 400+ tiles read 全场, a grid beyond 6 × 7 draws smaller cells in the same box.
- **Now (record range)**: `attackRangeGrid` = the range a unit is deployed with — a selected "被动效果：攻击范围扩大" skill (引星棘刺 S3 3-9), else a module's 攻击范围扩大 grid, else its own, plus the 特性's permanent 攻击距离 (`traitRangeExtend`: 信仰搅拌机 SPT-Y +1; 空弦 ISW-A works in 集成战略 only) — used by the card without a live entry, the board overlay, the deploy wheel, the server's `summonRange` and the bot; over all 1284 loadouts it equals the battle's range at deployment.
- **Now (kits)**: 烛煌 S3 = one target on 4-11 + a 1.7 splash (中点判定; it used to hit every enemy inside 4-11); the 灼燃 burst bonus is dealt before the hit, on main and splash victims, from the attack as fired (her last shot lands after `end('ammo')` and keeps it); each landed shot emits fx 'splash'; a burst refills ammo only up to 18 / 21. 信仰搅拌机 精锐 + SPT-Y + S3 was 11 tiles, now exactly 3-13 (`targeting.noRangeExtend`). 荒芜拉普兰德 S1's target grid only selects (`targeting.showOwnRange`: the card keeps 3-1).
- [ASSUMED]: 技能范围 areas (古米, 泥岩, 山 S3, 锏) show while their skill runs; content extra tiles (蕾缪安, 维娜 S3) are not drawn; the 全场 / 6 × 7 thresholds (远牙's line is 2.1 px per cell on an 844 × 390 phone); the bonus also lands on a hit that is then dodged.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1e-skillrange.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1e-range.test.js`, `test/render/feedback1e-splash.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest2.test.js`.

### 21.17 Healers that block keep healing — not reproduced (E2) — `sim/ai.js acquireTargets`, `sim/skills.js _defaultCondition`, `Battle.blockedTargets`, `test/sim/feedback1e-healers.test.js`

- **Seen** (relayed): "走炎盟约的时候，如果是那种会到处跑的BOSS，被医疗阻挡之后，医疗不奶人了，会输出BOSS而且如果是那种强力击类型的技能也会在BOSS身上打出正确数值的伤害"; the user asked to check every healer under every bond.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 (技能策略) "对于医疗干员（咒愈师分支除外），攻击目标为需要治疗的单位"; PRTS 仇恨: a medic's heal uses HP_RATIO_NOT_FULL_ASC (full-HP units excluded, itself counted); PRTS 选择器: blocked units are added only if the selector's side can pick them; 咒愈师 "攻击造成法术伤害".
- **Verified, not reproduced** (real Battle, skills made ready while blocking): the 炎 lineup (9 炎, tier 3, 2 炎佑) — 录武官 normal / elite × S1 / S2 holds 卢西恩 / 不祥幻影, casts while holding, deals 0 damage and keeps healing; every pure healer (医师 / 群愈师 / 疗养师 / 链愈师 / 行医: 26 records, 56 loadouts, each skill cast while blocking) × 23 bonds as a member, and 112 items: 0 damage; the 20 boss / hidden fields: 0 healer damage (healers block 卢西恩 / 不祥幻影 and a 碎铳之簧 with its shield down; the other leaders and parts cannot be blocked by a block-1 unit). 古米 S1 / S2, 塞雷娅 S1, 波登可 S1 heal allies while blocking; 瑕光 S1 / S3 strike + heal by text; 咒愈师 hit what they block (§20.3); 吟游者 make no normal attack.
- **Likely what players saw** (all official): 卢西恩 evades only while unblocked (in the 炎 run 11 % of hits dodged and 1,162 dmg/s while it roams, 0 dodged and 6,751 dmg/s while the medic holds it — the numbers come from the others and 炎佑); a blocking medic heals itself first when it is the most injured; 神经 bursts stun it for 10 s; operators do not turn, so its heal animation faces the boss.
- **Now**: no behaviour change; the rule is written in §5.5, SIM.md, the ai.js header, `Battle.blockedTargets`, targeting.js and PLAYING, and locked by tests. Not checked: whether 华法琳 S2's official 流失 can kill an ally (the remake's can; PRTS gives no non-lethal note).

### 21.18 阵法术师 / 轰击术师 strike every enemy in range (E3) — `sim/professions.js` (SUB.phalanx / blastcaster, `resolveProfile` rangeAoe), `kits/tier4.js` 卡涅利安, the 流形 copy (`content/tokens.js`, `kits/tier6.js`), `render/fx.js`

- **Seen**: "干员卡涅利安的攻击不是真群攻". **Real**: the trait "技能开启时攻击造成群体法术伤害" was one bolt at the priority target + a 1.1 splash — spread enemies took 1 hit of 5 per attack; on act2 m01 R10 the elite 卡涅利安 hit 2.3 of 12.4 enemies in range per attack, 薄绿 2.0 of 12.6; on a real Match every 阵法术师 and 轰击术师 hit exactly 1 target while 2.7–4.7 stood in range.
- **Official**: a 阵法术师 / 轰击术师 attack strikes every selectable enemy on its range at once, the same damage near and far — primary for the 轰击术师: PRTS 作战机制 §AOE伤害判定, a 锁定攻击范围 AoE "在攻击前摇结束时选取范围内的全体目标，同时造成伤害" (its example, 伊芙利特's 炎爆, is her next-attack skill "下次攻击造成…"); for the 阵法术师 Terra Wiki Phalanx Caster (secondary) and PRTS 林 S3 备注 (one normal attack can kill several); supporting only: PRTS 溅射半径一览 has no radius for either (it omits 撼地者 too). 扩散术师 keep a 1.1 splash around the target (格雷伊 1.0, §21.19).
- **Now**: phalanx and blastcaster are `rangeAoe` profiles — `resolveProfile` applies the flag after every override (kit trait included): `allInRange` + instant `'beam'` hits drawn as a line to each victim (no splash ring, no shells). Blocked enemies are struck too (§20.3); a stealthed one only when revealed or blocked. Per-hit kit effects run on every enemy struck (薄绿 S2 拖拽 — a 静态刚体 is not moved, §21.9 —, 卡涅利安 S2 停顿 / 束缚, 阿罗玛 浮空, whose first-attack mark is shared between 阿罗玛 since §21.19). 卡涅利安 (PRTS 备注): a charged S1 keeps the skill-off trait — the guard AND 不攻击 for the whole skill (players will notice); S3's ATK climbs in 1 s steps to +140 % / +200 % at 20 s; the charged mark stacks before the damage, one `billro:mark` per enemy (another 卡涅利安 only adds stacks), ending with its setter's skill. A 流形 copy of a 'beam' becomes a bolt.
- [ASSUMED]: no target cap (no source states one); damage at the attack instant (no wind-up modelled); the beam look.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1e-aoe.test.js` (incl. a real wave counting the damaged enemies per attack id); opt-in `test/render/phalanx-aoe.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`).

| Branch (pool) | Official | 0.1.0 | Now |
|---|---|---|---|
| 阵法术师 (薄绿, 蜜蜡, 卡涅利安, 圣聆初雪) | every enemy in range | bolt + 1.1 splash | all in range, instant |
| 轰击术师 (协律, 阿罗玛) | every enemy on the line | bolt + 1.1 splash | all on the line, instant |
| 扩散术师 (格雷伊, 莫斯提马, 莱恩哈特, 夕, 寒檀) | 1.1 splash (格雷伊 1.0) | 1.1 | 1.1, 格雷伊 1.0 (§21.19) |

### 21.19 Integration follow-ups (`feedback1`) — `render/interp.js`, `render/app.js`, `battle/runner.js`, `screens/game.js`, `sim/Battle.js foesInRadius`, `sim/professions.js`, `sim/constants.js CHAIN_RADIUS`, kits tier1–6, `content/{enemies,tokens,generic}.js`, `content/items/battle.js`, `content/bonds/core.js`, `ui/ticker.js`, `match/Match.js`, `match/bot.js`

The last review of §21.4's client fix, the cross-workstream notes and the last review rounds were checked on the merged branch; the worthwhile items with a primary source are done here (the rest: §21.20):
- **Form fx in the render engine** (review of the final §21.4 fix, major): `render/interp.js isCosmeticEvent` counted every 'fx' as cosmetic, so `processEvents` dropped one stamped > 1.5 game s behind the render clock and a full queue shed it — after a main-thread stall over ≈ 0.78 s real at 2×, on a watched replica after a hidden tab (catch-up frames of 8 game s), after Chrome's intensive throttling, and for the game screen's replayed early buffer (stamped 0). Reproduced in headless Chrome: 转译基底·α ended `dead:null:B_Die` — report #5's look. **Now** a form fx is state for the queue too; one handed out late switches the model without its telegraph, with its closing clip shortened by the lateness and without a change clip that would already have ended. The runner compacts an overflowing hidden-tab backlog (`compactHeld`) instead of re-entering the view, flushes the backlog and a last snapshot of a battle that ended while the tab was hidden, and the game screen buffers the frames of a re-sent field meta for the field on screen until it re-enters it. The browser test stalls the main thread (blocking 1 s long tasks; the old `sleep` left the renderer's ticker running).
- **Area damage and 隐匿** (PRTS 作战机制 §AOE伤害判定 "AOE的判定是对攻击范围内的每个可以被选中的敌人进行判定"; 隐匿 "隐匿状态下的单位一般无法被敌方的索敌机制和Buff选择器选中为目标"): an operator's radius area effects — profession splash, aftershocks, skill circles and zones, counting conditions — use `Battle.foesInRadius` (no untargetable, no unblocked unrevealed 隐匿 enemy); enemy-side auras / heals and physical collisions keep `enemiesInRadius`. The 逐火 embers and every 隐匿 enemy take less splash than in 0.1.0 (officially they took none).
- **Splash radii** (PRTS 溅射半径一览, 特性 / 特殊): 投掷手 0.9 (was 1.0), 链术师 jumps 1.7 (1.8; `CHAIN_RADIUS`), 格雷伊 1.0 (1.1); 迷迭香 S2 末梢阻断 1.5 (the table's skill row; was ×1.3 [ASSUMED]).
- **阿罗玛's 非首次标记** (PRTS 阿罗玛 天赋 备注 "…自身离场时移除自身已施加的标记，不同阿罗玛之间的非首次标记通用"): one mark per enemy (`aroma:bubbled`, the setter as source) shared by every 阿罗玛, removed when its setter leaves (two used to trigger on one enemy; E3 made it show).
- **奥术法阵 on the field** (PRTS 盟约记录 备注, the same as 源石溶剂's "…敌人类我方单位也会获得此装备的“造成伤害时使目标失去特殊能力5秒”效果"): 炎佑's damage silences its target for 5 s while a carrier is on the field [ASSUMED: it ends with its carriers].
- **重生 cooldowns** (PRTS 特殊机制 §重生 "重生结束时，重置自身的通用技能与当前形态的技能冷却为初始冷却"): every ability with a cooldown restarts from its initial cooldown when a 重生 ends (`rebirthCooldowns`: `reborn()`, the husk's 1 s 重生, the statue's instant one).
- **高准度伦蒂尼姆城防自行炮** (悬赏 target; PRTS 技能 "以攻击范围内生命上限最高的我方单位为中心，生成9格的炮击区域，6s内每0.5s对炮击区域中生命比例最高的我方单位进行攻击…(最多进行10次攻击)", 备注 失衡 / 晕眩 / 冻结 / 浮空 / 沉睡免疫 during the animation): the lock is in its range (it was any ally), the area the 3×3 tiles, 10 shots 0.5 s apart (were 3, 1 s apart [ASSUMED]), 失衡免疫 + those immunities over the bombardment [ASSUMED: the animation's length].
- **琳琅诗怀雅 S3's coins** (PRTS 备注 "前方范围2-4内的地面敌方单位与自身阻挡的所有单位"): range_table 2-4 in front plus the units she blocks (her 1-1 attack range before).
- **阿戈尔's devour** (PRTS 盟约记录 "造成5000点物理流失", 修正 "【吞噬】的物理流失来源为被付与目标自身"; PRTS 作战机制: a 物理流失 "会受到目标当前防御力…影响而相应衰减"): 5000 less the target's DEF, still a 流失 (no shields, dodge or multipliers), the kill to the marker (DEF-free [ASSUMED] in 0.1.0).
- **The ticker by priority** (research 06 §9.2, WJ's note): §21.10.
- **AI 托管 and temp** (the promise "你开 AI 托管时，它不会销毁你的道具"): on a human seat under AI 托管 an item left in temp with a full hand gets a hand slot (a bench operator sold) instead of being destroyed; only a hand of nothing but items still drops it, as the temp deadline would.
- **The bot** (§21.6): reconciled with the merged rules as listed there.
- **0.1.0 bug found in the merge**: a 联防 battle timed out during 隐德来希 S3 reported its 心烛 as leaks, so the server refused the honest client's result (fullmatch-coop3 seed 116) — candles carry `mem.noLeak` and `Battle._timeout` skips them.
- **Checked, no change**: 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤 stay unblockable while grounded (their PRTS 天赋 lists 不可阻挡); the database's lpr-0 enemies (简饲源石虫, 节日气球, leader parts, tokens) still cost 0 LP in a leader round (the official `lifePointReduce`); 鸭爵 swaps skip bounty-tagged spawns; 转译基底's bounty coin is paid once.
- **After the independent QA of the merged branch** (residuals, each checked against its source first):
  - **WB's 22-match follow-up merged** (QA major): on the 11-match model 16 of the 58 official bounty drafts of the 22 matches could not be generated (two series-20 cards in an R3 set, named bosses apart at R9, an R11 draft without a giant); with the follow-up every one is reachable — §21.2.
  - **A status of the knocking-out hit** (§21.4): 妮芙 S2's fear (and a 叙拉古 6 proc, 铃兰's slow…) landed after the 重生's cleanse — the same hit runs `if (target.alive) applyStatus` after the kill, and the husk is still alive — so an ember walked unblockable + 隐匿 for ≈ 3 s more. A unit both 无敌 and 无法选中 now takes no status from the other side (PRTS 无敌 "无法被不同阵营选中"; `Battle.applyStatus`).
  - **余 S2 and 自缚** (in 0.1.0 already): 厚礼上宾 teleported a 守墓石像 in its 转换模式 onto 余's tile. PRTS 余 S2 备注 "处于消失状态的/持有自缚的单位不视为可达目标"; the statue's PRTS 天赋 lists 自缚. Flag `selfBound` (the statue, the 自缚 leaders, the 胄 parts' anchor) beside `noMove` — not `noMove` itself, which 束缚 sets too.
  - **信仰搅拌机 S2** (in 0.1.0 already, [ASSUMED] then): with fewer than 30 bullets the lethal hit went through. PRTS 备注 "弹药量不足时仍可抵挡致命伤害，此时将消耗所有剩余弹药并退出技能状态" and "自身持有不死时此效果不会生效": it blocks, spends what is left and ends; while 坚固维式重锤's 不死 window holds her (`items/battle.js holdsUndying`: this deployment's window) it steps aside (骑士戒律's earlier 不死 already did).
  - **Late form fx after a hidden tab or a catch-up** (§21.4's client half): the runner sent the hidden-tab backlog and a catch-up frame's events stamped with the frame's game time, so the render engine saw a 16 s old form fx as on time and replayed the 2 s change clip on a walking 转译基底·α (the final form was right). Such a step now drains its events every `EV_SLICE` (15) ticks and each batch keeps its own game time (`battle/runner.js stepEntry / drainSlices / heldAt`); render/app.js then skips the change clip as for a stall.
  - **The bot's bench shed** (§21.6): the buy loop's pre-emptive shed (one hand slot left) sold the single it had just bought for 3–5 back for 1 — the buy picks by lineup gain, the shed by piece value (co-op 绝境, 2 AI 托管 + 2 bots, seeds 21–26: 29 same-prep buy → sell, all from that line, vs 7 in 0.1.0). It now never sells a piece that came this prep (`sellWeakestHand keepFresh`); 3 remain, from other paths (a reward pick's room, `arrange`, `sellJunk`).
  - **Checked, not changed**: the QA's other notes are on the user-facing list (wording) or the release packaging (the 灼热 / 炽焰源石虫 models come from the gitignored local extraction: `public/assets/local/spine/enemy/enemy_1305_mhslim{,_2}` + `data/local-assets.json`).
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1-followups.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-integrate.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-residuals.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1-transform.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-runner-forms.test.js`, `test/render/interp.test.js`, `test/ui/bosshit-ticker.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js`, `test/match/bot-smarter.test.js`, `test/content/{enemies_bosses,bonds_core,kits_alt_t4,kits_alt_t6}.test.js`.

### 21.21 坚固维式重锤's lock, once per deployment; 休露丝's discount (F1, F2) — `content/items/battle.js hammerAcquire / deploymentOf / holdsUndying / revivedInPlace / PRIO_REVIVE / PRIO_RESPAWN`, `content/bands/battle.js PRIO_BAND_REVIVE`, `kits/tier4.js` (信仰搅拌机 S2), `content/bands/meta.js` (休露丝), `match/PlayerState.js buy / pickReward`

- **F1 seen**: "维多利亚坚固锤子的锁血没生效". **Official** (act2autochess eff_acarm043 / eff_acgarm043): "首次受到致命伤害时生命值不低于1，持续8秒", `undeadable_duration` 8 (the latest upstream data agrees; PRTS still prints the act1 15 s); 异常效果 不死 "重设常规生命值时不会使其低于1".
- **Measured on 0.1.0, the lock works**: every HP loss of a unit without a boss pool fires `fatal` (hits of every type, element bursts, 无来源 damage, `loseHp` 流失) — on every visible chess against scaled real enemies, in forced-hammer bot matches and in the browser's local battle.
- **Why it looked missing**: it was once per battle — the hammer state lives on the unit, which a redeploy reuses, so a carrier knocked out after its lock came back with none (forced-hammer co-op matches, 3 绝境 + 4 困难: 27 of 382 carrier knock-outs); the 阿戈尔 battle-start devour (a lethal hit on the operator in front: 10 of 179 locks on 绝境) and the carrier's own 源石溶剂 spend it; with an M3茧甲 equipped first, its revive (same priority, registered first) took the first lethal hit.
- **Now**: once per **deployment** — the user's first-hand memory of the official mode (2026-10-03, "每次部署一次"; the text says only 首次 where M3茧甲 says 一场战斗 and 拉特兰桥夹 每次部署): a new deployment of the carrier (every deploy — the redeploy after a knock-out, a 突袭 retreat + redeploy, 阿戈尔's 立刻复活 — and an in-place 复活, below) re-arms the lock and ends a window still running. The `fatal` order is kits' own savers (10 … −60) → items' 不死 `PRIO_REVIVE` −100 → items' 复活 (M3茧甲) `PRIO_RESPAWN` −101 → 埃芒加德 −110: PRTS (M3茧甲 / 埃芒加德 / 阿戈尔 备注) "“复活”的实现方式为：受益者因移动之外的原因退场时下次部署的再部署时间和费用归零" — a revive acts on a knock-out, which a 不死 prevents. No status icon shows the 8-s window (the client has none for 不死; a one-shot fx marks the lock).
- **QA after the integration, fixed**: (1) the lock lived in the hooks of the carrier's hammer grants — a hammer lent by 萨尔贡 × 娜仁图亚 (60 s) took its re-arm hook along when the lend ran out, so a borrower knocked out while lent came back with its lock spent when lent again (real Match, 萨尔贡 6: no lock in the second deployment), and a window started through the lend ended with it (knocked out 4 s into its 8 s). The lock now belongs to the deployment — `deploymentOf` (the deploy counter plus the in-place revives) marks the deployment whose lock is spent — and one battle-level hook holds every running window (`holdsUndying`, also what 信仰搅拌机 S2 steps aside for): a borrower is re-armed in its next deployment like an owner, a lend refreshed in the same deployment re-arms nothing, and a window outlasts the lend [ASSUMED: the 异常效果 不死 outlasts its source]. (2) M3茧甲 and 埃芒加德 revive in place, which kept the lock spent, while 阿戈尔 5's 立刻复活 — a real redeploy — re-armed it; PRTS describes all three the same way ("“复活”的实现方式为：受益者因移动之外的原因退场时下次部署的再部署时间和费用归零"), so under the per-deployment rule a revive is a new deployment: both in-place revives now call `revivedInPlace` and the revived carrier locks again on its next lethal hit [ASSUMED].
- **F2 seen**: a 谢拉格 taken from the promotion reward used up 休露丝's "每回合购买的首名<谢拉格>干员消耗资金为1". **Not real**: only `g.buy` dispatches `onBuy`, where every 购买 counter lives (休露丝, 玛恩纳, 升华, 整备); the promotion reward, 寻呼模块's pick and the 拟态物质 / 紧急调度券 / 简易通讯机 grants never touch it (pinned through real intents; META `onBuy`). Buying the copy that completes a merge uses it, as it should.
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20.
- Tests: `test/content/feedback1f-hammer.test.js` (per deployment, a retreat + redeploy, the devour, the M3茧甲 order, the M3茧甲 / 埃芒加德 revives, a lent hammer), `test/sim/feedback1-residuals.test.js` (信仰搅拌机 S2 and the window of an earlier deployment), `test/match/feedback1f-sciurus.test.js`.

### 21.22 蒂比's 起飞 is 对地规避 (F3) — `sim/targeting.js evadesGround / canTargetAlly`, `sim/damage.js liftoffEvades` (DamageInfo `ignoreSelect`), `Battle.applyStatus / _blockerFor`, `buffs.js` (`liftoff`), `kits/tier2.js LIFTOFF_FLAGS`, `kits/tier5.js` (隐德来希), `content/enemies.js`, `content/bosses.js`

- **Seen**: after 蒂比 took off, enemies still hit her. **Real**: 起飞 was `unit.ground = false`, which freed her from melee enemies only; ranged ground enemies kept shooting her and their areas and statuses kept landing (46 of 241 enemy types hit her while airborne; the client replica the same).
- **Official**: ba.liftoff "不阻挡地面敌人且不会被地面敌人攻击，可以阻挡飞行敌人"; PRTS 术语释义: 起飞 includes 对地规避, a 无法选择 effect only selectors whose 行动方式 is ground respect, and she keeps a ground 行动方式; PRTS 异常效果: direct picks and abilities that "无视无法选择" are not stopped (these effects act "仅在选择时"); PRTS 作战机制: an AoE judges only selectable units.
- **Now**: 起飞 is the skill flag `liftoff` (S1 and S2, normal and elite). No ground enemy (not `Unit.isFlying`) selects her — normal attacks, ability picks, 周围四格 additions, chains, bounces, shells, the “帝国的甲胄” barrage, 鼠王's 沙狱, 烹泉's blast and steam, ground zones (集团军重型火炮's 燃烧区域 is "不可对空", 迷彩 alone exempted) and their statuses; the pipeline refuses a ground enemy's damage on her before the `hit` hook, so a refused hit neither sets off her S2 nor restarts 片场工作指南. She blocks flyers only; flyers, 近地悬浮 and 浮空 enemies still attack her with no 对空 check; she stays a ground unit (污染秽蚀 at the low-ground 50/s; 隐德来希 S2's 血镰 on her cuts air).
- **Still reaches her** (no selection — DamageInfo / `applyStatus` `ignoreSelect`, or 无来源): the "无视无法选择" abilities (【污染秽蚀】, 【盲信之誓】, 萨卡兹悖谬暴虐兵长's 暴击 splash — her 片场工作指南 may dodge it); 碎铳之簧's counter and 重装侦察兵's 暴露 on their attacker; the 刺胄之弹 / 剑 / 锤 blasts (flying units); the ticks of a debuff a ground enemy put on her before take-off (出血, 沙狱, burning, 淤困, 【自然涌动】 — their mods stay); sourceless damage (毒雾, the chimera aura, 源石溶剂).
- **QA after the integration, fixed**: 卢西恩，“猩红血钻”'s 【aoe】 (radius 2) and 锏's CircleAttack (first form, radius 1.5) decided to cast by counting every ally in their radius, so an airborne 蒂比 alone in range set them off and the blast skipped her (6 casts in 60 s, 0 hits on her); they count only the allies they can hurt (`!evadesGround`) [ASSUMED: no PRTS line states the trigger]. The player text (PLAYING §10, CHANGELOG) now says that ground-enemy auras / field effects and counters on her as an attacker still reach her.
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20. Audit: 蒂比 is the mode's only 起飞 / 对地规避 operator; outside F3, ranged enemies still pick invulnerable allies (锏 S3, 泥岩 S3) and enemy splash still hits 隐匿 allies (§19.1).
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1f-liftoff.test.js` (14), opt-in `test/render/feedback1f-liftoff.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`), `kits_t1t2` / `kits_alt_t2`.

### 21.23 深巡 on a fenced tile; fenced units block no ground enemy (F4) — `sim/Battle.js _blockerFor`

- **Seen**: "干员深巡放在围栏围着的地块里不会主动攻击". **Official**: the fenced tiles are 围墙 (`tile_fence_bound`, "可放置近战单位，敌方无法进入") and 围栏 (`tile_fence`, "不可以通行") — LOW, buildable ALL, passable to flyers only; PRTS 围墙 / 围栏 地形机制 "部署在其中的单位，若当前阻挡类型为'地面阻挡'则无法阻挡敌人"; 深巡 is a 哨戒铁卫 ("能够阻挡三个敌人，可以进行远程攻击", range 2-2); defender skills fire "受到伤害时" (PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助).
- **Not reproduced**: from a fenced tile she attacks every targetable enemy on her range tiles — every deployable fenced tile of the 7 stages that have them, every direction facing walkable ground, normal and elite; the Final Assault halves and 联防; a real Match; the browser's battle (7 attacks, 1977 damage). Targeting reads range tiles only (no path or line-of-sight condition).
- **Likely what players saw** (official): in some directions her 3-tile line faces only fence or forbidden tiles; 阻隔工事 crates hold some fenced tiles from the start (act1 m01: 3 of each 6-tile block; act1 m04, act2 m03); nothing hits her there, so her skill rarely fired (since §21.29 her default S2 casts with an enemy in range, a deliberate deviation; S1 still waits for a hit); an own-tile melee unit never attacks from a fenced tile.
- **Fixed** (found while checking F4): a push or pull stops an enemy at the fence edge, 0.5 from the fenced unit — inside the 0.7071 ground radius — and 0.1.0 let that unit block it (薄绿 S2 on a fenced tile held the enemies she dragged). `_blockerFor` now refuses ground blocking from a tile ground units cannot pass (on the stages exactly the `b` tiles).
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20. Tests: `test/sim/feedback1f-fence.test.js`; PLAYING §10 tip.

### 21.24 Knocked-out operators keep their tile (F5) — `sim/Battle.js downOn / restTile / _layBody / isReservedTile / redeploy / _checkRedeploys / _downState / snapshot`, `sim/units.js body`, `content/bonds/addon/battle.js` (突袭, 不屈), `content/bonds/core.js` (阿戈尔), kits tier1 / 3 / 4 free-tile helpers, `render/interp.js`, `render/units.js setDown`

- **Seen**: "突袭盟约的干员会移动到濒死干员的尸体上". **Real**: a knocked-out operator left the occupancy map at once and only its home tile stayed reserved — a 突袭 member that fell after its jump lay on its landing tile, which the next jump picked; a timed redeploy came back on its home, where a teammate that had jumped there lay (96 real battles: 6 placements on a body; the shared boss field mirrored: 38).
- **Official** (PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §作战阶段 单位部署): "干员退场后…原地留下一个“倒地干员”…满足再部署条件时，移除场上的该倒地干员并自动部署至该位置"; "倒地干员所在地块视为可部署，但所有我方单位在此处的部署行为将被阻止"; "若干员被击倒的位置为其他干员或召唤物的初始位置，则在被击倒后，尝试返回其自身的初始位置". The 突袭 text "再部署至一名地面敌人周围" adds no tile rule.
- **Now**: a knocked-out operator lies on `body` — where it fell, or its own home when it fell on another board piece's home and its home is free (x / y / tile keep where it fell for the 被击倒时 handlers). Every redeploy comes back there (timed, 不屈, 阿戈尔), also for an operator forced out by its own effects (余烬, 耀骑士临光 S2, 骑士戒律 + 竞技旗, 伊内丝 S3, a 商人 that cannot pay; §22.15); only the 突袭 retreat leaves no body. `downOn`: `_deploy` (redeploys, the 突袭 landing, summons), `spawnDevice` and `relocate` refuse the body tile for every ally; `isReservedTile` reports it, so the 突袭 landing, tactical points, summon / device pickers and 维娜's lions skip it. b.snap `down` carries the tile (§8.2) and the client draws the body there; WAIT_TILE matches the `_checkRedeploys` hold-back and is a safeguard. After the fix: 0 placements on a body in 526 jumps. **Superseded**: "later redeploys use the board tile" — an operator moved off its board tile (a 突袭 jump, 乌尔比安's anchor) now redeploys where it fell; research 01 §4.3 carries the PRTS quotes.
- **QA after the integration, fixed**: rule 3 counted only the board pieces that were not removed, but a summon leaves its home free only once it has expired or been killed (a docked one keeps its tile reserved), so the 召唤物 clause never applied — a 突袭 member that landed on the home of 浊心斯卡蒂's expired 海嗣 and fell there lay on it and kept the 海嗣 (远古血亲) from coming back. Every board piece's home counts now, on the field or not. Cost: the body bookkeeping added about 2.5 % per tick on 519 real specs (8 % on boss fields; whole matches and the perf budgets unchanged); `_checkRedeploys` now checks the DP before the tile scans, so an operator waiting for DP costs no ally scan — on the same 519 specs, against the integration head, the same tick counts and boss fields 11.9 → 10.9 µs per tick (a tile → body map is left for later).
- [ASSUMED]: §21.20. Tests: `test/sim/feedback1f-downed-tile.test.js` (rule 3 for a summon that has expired or been killed), `test/render/feedback1f-down-tile.test.js`, opt-in `test/render/feedback1f-down-tile.browser.test.js`; `downelem`, `bonds_addon`, `engine-requests`, `robustness` follow the rule.

### 21.25 Hardening credited to pull requests (PR #2, PR #7, PR #10, PR #14) — `match/effectsMeta.js dispatch / stillEquipped`, `tools/fetch-assets.mjs shrinkGuard`, `tools/assets/manifest.mjs droppedEntries`, `assets.js loadImageElement`, `ui/guide.js preload`, `css/screens/loadout.css`

- **The prep dispatcher's equipped items** (pointed out in PR #2, whose 突变细胞 bench rule §21.1 now follows — official footage confirmed it —, its merge path excepted): step 5 iterated `holder.items` live while handlers move and destroy pieces — normal 博士投影 destroys itself at the round start (the splice skipped the next item on that holder), 突变细胞 transforms its holder (the next item ran with a holder that was gone). It now walks a snapshot of the owned chess and of each holder's items and runs an item only while it is still equipped on its still-owned holder; a piece gained or equipped meanwhile waits for the next dispatch (effectsMeta header, META §2.2). No shipped item has a second hook on those events, so play is unchanged; a synthetic spy handler pins the rule (`test/match/feedback1-dispatch-snapshot.test.js`). QA after the integration: the items were read per holder as the walk reached it, so an item equipped meanwhile onto a holder not yet walked ran in the same dispatch and an item moved to a later holder ran twice (latent: no shipped item equips or moves items on those hooks); every [holder, item] pair is now taken before the first item runs, and two more synthetic cases pin it.
- **fetch-assets never shrinks the committed manifest by accident** (pointed out in PR #7, whose `data/assets.json`, regenerated on a machine where some downloads failed, was 42 audio entries short — all 42 still resolve upstream): a rebuilt manifest leaves out every entry whose files are missing on that machine. When it would lose an entry of the current `data/assets.json`, the run keeps the current file, prints the entries it would drop (also the report's `droppedEntries` / `manifestWritten`) and exits 1; `--allow-shrink` (or `--prune`) writes it (ASSETS.md; `test/fetch-assets-shrink.test.js`).
- **crossOrigin on the two `new Image()` loads** (PR #10, merged on GitHub): with `/assets/` served from another origin (a CDN or object storage) an image loaded without CORS approval taints the canvas and breaks the WebGL textures (an empty map, a white 3D board). `assets.js loadImageElement` — the asset store's only image path, §21.27's seeded store included — and `ui/guide.js preload` set `crossOrigin = 'anonymous'`; a same-origin page is unaffected.
- **The loadout screen's background layer** (pointed out in PR #14): `css/screens/loadout.css` `.lo > * { position: relative }` — one class, a later rule — overrode `.lo__bg { position: absolute }`, so the mint glow and grid layer was a 0-px flex item and never drew. Now `.lo > :where(:not(.lo__bg))`: the same one-class specificity for every other layer, never the background. Checked in headless Chrome at 1920×1080 and 844×390 (touch): the layer covers the overlay and every other box of the screen is where it was (to 0.1 px). Tests: `test/ui/loadout.test.js` (the cascade), opt-in `test/ui/loadout.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`: the layer's box and stacking at both sizes).

### 21.26 Gaps found while triaging GitHub issues #1 / #8 (v0.1.1)

Where: `ui/gameLogic.js morphPairings / HARMONY_BOND / harmonyMembers`, `ui/detailPanel.js MorphPairings / MorphGrantLine / ItemDetail / ChessDetail / BondChips`, `ui/bondStrip.js BondPopup / BondStrip`, `match/bondsMeta.js computeBonds / bondList`, `shared/bandBonds.js` (→ bands.json `bondIds`), `match/gamedata.js bandBondIds`, `match/bot.js botPickBand`, `screens/bandDraft.js BandOffTag / BandOffNote`, `ui/gameLogic.js bandOffBonds`, `tools/botbench.mjs` (`--band`), `match/fields.js layerAllowanceOf`, `public/dev/game-mock.js` (variants `funny` / `morph` / `harmony`). Three gaps the user decided to close in 0.1.1 (no rule changes), and a false rejection of honest client results found on the way.

- **变形同构体 shows its pairings** (issue #1: players read the "转职球" as doing nothing). Its text says "具体对应关系可在模拟中查看本装备天赋栏", and no screen showed that 天赋栏. **Now** the item card (shop, reward, hand / temp: `ItemDetail`) has a 天赋 section: "搭配以下装备时，携带者视为对应盟约的成员：", then one line per bond in bonds.json order — 【bond】 and the items whose `giveBondId` is it (one name per item family, by tier): the 14 pairings of the official talent (character_table trap_1073_acarm073, "“炎国短刀”→【炎】盟约 …"; a test checks the two against each other), its "“维式重锤”系列装备" as the 5 hammers — 18 items, built from the same `giveBondId` the server counts with (`morphPairings`; `pieceBonds` / `grantedBonds`). A bond the mode never activates is struck through with 本局禁用 (`modeOffBonds`, §21.7; 标准: 拉特兰 阿戈尔 卡西米尔 奥术 突袭). On a wearer's card (its 装备 rows; also a teammate's unit and the card a 同构 row opens) the pairing it wears is highlighted — 生效中, or 已搭配 when that bond is off — and a wearer without one reads 暂未生效. A bond item's card and row say "与变形同构体一同装备时，携带者视为【X】成员" (`MorphGrantLine`). The 机密商店 cards have no detail view and keep the official text. Phones: the list keeps ≥ 9 px (px floors in `game-panels.css`).
- **调和's +1 explained** (issue #1: "一些不考虑整备区的盟约计算人数时也会把整备区的干员算进去" — the +1 read as a miscount; §21.11 had left "调和's +1 raises 在场 for core bonds without a member row"). The rule is unchanged (§6.3; one +1 total, research 02 §2.1 [ASSUMED]). **Now** a bond state whose count holds the +1 carries `harmony: 1` (`computeBonds`) and both views send it — m.private `bonds[]` and m.public `players[].bonds[]` entries `{ …, harmony? }` (absent otherwise; not in the battle input, `bondSnapshot`) — so the client never re-derives the rule. The popup says 在场 n（含调和 +1） and a 调和 row heads its member list, naming the 调和 operators on that board (`harmonyMembers`: 缪尔赛思, Pith's 盟约·辅助干员; a tap opens the first one's card; with that board not on screen "调和已激活：核心盟约激活人数 +1") — for your own strip and a teammate's (§20.15: the entry from m.public, the operators from the field on screen). The strip disc's and the card chip's titles add （含调和 +1）.
- **Bots skip strategies built around a switched-off bond**: in 标准 the bot took 潘格尼尼 (【定制铳械】 an elite <拉特兰>), 克莱门莎 (<阿戈尔>) or 玛恩纳 (<卡西米尔>) in 5 of 200 solo and 23 of 800 co-op picks (seeds 1–200). bands.json `bondIds` holds a band's tie, read from its own data at build time (`tools/build-data.mjs` through `shared/bandBonds.js`; `GameData.bandBondIds` and the client read the field) — the bond names in <…> of its text (the official note "在<X>部分干员缺席时体验可能不完整" among them; a bracketed item or enemy adds nothing) and the bond ids / bond pools of its blackboards (`bond`, `bond_id`; `pool` → choices.json `pools[pool].bond`, 潘格尼尼's pool_char_later) — nine bands are tied (杜遥夜 炎, 佩佩 / 娜仁图亚 萨尔贡, 哈洛德 维多利亚, 休露丝 谢拉格, 潘格尼尼 拉特兰, 克莱门莎 阿戈尔, 玛恩纳 卡西米尔, 贾维 叙拉古). `botPickBand` weighs a band tied to a bond of `gd.modeInactiveBonds` 0 — one rng draw per pick, the default band when every offered band is excluded: 0 of 200 and 0 of 800 in 标准, while the other modes keep exactly the earlier picks (绝境: 5 / 200, 23 / 800). `tools/botbench.mjs --band` still forces any band.
- **The strategy draft marks them 本局禁用** for human players (the owner's follow-up): a band whose `bondIds` meet the mode's inactive bonds (`gameLogic.bandOffBonds`, the same field and rule as the bot — a test pins the two in every mode) carries a 本局禁用 tag on its card and, in the detail pane, "本局禁用【拉特兰】盟约，此策略效果可能无法发挥" with the bond struck through (§21.7's look); it stays selectable — the official game lets you pick it.
- **Found on the way — the client-result layer bound** (`match/fields.js layerAllowanceOf / validateClientResult`): with the bots' new picks the full-match suite's coop/FUNNY/107 (4 humans on AI 托管) reached a 6-谢拉格 freeze board at R12 (初雪 + 银灰: garrison_28, 50 % +1 per freeze in range, no per-battle cap in the data; 凛御银灰 hands out a 60 % +2) whose honest +112 谢拉格 layers passed the flat 60 + 4·12 = 108, so the server rejected the client and re-simulated (SP_VERIFY=all agrees with the client on all 57 battles of that match). Uncapped traits are common (菲莱 / 百炼嘉维尔 per skill, 斯卡蒂 per 2 kills, 莫斯提马 per 6 bullets …) and capped ones reach 100–200 (史尔特尔, 荒芜拉普兰德), so the flat bound now adds, per bond, what the player's IN_BATTLE layer 特质 can add to it — each trait of its units and each one their ADD_BOND traits hand out (once per operator), on the bonds it names, up to the cap the sim applies (`max_add_count_per_battle`, the handed-out 华法琳 trait's `GRANTED_CAP_OVERRIDE`); a trait without a cap — or a 魔王, whose +extra counts toward none — leaves its bonds bounded only by the 999 room. A forged gain on any other bond meets the flat bound as before.
- Normative lines rewritten with it: §6.3 (`harmony` in the views), §6.6 (the band pick), §8.2 / §8.3 (`bonds[]` entries `harmony?`), §14 / §20.12 (the layer bound), §21.11 (its 调和 note).
- Tests: `test/ui/feedback1-gaps.test.js`, `test/match/feedback1-gaps.test.js`; opt-in `test/ui/feedback1-gaps.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`; 1920×1080, 844×390, 756×366; `SP_E2E_OUT` moves the screenshots).

### 21.27 Client fixes for GitHub issues #5 and #8 (v0.1.1) — `ui/gameLogic.js prepCameraFor / foldCamera`, `screens/game.js`, `ui/fieldHost.js hudBands / cornerBand / seedAssets`, `render/app.js`, `css/devices.css`, `assets.js`, `render/units.js`

Two public GitHub issues after 0.1.0, both client-only. Normative lines rewritten with them: §9 (`setCamera` `shop`, the model fallback), §17.1 (the quiet budget), §19.8 (the collapsed shop).

- **#5 seen**: "准备阶段，收起商店界面时，界面并不会进行缩放（观战及作战时无此现象）". **Real**: folding the shop (收起) was UI state only — the own prep camera was requested once on entering prep, never with `shop`, so render/projection.js's official shop-collapsed camera (`left_prepare_camera_param` / `*_boss_prepare_*`, `presetCamera` `shop: false`) was never used, and `hudBands` always reserved the bar (measured: 110 px per tile at 1920×1080, 36 at 844×390, 31 at 756×366, the same folded).
- **Now**: while the own prep board is on screen and the bar is shown folded, the game requests `prepCameraFor(pub, myId, folded)` — the prep camera with `shop: !folded` — whenever the fold changes, with the normal 0.75 s flight (`foldCamera`): never over the pen (it folds the bar itself and returns to the camera it left), on a teammate's board, in battle or for an eliminated player (no bar); deferred while a piece is dragged or its direction is chosen (the drop target and the wheel sit on tiles of the camera in use) and applied when that ends; unfolding returns to exactly the shop camera; the fold still resets at combat start. The view hands the request's flag to `hud(kind, size, { shop })` (also on resize). The folded band is the higher of the tab (`.shopbar-tab`: .8rem + 3 px, above the bottom safe-area inset) and the corner buttons' hit areas (交流 / ⚙ / 📖 / ⛶, measured by `cornerBand`; on touch screens a 34 px button takes taps 5 px above itself, `--tap-min` 44 px): with the tab alone the corner covered bench pad 0 at 756×366 and the near edge of pads 0–2 at 844×390. Below 768 CSS px the corner wraps into two rows only because the open bar would cover it; with the bar folded it stays on one row (css/devices.css). Picking reads the camera of every frame. [ASSUMED] the folded band (the official collapsed HUD is not in the extracted bundles).
- Measured (headless Chrome; px per tile at row 10; every bench pad fully pressable open and folded, also the Final Assault prep halves): 1920×1080 109.8 → 130.7 (the official camera at its own scale, panned up 46 px to clear the tab); 844×390 35.9 → 44.3; 756×366 (touch) 31.4 → 40.1. A real drag deploy and a tap on a piece work folded; a drag or the wheel defer the flight; scouting (the 'normal' camera), the battle camera and the next prep are unchanged.
- **#8 item 5 seen**: "游览器切换页面后所有干员会变成方块+水滴的图标" — the image-less placeholder (render/textures.js) on every operator. **A plain tab switch does not reproduce it; a reload does** (phone browsers and Chrome's Memory Saver discard background tabs and reload them on return; the game resumes into the match): the asset store downloaded /data/assets.json a second time beside the game data's copy, `createFieldView` waited ≤ 4 s for it, every view looked its model and avatar up once in its constructor, and a failed download kept `{}` for the page — placeholders for good. Related without a reload: a battle that began in a hidden tab built no views (they are built in animation frames), nothing referenced a skeleton, and the quiet budget (§17.1) unloaded all of them ≈ 18 s later, so back in the tab every unit was an avatar diamond until its model downloaded again; a model that failed or timed out (20 s) was never retried.
- **Now**: the field host seeds the asset store with the game data's copies (`seedAssets` → `assets.seed` / `seedLocal`; the match screen already waits for them, so no second download). Without one, `ready()` retries a transient failure after 600 ms and 2 s (like data.js), then resolves `{}` without keeping it: the next `ready()` and a bounded background backoff (5 / 15 / 30 / 60 s) fetch again, and `onChange` tells the field view when a manifest arrives late — every view re-resolves what it could not draw (`UnitView.retryAssets`; prep item icons through `setPrep` / `ItemView.setIcon`). A view whose model failed or timed out loads it again after 2 / 6 / 15 / 30 s (`SPINE_RETRY_MS`; frames only, so never while hidden), `acquire(entry, { retry: true })` dropping the remembered failure; showing the tab again retries at once (also a missing manifest). Battle mode holds the Spine cache (`spine.hold()`, from `enterBattle` to the next `setPrep` or destroy): no quiet budget meanwhile, the 48 MB idle budget still applies, and the quiet budget keeps freeing everything in the lobby, room and result screen (the policy is in the assets.js header).
- Verified (headless Chrome, no console errors): a reload with every assets.json request delayed 6 s, with only a second download delayed (the 0.1.0 race: none is made now) and with the first 4 requests failing — operators drawn as Spine models at once; the first 6 failing — placeholders until the store's first background try 5 s later, then Spine models; a battle started in a hidden tab, back after 25–30 s (still running, ended meanwhile, or paused with Spine downloads slowed to 4 s) — every unit a model within 0.3 s of the return, no diamonds (before: the cache was empty from ≈ 18 s); six solo rounds with two battles started hidden — the cache follows the roster (4 → 24 skeletons, 6.9 → 25.2 MB estimated, idle ≤ 11 MB, under the 48 MB budget), never a diamond or a placeholder.
- Tests: `test/ui/issue5-fold-camera.test.js`, `test/render/issue8-placeholders.test.js`, `test/ui/playtest5-ui.test.js` (wiring), `test/render/assets.test.js`; opt-in `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/mock.e2e.test.js` (fold / unfold at 1920×1080 and 844×390).

### 21.28 Match info in the strategy draft (GitHub issue #8 item 1, v0.1.1) — `ui/matchInfo.js`, `screens/briefing.js`, `screens/bandDraft.js draftInfoStatus`, `ui/enemyDrawer.js InfoTab`, `css/screens/draft.css .minfo-dlg`

- **Reported** (public issue #8, item 1, "选策略时没法返回查看禁用的干员和盟约"): the disabled bonds and the banned operators were shown only in the INFO_CHECK briefing, but a strategy is chosen partly by them. The owner took it into 0.1.1 as a remake addition (no official screen is copied; the look is the briefing's).
- **One component**: `ui/matchInfo.js` — `matchInfoModel(pub, { bonds, chess, mode })` (pure: `gameLogic disabledBondSets` over `config.modes[modeId].inactiveBondIds` — the drawn set D "阵容不完整" and the mode's static inactive bonds "本局禁用", e.g. 标准's 10; every bond by `bondOrder`, then `identifier`, split 核心 / 附加; the banned operators of m.public `bannedChess`, known chess only, by tier; banned members per bond) and the hookless blocks `MatchBondRow` / `MatchLegend` / `BannedOperators` / `MatchInfo` (greyed disc with ✕, the red badge, the `briefingBondTip` tooltip, the legend, the 本局禁用干员 grid). The briefing's right column is `MatchInfo`; the in-game 本局信息 tab (left 🔍) reads the same model for its compact chips and avatars (its banned list is now by tier, like the briefing's).
- **The draft**: 查看禁用盟约与干员 under the order list opens `MatchInfoDialog` — `MatchInfo` in the components.js `Modal`, read-only, closed by 关闭, a tap outside or Esc (a press on the backdrop closes it on `mousedown`, so the click never reaches what lies under it — e.g. 确认选择). The draft runs on underneath: the dialog's footer repeats the turn and its seconds (`draftInfoStatus`: 轮到你决策 / X 决策中 + the step header's number, orange at ≤ 10 s; 已选择「…」after the own pick); a turn change (a pick, a skip, a turn that runs out, an AI pick) or the own pick closes it; the end of the draft unmounts it with the screen; it never changes the highlighted band (`g.bandFocus`) or the buttons.
- **Sizes**: on desktop the briefing's own sizes (1920×1080: no scrolling); every size in the dialog is max(design rem, px floor), so on phones (1rem = 40 px; 756×366, 844×390) the text stays ≥ 9–15 px, the dialog fits the safe area and its body scrolls under a fixed header and footer.
- Tests: `test/ui/match-info.test.js` (the model, greying by state, badges, tips, the legend, banned by tier, real 标准 / 绝境 matches, `draftInfoStatus`, briefing / draft / drawer reading the one module); opt-in `test/ui/match-info.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`: solo 标准 on desktop — dialog = briefing, the three ways to close, a confirm afterwards, the in-game tab; co-op 绝境 with a desktop and a phone — the live countdown, a turn change closing it, 844×390 / 756×366 layout and scrolling, the draft's end unmounting it).

### 21.29 Deliberate deviation: six 重装 skills cast with an enemy in range (GitHub issue #4, PR #12) — `tools/build-data.mjs TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS / resolveTrigger / validateAll`, data/chess.json `skills[].trigger`, `kits/tier1.js` (深巡, 雷蛇), `kits/tier2.js` (灰毫), `kits/tier5.js` (号角)

- **Reported**: public issue #4 — 深巡 (哨戒铁卫, range 2-2) never cast her default S2 行动能力剥夺 until something hit her, in practice until she blocked; PR #12 (merged on GitHub) gave 深巡 S2 and 雷蛇 S2 `trigger: 'DEFAULT'` in their kits. Many players asked the same of other defenders; the owner (2026-10-03): "反馈的人太多了".
- **Official, unchanged**: the 下半 (act2autochess, 2026-03) `skillTriggerDataList` row `TANK | | | 0 | TAKE_DAMAGE` and PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助's 重装 strategy "不受技能范围影响，受到伤害时释放技能" (下半, 2026/3/14) — a class row, covering every MANUAL 重装 skill whatever its index (§20.2); 上半 (act1autochess, 2025-11) had no TANK row, so 重装 skills used the basic strategy then (research 03 §1.4 keeps the history). PR #12's reasons — the row as "skill 1 only" and "offensive skills activate when an enemy is in their skill range" — rested on a community summary (arknights.wiki.gg), not on the CN client or PRTS; that quote is removed from research 03 (promised on PR #12).
- **Decision** (the owner, 2026-10-03): a deliberate, documented deviation — six offensive 重装 skills take the basic strategy (DEFAULT: SP ready, about to attack, an enemy inside the initial range or one it blocks): **深巡 S2 行动能力剥夺, 雷蛇 S2 反击电弧, 号角 S2 暴风号令 and S3 终极防线, 灰毫 S1 攻击力强化·γ型 and S2 专注轰击**. Every other MANUAL 重装 skill keeps TAKE_DAMAGE — 深巡 S1 侵袭破坏应对 included.
- **Where**: in the data, so the server, the browser battle and the bots read one rule. `TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS` is keyed by the normal chess id and the skill id (the elite record follows through its `baseId`), never by the skill id alone: 灰毫 S1 is the generic `skcom_atk_up[3]`, which 惊蛰, 幽灵鲨, 耶拉, 莫斯提马 and 莱恩哈特 carry too. `resolveTrigger` applies it last; the 12 records read `trigger { rule: 'DEFAULT', rawRule: 'TAKE_DAMAGE' }` — `rawRule` keeps the official row for traceability — and `validateAll` fails the build when an entry no longer meets a TAKE_DAMAGE row. PR #12's kit lines (`trigger: 'DEFAULT'` on 深巡 / 雷蛇 S2) stay, agreeing with the data (a test pins kit = data); their comments and the contributor's tests now give this section's reason.
- **Measured** (a real battle: 战场#01(下半), one 萨卡兹枯朽前锋 walking the lane from the gate, the operator SP-full on (9,5) facing it): 深巡 / 雷蛇 S2 cast 6.2 s after the spawn, when the enemy steps onto the 2-2 line, and 号角 S2 / S3 and 灰毫 S1 / S2 within 1.3 s (their 要塞 range reaches the gate) — each before anything has hit it (the enemy reaches the defender after ≈ 24 s, if at all); each of the other 46 MANUAL 重装 records (normal + elite) casts on the first hit it takes, at 23.8 s.
- **Players notice**: these six fire sooner and more often — from range, before anything reaches the defender; 号角 S3's 过载 HP drain therefore starts sooner; 灰毫 S2 (block 0) lets an enemy pass as soon as one comes into range, not after the first hit; on a fenced tile (§21.23) 深巡's S2 fires.
- One-line flip: remove the entry from `TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS` (for 深巡 / 雷蛇 also the kit line) and rebuild.
- Normative lines rewritten with it: §5.6 (`TAKE_DAMAGE`), §19.2 (灰毫 S2), §20.2 (players notice, the table), §21.20 (a settled row), §21.23 (深巡 on a fenced tile).
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback1-tank-triggers.test.js` (the data, kit = data, the real battle for the six and for every other MANUAL 重装 record), `test/data.test.js`, `kits_t1t2` / `kits_alt_t1` (PR #12's), `kits_alt_t2` (灰毫 S2), `kits_t5` / `kits_alt_t5` (号角), `test/docs-consistency.test.js`.
- Extended 2026-10-04: 余 S2 (SKILL_RANGE on its x-1, §22.10); 14 records deviate now.

### 21.30 干员战斗语音: an operator's official lines, in battle only (user request, 2026-10-03) — `tools/assets/plan.mjs` (`voiceSlots`), `tools/assets/audio.mjs` (`indexVoice` / `VOICE_SLOTS` / `VOICE_BATTLE_SLOTS` / `VOICE_PREP_SLOTS` / `VOICE_DIRS`), `tools/assets/cache.mjs`, `tools/fetch-assets.mjs --voice-lang / --voice-all`, `public/js/audio.js` (`VOICE_PRIORITY` / `VOICE_COOLDOWN_MS` / `VoiceGate` / `resultVoiceSlot` / `resultSpeaker` / `voice()`), `server/sim/ai.js performAttack 'engage'`, `battle/runner.js emit('result')`, `screens/game.js onResult`, `ui/detailPanel.js voice`, `ui/settings.js`, `ui/gameLogic.js sanitizeSettings`

User: 「给每个卫戍干员都加语音」, then, after the first build, 「整备阶段不需要干员语音，战斗开始后才需要语音，也就是只需要作战中语音」.

**Where the lines come from.** Every operator the mode can field already has official battle lines in the same voice dump the BGM and SFX come from — `voice_cn/{charId}/cn_nn.mp3` (JP/EN/KR: `voice/`, `voice_en/`, `voice_kr/`, the same file names, `tools/fetch-assets.mjs --voice-lang=jp|en|kr`). `charword_table.json`'s `placeType` says when each line is used, and `indexVoice()` turns it into the manifest's `audio.voice[charId]`: 行动出发 `start` (BATTLE_START), 行动开始 `faceEnemy` (BATTLE_FACE_ENEMY), 选中干员 `select`, 部署 `place`, 作战中1-4 `skill1..skill4` (BATTLE_SKILL_N), 编入队伍 `squad`, 任命队长 `squadFirst`, 结算 `resultFour/Three/Two/Lose` (完成高难行动 / 3星结束行动 / 非3星结束行动 / 行动失败) and 干员报到 `gacha`. A slot with several lines stays an array and the client draws one. Only the base word key is planned (`wordKey === charId`): the dump has no folder for a skin variant's word key (`char_x_ita`, `char_x_epoque#28`), so those lines 404. **Only the slots a battle can play are planned** (`audio.mjs VOICE_BATTLE_SLOTS`): 干员报到 / 编入队伍 / 任命队长 are lines of the official client's own 养成 / 编队 UI, nothing in this mode ever requests them (`test/docs-consistency.test.js` checks the client's `voice(...)` call sites), and downloading them cost 360 files (19.3 MB) on every run — `--voice-all` adds them back for the complete official set. Measured: 120 of the 121 in-mode charIds have a full set — the 17 预备干员 and the mode's own 盟约·辅助干员 have no battle voice at all, so they simply get no entry — 1680 lines of the 2040 upstream, in a manifest of 5694 files / 324.2 MB (files and `stats.voiceChars` in `data/assets.json`).

**Where each line plays — battle only.** Every line comes from a running battle's own event stream or from its settlement, so no hook needs a phase check and the 休整期 stays silent: the shop does not say 干员报到 (a merge-completing buy neither says 任命队长), a retreat does not say 编入队伍 and dragging a piece onto the board in prep says nothing. In battle: 开战 — the first operator deployed says 行动出发 and the others 部署 (the sim's `deploy` events; `setFieldUnits` resets the flag per field — in this mode every piece enters at once, which is why the gate below exists, and a knocked-out operator's redeploy says 部署 again); 接敌 — the sim's new `['engage', id]` (a unit's first attack that hits an enemy, official ENCOUNTER_ENEMY); 技能 — `skillN` from the unit's `skillIndex` (作战中1-4); 选中 — the detail panel opening on an operator **while a battle runs** (`DetailPanel voice` = the game screen's `combat`); 结算 — `resultVoiceSlot()`: 完美作战 ⇒ 3星结束行动 (绝境 / 终极 ⇒ 完成高难行动), 有漏怪 ⇒ 非3星结束行动, 一个没杀 ⇒ 行动失败, spoken by a drawn operator **of that battle's own field** (`resultSpeaker`, from the result's `perPlayer.unitsEnd`: the operators of that field, survivors first, a summon piece never), driven by the runner's new `emit('result', …)` — never the field on screen, so watching a teammate cannot put their operator's voice on the viewer's settlement line (review on #73).

**Why a channel, not SFX.** Voices are 1–4 s long and a battle deploys eight operators, then fires dozens of skills; playing them like SFX would flood the mix. `VoiceGate` copies the official scheduling data (`audio_data.json battleVoice.voiceTypeOptions`): one line at a time, a 1.2 s global gap, a per-unit per-slot cooldown (10 s for 作战中, 3 s for 接敌, 1.5 s for 选中) and takeover when a line's priority beats the playing one by a margin (the official 0.1 s `crossfade`) — the priorities are the official ones (BATTLE_START 100, ENCOUNTER_ENEMY 90, SKILL_ACTIVE 70, PLACE_CHAR 20, FOCUS_CHAR 10) and the settlement lines get 85, above 作战中 but below 接敌. Every deferred step of a line — its decode, its `onended`, the safety timer — carries its own token and returns without touching the gate once it is stale, so a line taken over while it was still loading can never free the channel its replacement just took (review on #73). The channel has its own gain and its own slider (设置 干员语音 / VOICE; `sanitizeSettings` adds `voice`, so a saved profile without it keeps the default and players can turn voices down without losing SFX). The buffer cache also gained a decoded-PCM budget (64 MB) beside its 180-entry cap: a voice decodes to ~0.4–1.3 MB, and 180 of them would be ~100 MB.

**[ASSUMED].** 接敌 as "the unit's first attack on an enemy" (the official trigger is the first enemy it encounters, not necessarily one it hits) and the settlement mapping (漏怪 ⇒ 非3星, 完美作战 in 绝境 / 终极 ⇒ 完成高难行动) are our readings. Lines follow the field on screen: watching a teammate's battle (前往查看) plays *their* operators' lines, while the settlement line fires for the player's own battle only (`e.own`, `battle/runner.js`) and is spoken by an operator of that battle's own field (`resultSpeaker`). 部署 and 选中干员 stay in the plan although the official groups them with the prep lines: a battle *does* play them (the first operator says 行动出发 and the others 部署, and the detail panel says 选中 while a battle runs), so only the three slots no call site ever asks for are dropped — `VOICE_PRIORITY` / `VOICE_COOLDOWN_MS` keep every level, so `--voice-all` restores 编入队伍 / 任命队长 / 干员报到 for the day the 休整期 speaks again.

- Tests: `test/ui/audio.test.js` (the priority table, the settlement slot and its speaker, the gate's global gap / per-unit cooldown / takeover, the stale-callback regression, and the battle events that drive the lines against a fake Web Audio), `test/ui/gameLogic.test.js` (the `voice` setting), `test/sim/battle.test.js` (the wire format: every emitted tuple is a known `EV` — the new `engage` included); the manifest's 1680 voice paths are covered by `test/assets.test.js`.

## 22. GitHub issues after 0.1.1 (v0.1.2)

Public GitHub issues filed after the 0.1.1 release (2026-10-03), each triaged against 0.1.1 first; the real ones were fixed for 0.1.2 in fix branches WA–WL, each independently reviewed before it was merged into `feedback2`. Where each is handled: #25 back-facing operators keep their attack pose when knocked out → §22.1; #51 突袭's idle redeploy → §22.2; #33 items 1–2 阿戈尔's devour and 乌尔比安 S3 → §22.3; #33 item 6 活性源石, with the 沼泽 and 深水区 terrain → §22.4; #42 emotes and guide pages without a local client, and the English title (#38) → §22.5; #32 item 4 钩索师 / 推击手 on 高台 → §22.6; #52 史尔特尔's 余烬 → §22.7; #43 an enemy's 隐匿 after a block, and the 深池逐火 ember window → §22.8; #32 item 7 荒芜拉普兰德 S3's drones → §22.9; #32 item 1 余 S2 → §22.10; #44 归溟幽灵鲨's 替身 → §22.11; #32 item 6 enemy splash against a 隐匿 operator → §22.12; #35 / #16 (PR #48) 要塞 against air units and 莫斯提马's slow icon → §22.13; #58 / #60 / #64 follow in §22.14–§22.16. Decisions of the owner, 2026-10-04: 荒芜拉普兰德's drones measure the distance to an enemy's position, a huge enemy's centre (§22.9); 余 S2 casts with an enemy on its x-1 — a deliberate deviation from the official TAKE_DAMAGE row, added to §21.29's six (§22.10); 乌尔比安 keeps the devour gains through his 【移动】 — the deviation stays (§22.3).

### 22.1 Back-facing operators play their fall (GitHub #25) — `render/units.js _wantsBack / _syncModel / die / revive / _acquireSpine / _dropActor / dieClipDur`, `render/spine.js SpineActor.die / dieClip / deployElapsed`

- **Seen** (public issue #25, "部分干员被击倒后在部署状态时仍有攻击动作"; the screenshot: 跃跃 seen from behind under its redeploy ring, still in its attack pose, while 拉普兰德 beside it lies down; the reporter also saw it on 拉普兰德, 异德 (缄默德克萨斯), 斯卡蒂 and 德克萨斯, "并非稳定出现" — all five have a Back skeleton without a Die clip). **Real** in 0.1.0 and 0.1.1, drawing only (the sim had the operator down; it never attacked): an operator deployed facing UP is drawn with its Back model (§9, research 07 §5.5), and 131 of the mode's 135 Back skeletons (data/assets.json) have no Die clip — only 薄绿, 玛恩纳, 松果 and 预备干员-重装 have one, while every operator's Front skeleton has one. Not every time: only operators facing UP.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 says a knocked-out operator stays on its tile as a "倒地干员" (§21.24); which model the official client lies it down with is in no source. The project's asset plan already had the rule — research 07 §5.4 "`die`: `Die` (if the Back model lacks it, swap to the Front model for the death animation)", docs/ASSETS.md Roles `die: null` — but the renderer never implemented it. [ASSUMED] the official client does the same (a Back skeleton without a Die clip cannot show a fall), and the four Back skeletons with a Die clip fall with it.
- **Cause**: `UnitView._wantsBack()` ignored whether the unit stands; `SpineActor.die()` found no clip, returned 0 and changed nothing, so the attack / skill / idle loop playing at the knock-out went on under the ring for the whole respawn timer.
- **Now**:
  - `_wantsBack()`: facing UP with a Back model, while the unit stands; a dead or knocked-out one keeps the Back model only when that skeleton has a Die clip (`dieClipDur` of its manifest entry), so the other 131 fall and lie with the Front model (unmirrored, as facing UP).
  - `die()` and `revive()` mark the view, and its next `update()` — after every event and the snapshot of that frame (render/app.js) — runs `_syncModel()`: the model the state wants is loaded when it is neither shown nor loading, and swapped in place like a direction change (no avatar diamond in between). A death and a redeploy handed out in the same frame load nothing: a 突袭 jump (retreat + redeploy in one tick), a 不屈 revive (from the death hook), a backlog delivered after a hidden tab.
  - The Front model replays the state: the Die clip where it would be (`dieT`), a knocked-down one holding its end; `die(true)` and a view built for a unit already down start on the held end (the 联防 entry `FORCED_EXIT`, a field entered late, the re-entry from the field meta after an overflowed hidden-tab backlog). Its first frame gets the knocked-down grey (`_dropActor` drops the cached tint; a swapped-in model used to keep the previous model's tint state). A view that gets no `down` entry (summons and devices) fades like one facing RIGHT — a forced-out operator facing UP lies down like a knocked-out one (§22.15): the Front model's Die clip times the fade (`_fallDur`).
  - The redeploy (a `deploy` event: timed, 不屈, 阿戈尔 — every `Battle._deploy` — or a revive seen only in the snapshot) brings the Back model back; a deploy clip the Front model is playing goes on on the Back model where it is (`SpineActor.deployElapsed`).
  - Safety net: until the Front model is there, or when it fails to load, the Back model holds still — `SpineActor.die()` on a skeleton without a Die clip stops whatever looped and holds the first frame of its idle clip (frozen when it has none either), and nothing restarts an attack on a dead actor. The 20 summon / enemy skeletons without a Die clip (e.g. 浊心斯卡蒂's 海嗣) now fade holding still instead of looping [ASSUMED look].
