- Tests: `test/sim/feedback2-enemy-attack-stand.test.js` (7 — `attackAnim` matches the manifest for every enemy; 隐形弩手 with an operator in range stands for its clip at each attack and walks between attacks; a 1 s attacker with a 2 s clip never walks while it has a target; an `attackMoves` enemy keeps walking while the same enemy without it stands; the fallback, and a stun ending the stand; on a 3 s WAIT checkpoint the wait ends at the same time with or without a target, and the enemy walks on once its current clip is over; DISAPPEAR / APPEAR legs proceed during the stands, and hiding ends the stand), `test/render/feedback2-enemy-attack-clip.test.js` (4, fake PIXI with the real manifest: wind-up, one clip per attack at its own speed, then Move; a quick attacker's clip at 2×, an attack seen late starts on its strike frame; an operator's loop unchanged; enemy views use a clip per attack, operator views do not); `test/sim/combat.test.js` keeps the 0.35 s fallback.

### 22.15 An operator forced off the field lies down and comes back there (#60) — `sim/Battle.js isDown / retreat` (with `_remove`, `_layBody`, `restTile`, `downOn`, `_checkRedeploys`, `snapshot` unchanged), `content/kits/tier5.js` (余烬)

- **Seen**: 「42突袭死后会消失」 (42 = 史尔特尔, a community nickname). **Real**: her talent 余烬 keeps her at 1 HP after a lethal blow and forces her out 8 s later (`battle.retreat`, reason `'retreat'`). `Battle.isDown` took only `'killed'` and `FORCED_EXIT`, so she left no body: no b.snap `down` entry, so the client played Die and faded her out, and no redeploy ring. `restTile` sent her home, so 70 s later she came back on her board tile, not where she fell. Measured on feedback2 6b634fe: on (10,3), out at 9 s, back at 79 s on (10,3), with nothing on the field in between; after a 突袭 jump to (12,8), out at 21 s, back at 90 s on (10,3), then she jumped again. The same path was used by 耀骑士临光 S2 (`tier6.js`), the 骑士戒律 + 竞技旗 combo (`items/battle.js`), 伊内丝 S3's first deployment (`tier4.js`) and a 商人 that cannot pay (`professions.js`, `tier3.js` 琳琅诗怀雅).
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §作战阶段 单位部署: "干员退场后，将返回隐藏的待部署区，并原地留下一个“倒地干员”以供查看信息，满足再部署条件时，移除场上的该倒地干员并自动部署至该位置。" This covers every 退场, not only a knock-out (the owner's decision, 2026-10-04: every operator that leaves the field leaves a 倒地干员 on that tile and is redeployed there). PRTS 史尔特尔 天赋 余烬: 「受到致命伤害时持续使生命值不低于1，8秒后强制退出战场」. Her page's 备注 "强制退出战场视为撤回干员" describes the regular game, where a withdrawal refunds part of the cost; 卫戍协议 has no withdrawal in battle.
- **Cause**: §21.20 / §21.24 [ASSUMED] "a withdrawn operator leaves no body": `isDown` listed two removal reasons.
- **Now**: `isDown` takes every removal of an operator that may come back, except the 突袭 retreat (`'raid'`, which redeploys at once on its landing tile): `'killed'`, `FORCED_EXIT` (联防), `'retreat'` (余烬, 耀骑士临光 S2, 骑士戒律 + 竞技旗, 伊内丝 S3) and `'merchant'`. The §21.24 rules then apply unchanged:
  - `_remove` lays the body where the operator stood (its own home when that tile is another board piece's home and its home is free).
  - b.snap `down` and the field meta list it, so the client draws the body and the ring. The client needed no change: it draws whatever `down` lists.
  - `_checkRedeploys` / `redeploy()` bring it back on that tile, and `downOn` / `isReservedTile` keep allies off it.
  - A forced exit is still no kill. Its death reason stays `'retreat'` / `'merchant'`: no `kill` hook, no 被击倒 effects, no 不屈 or 阿戈尔 redeploy, no knock-down count (`perPlayer.deaths`) and no knock-down sound.
  - 联防 is unchanged: any operator not alive at the end of its own combat already entered 联防 down (`carryState.down`, `FORCED_EXIT`).
  - After the fix, the same layouts: down on (10,3) — `down` [1, 79, 70, 0, 10, 3] — and back there at 79 s; after the jump, down on (12,8) and back on (12,8) at 90 s; 耀骑士临光 S2 lies on her tile with her ×1.25 redeploy ring.
- [ASSUMED]: `'merchant'` counts as a 退场 too (the PRTS sentence and the owner's "every operator that leaves the field"; the triage had left it out); the regular game's partial refund for a 强制退出 (视为撤回干员) is not modelled; the 突袭 retreat leaves no body, since it is redeployed on its landing tile in the same tick (should both tiles refuse it, it waits for its timer and comes back home).
- Normative lines rewritten with it: §5.4 DP / redeploy, §8.2 `down`, §21.20 ([ASSUMED] list), §21.24 (Now), §22.1 (the withdrawn-operator sentence); docs/SIM.md, docs/PLAYING.md.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback2-forced-exit-down.test.js` (4 — 史尔特尔 after 余烬: `down` on her tile, the field meta, no kill / knock-down count, back on that tile after 70 s; after a 突袭 jump: down on the landing tile, the tile reserved, back there with her home unchanged; 耀骑士临光 S2: down on her tile with the ×1.25 ring and back there; a real knock-out unchanged); `test/sim/downelem.test.js` (a forced exit is `down`; the 突袭 retreat, permanent removals, summons, devices and enemies never are) and `test/sim/engine-requests.test.js` (a retreated operator lies where it stood and comes back there) follow the rule.

### 22.16 干员调配 shows the stats, 攻击范围, 特性 and 天赋 the chosen skill / module fight with (#64) — `screens/loadout.js statsPreview / LoadoutStats`, `ui/detailPanel.js chessStatsBlock / chessTalents / traitText`, `css/screens/loadout.css .lo-sec--stats`

- **Seen** (GitHub #64): the 干员调配 detail had only 技能 and 模组 — no 生命上限 / 攻击 / 防御 / 法术抗性 / 攻击间隔 / 阻挡数 / 部署费用 / 再部署, no 攻击范围, no 特性 or 天赋 of the operator (headless 1920 × 1080: block titles `["技能 SKILL", "模组 MODULE"]`, 0 `.dstats`, 0 `.rgrid`). A module card lists only its own overrides (`ModuleInfo`: 属性 +80 …), so a player could not tell what a module or a skill choice does to the operator without leaving for the board and opening its card.
- **Official**: no rule to look up — the screen adds none. The rule it serves is unchanged (§16, "开始游戏前无法调整干员的等级，但可调整其所携带的技能和模组"); the numbers shown are the ones the sim fights with, from the same composition (`shared/loadoutRecord.js`, §16): an elite's stats = `statsBase` + the module's `attr` (不装备: `statsBase`), its 特性 = the module's `traitOverride` (不装备: `traitBase`), its talents = the base ones + the module's `talentChanges`, its range = `attackRangeGrid` (a selected "被动效果：攻击范围扩大" skill, a module's own grid, the 特性's permanent 攻击距离 — §21.16).
- **Cause**: `Detail` rendered the two choice sections only; the card's stats block was inline in `ChessDetail` and its 特性 helper `traitText` private, so there was nothing to reuse.
- **Now**: a **局内数值** section between 技能 and 模组 (next to both choices; the first `.lo-seg` stays the skill-level toggle). `statsPreview(base, golden, entries, level, getChess)` picks the variant — the 精锐 record when asked for and the chess has one, else the normal one — and resolves it with `chessLoadout(variant, entries, getChess).record` (`gameLogic.js`, the call the shop card and the detail card make); no stat arithmetic of its own. `LoadoutStats` then draws `chessStatsBlock` (the card's eight-stat grid and 攻击范围 mini-map — the card now calls the same function, `blocks.stats`, so the two cannot drift), a 特性 / 天赋 card in the module-info card style (`traitText` and `chessTalents`, the card's own rules: the 特性 line exists when the chess has one and follows the chosen module; named, not hidden talents) and a caption. A 普通 / 精锐 toggle has its own state (`statLevel`, default **精锐**: the module exists only there, so a module click changes the numbers at once; it does not move the skill descriptions' toggle); 精锐 is disabled for a chess without an elite record. The numbers are the record's — no live entry, so none of the card's 实时 / 开战时 tag or green / red; the caption says what they leave out: "数值含所选模组；不含技能发动、装备、盟约等局内加成" (in 普通: "普通干员没有模组，所选模组在「精锐」中生效；…"). Measured over the data: 信仰搅拌机 SPT-Y 3 → 4 tiles, 莫斯提马 SPC-X 9 → 10, 引星棘刺 S3 13 → 19 (both variants); 隐现 elite 1,421 / 508 with MAR-X, 1,341 / 486 on 不装备, 1,123 / 399 as the normal chess; 异客's other module rewrites 机理分析 (80 % → 70 %).
- **Now (css)**: the card's `.dstats` / `.dstat` / `.drange` / `.rgrid` (game-panels.css, loaded on every page) at this screen's reading sizes — every text size keeps a px floor (labels 9, values 12, range cells 7 px at the phones' 1 rem = 40 px); the stats grid takes `flex: 1 1 max(3.2rem, 190px)` and the range box wraps under it when the column is narrower than about 260 px. Headless Chrome (`test/ui/loadout.e2e.test.js`, screenshots `test/e2e/out/loadout-stats-*.png`): 1920 × 1080 the grid with the range box beside it; 844 × 390 (a 240 px detail column) the range box under the numbers, no sideways overflow, no label cut off; 667 × 375 (the detail slid over the roster) beside again. Portrait phones get the app's "rotate device" screen, so they are no target.
- [ASSUMED]: the section's place (the issue offers before 技能 or after 模组 — between them keeps both choices within a screen of the numbers on a 1080p display), 精锐 as the default variant, the caption wording, no difference colouring against 不装备, and that the 特质 (garrison) block is not repeated (the issue names 特性 and 天赋 only).
- Tests: `test/ui/loadout-stats.test.js` (9: the variant and the loadout resolution, the sim's range changes, the section against `ChessDetail` for 11 chess / loadout / level cases, the eight values, 特性 / 天赋 following the module, the toggle, the section's place, the css floors), `test/ui/feedback1e-range.test.js` (the source contract now points at `chessStatsBlock`), `test/ui/loadout.e2e.test.js` (+2 opt-in browser tests: desktop and 844 × 390 touch).
- **Review (Grok, 2026-10-04)**: the 特性 line fell back to the class trait on a record cloned for a non-default skill or module (e.g. 隐现 on S1 showed 优先攻击空中单位 instead of MAR-X's 攻击空中单位时攻击力提升至110%; 信仰搅拌机 SPT-Y showed the class trait instead of 攻击距离+1) — the in-match card shared the bug since 0.1.1. `traitText` now returns the record's own module line whenever there is one (a 不装备 record carries none: checked on all 133 elite records); test in `test/ui/loadout-stats.test.js`.

## 23. Community reports after 0.1.2 (v0.1.3)

Reports after the 0.1.2 release, fixed on `feedback3` in workstreams W3A–W3J. Each was checked against the official data and PRTS; anything with no source is marked [ASSUMED]. Where each is handled: 联防 carry (#82) → §23.1; 联防 devour of operators who enter down → §23.2; 不屈 on a 撤退 and a 替身 switch → §23.3; SP during stun → §23.4; 碎骨's grenade → §23.5; the enemy numbers left open in §22.12 → §23.6; 圣聆初雪 S2 and the blue gate → §23.7; 琳琅诗怀雅 S1 heals without an attack → §23.8; 芬's 信标 (#86) → §23.9; 远见 to 0 → §23.10; ready with funds left → §23.11; the host kicks a human → §23.12; 折桠's skill idle → §23.13; the ammo bar → §23.14; unit-sound mix → §23.15; a stuck model load → §23.16; shot heights → §23.17; the boss leader in prep → §23.18; spectator seats → §23.19; 阿戈尔 revives by position → §23.20; a 傀儡师 switch ends at full HP → §23.21; 联防 fights with the layers the combat reached → §23.22; deployment by column → §23.23; nest spawners and 节日爵士乐手 → §23.24; a split child's bounty → §23.25; a 隐匿 blocker and areas (#97) → §23.26; stacked 寒冷 → §23.27; 「地面干员」 → §23.28; 叙拉古 on every normal hit (#79) → §23.29; mode-off bonds on the strip → §23.30; an item gained as prep ends → §23.31; 乌尔比安 S3 as a free redeploy → §23.32; 波登可's spore radius → §23.33; 琳琅诗怀雅 S3 shoots at 10 coins → §23.34; 高台 only for elite 歌蕾蒂娅 with HOK-Y → §23.35; watching a board in prep (#87) → §23.36; 折射 while silenced → §23.37; a toast when a bond grants a chess → §23.38; an emote manifest timeout (#99) → §23.39.

Decisions of the owner, 2026-10-04: a 联防 devour counts members who enter already down (§23.2); 不屈 rolls on a 撤退 and on a 替身 switch (§23.3); the host may kick a human before the match (§23.12); a co-op room has two spectator seats (§23.19); the boss leader is shown in prep, on the red tiles it can be hit from (§23.18); 远见 can bring a price to 0 (§23.10); 琳琅诗怀雅 S1 heals an injured neighbour without attacking (§23.8); an item that arrives as 休整期结束 merges at the next prep (§23.31); a splitting enemy's bounty stays on the main body (§23.25); only elite 歌蕾蒂娅 carrying HOK-Y may use a 高台, and the bot places her on one that covers the road (§23.35); 琳琅诗怀雅 S3 shoots when the purse reaches 10 and a ground enemy is in the skill attack range (§23.34).

### 23.1 联防 carries the HP ratio and the SP only — no running skill (community report #34, GitHub #82) — `match/unite.js uniteBattleOpts`, `sim/skills.js` (`reset`, `spTotal`, `setSpTotal`), `sim/Battle.js` (`_buildResult` unitsEnd, `_deploy`, `_startDeploying`), `match/fields.js` (client-reported unitsEnd), `match/PlayerState.js battleInput`

- **Seen** (community report #34 「圣聆初雪2技能联防被吞技能」; GitHub #82): every skill that was running when a helper's own combat ended was already on at the 联防 start, with an empty bar — 圣聆初雪's S2 (a toggle: SP 0 once cast) looked "eaten" although it ran.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §联防阶段 "部署完成后，将对应单位的生命比例、技力修改至与上一阶段结束时相同（召唤物仅修改技力，上一阶段为退场状态的干员强制退场）"; the official setup message carries per unit only `charBattleStatusList: [{ instId, hp, tech }]` (research 09 §3 `HelpBattleInfo`). The 技力 of a skill that is running: PRTS 技能 "触发技能后，触发技能的单位将消耗相应的技力，随后在技能持续期间进入阻回" — spent at the activation, nothing recovered while it runs. PRTS 技能 "可充能X次…当前技力上限等于该技能技力需求的X倍": a charge skill's 技力 includes its stored charges.
- **Cause**: `unite.js` copied `unitsEnd.skillActive` into `carryState`, and `Skill.reset` re-activated a carried timed skill for free (a fresh duration / ammo, no charge spent). Two more gaps in the carried SP: `unitsEnd.sp` was the SP towards the next charge only, so stored charges were lost (a full 2-charge skill came back with one); summons carried nothing (DESIGN §19.3 had left 召唤物仅修改技力 out).
- **Now**: `carryState` is `{ hpPct, sp }` for an operator, `{ sp }` for a board summon piece, `{ down: true }` as before. `unitsEnd.sp` is `skill.spTotal` (stored charges × cost + the SP towards the next charge); `reset` rebuilds the charges from it. A skill running at the end enters 联防 switched off with the SP it had left — 0 for a one-charge skill — and recharges from there (圣聆初雪 S2: off, SP 0, 60 s to recast — 52 elite; S3 likewise; her S1 with one charge and 5 SP stored comes back with exactly that). `unitsEnd` now also lists the board's summon pieces (a board uid); the server accepts them from a client-reported result (`fields.js`: `own.all`), `unite.js` gives a summon on the field at the end its SP, `battleInput` hands it over, and `_deploy` applies a carried HP ratio to operators only. A board piece that content deploys during the start deployment (a tactician's 流形 / 狼群 comes with its owner's deploy, `initial: false`) takes its carry on that first deployment (`_startDeploying`). The carried SP is set "部署完成后", as PRTS says: `_deploy` applies it in `reset` and sets it again after the `deploy` hook (`skill.setSpTotal`: SP and charges rebuilt, nothing fired, a passive or an already running timed skill left alone), so a deploy-time SP gift does not come on top — a bar that ended at 30 / 40 used to come back ready with 独行 ("初始技力+15") or 黄沙罗盘 ("初始技力+30"); later redeploys in the same battle keep those gifts (initial SP 10 + 15 / + 30). In practice only 缪尔赛思's 流形 (cost 100, initial SP 95) has an SP skill among the placeable summons: it now enters with its end SP. `unitsEnd.skillActive` is still reported, never carried.
- [ASSUMED]: a summon off the field at the end of the own combat enters fresh (its 技力 at the end does not exist). Not changed: the HP ratio is still applied at the deployment (PRTS sets it "部署完成后" too; no deploy-time effect is known to change it).
- **§22.4 needs a word changed**: its "联防 carries no effects (HP, SP and a running skill only)" now reads "(the HP ratio and the SP only)".
- Docs: DESIGN §5.1 `carryState` / unitsEnd comments (pinned in test/docs-consistency.test.js), §19.3's summon sentence, docs/SIM.md §1.1 and the result shape, docs/META.md (联防), docs/PLAYING.md (联防).
- Tests: `test/match/feedback3-unitecarry.test.js` (unite.js carry: HP + SP for an operator, never `skillActive`, `{ sp }` for a summon on the field, nothing for one off it, `down` as before; `battleInput` hands a summon its carry; a real own combat whose 30 s skill still runs at the end → unitsEnd sp 0, skillActive true → the 联防 battle: skill off, no activation, SP from 0, no skill ATK, a 2-charge skill's stored charges back; 缪尔赛思's 流形 with `{ sp: 40, hpPct: 0.3 }`: SP 40 instead of 95, HP full; 独行 and 黄沙罗盘 with a carried 0 / 30 of 40: the bar comes back as it ended, not ready — 15 / 30 more and ready before the fix —, a later redeploy 25 / 40), `test/sim/fidelity.test.js` and `test/sim/skills.test.js` (rewritten: a carried `skillActive` starts nothing), `test/match/{combat,clientCombat,unitedown}.test.js` (the carry shape).

### 23.2 联防: 阿戈尔's devour counts the operators down since their own combat (community report #3, GitHub #33 item 3) — `content/bonds/core.js devour`

- **Seen** (community report #3): "阿戈尔在进协防的时候，即使已经处于撤退状态的干员也应该会在协防阶段时重新参与进阿戈尔的吞噬攻击力计算，不是像现在这样数值被重置" — a member's devour gains vanished in 联防 when an operator of its chain had been knocked out at the end of the own combat.
- **Official / decision**: no text gives the order of the 联防 forced exit and the battle-start devour (§19.3, §22.3: [ASSUMED] forced exit first). **Per players' reports (two players), owner's decision 2026-10-04**: in 联防 the operators that were down at the end of their own phase still take part in the battle-start devour — marking order, the unit in front, base ATK / block gains — and are then forced out.
- **Cause**: `Battle.start` forces those operators out right before `battleStart`; `devour()` took only units on the field, so the chain broke at the down operator's tile: 乌尔比安 → 幽灵鲨 (down) → 歌蕾蒂娅 → 隐现 gave 乌尔比安 nothing (an empty tile in front of him).
- **Now** (the smallest change; the forced exit keeps its place, so no other `battleStart` handler — devices, 征召's row check, 炎, the add-on refresh, kits, garrisons, bands — sees anything new): `devour()` treats an operator forced out at this start (`carryState.down`, removal reason `FORCED_EXIT`, lying on its tile) as standing for the whole devour but what would land on it. It marks in its turn (left first, then top), it is found as "the unit in front" (`battle.downOn`; the chain goes on through it when it is a member), its base ATK / block count for its marker, and its own marks resolve: their 物理流失 lands on its targets, a kill is credited to it as usual, a member it knocks out spends a 5-tier revive like any first knock-out (whose pending marks are then cancelled). Only the marks on it resolve nothing (`knocked`: it is off the field) — it is never knocked out again, never revived, never devoured (no layers, no `deaths`). Its own marking gains are a persistent buff, as for every marker since 0.1.0: it has them when it redeploys. Measured (real data): the chain above (3 members) gives 乌尔比安 +1569 ATK (幽灵鲨 530 + 歌蕾蒂娅 640 + 隐现 399) and +5 block — exactly the gains with 幽灵鲨 standing; 幽灵鲨 lies forced out with no knock-out, no revive, no 物理流失 on her; 歌蕾蒂娅 and 隐现 fall to 乌尔比安's marks as with her standing (her own marks find them knocked out already). With 幽灵鲨 (down) at the head of 幽灵鲨 → 歌蕾蒂娅 → 隐现, her two marks knock both out, exactly as standing (3 members: same knock-outs; 5 members: 歌蕾蒂娅's revive is spent and she stands again at full HP).
- [ASSUMED]: nothing lands on a down operator (it is forced out right after; a 物理流失 on it could only knock it out a second time or spend a revive), so — unlike a standing one that an earlier mark knocks out — a down marker always resolves its own marks. **Review fix (Grok, round 4)**: the first version skipped the down marker's own marks too, so the unit in front of it took no 物理流失 and a 5-tier revive stayed unspent while it still passed its base ATK / block on.
- **§22.3 needs a word changed**: its "The order of the 联防 forced exit and the battle-start devour stays [ASSUMED] (§19.3: forced exit first); no source gives it" now points here. §19.3's [ASSUMED] sentence names the exception (edited in place).
- Tests: `test/content/feedback3-egir-down.test.js` (the chain 乌尔比安 → 幽灵鲨 (down) → 歌蕾蒂娅 → 隐现 in a 联防 battle: 乌尔比安's devour buff equals the chain with 幽灵鲨 standing; she is forced out — no `'killed'` death, no redeploy, no 物理流失 on her, one knock-out fewer in `deaths`; her marking gains kept; 幽灵鲨 (down) at the head of the chain with 3 and with 5 members: the resolved marks, kills, knock-outs and — at 5 — 歌蕾蒂娅's spent revive equal the chain with her standing, and she stays forced out); `test/content/bonds_core.test.js` unchanged and passing.

### 23.3 不屈 rolls on a 撤退 and on a 傀儡师's 替身 switch (owner's decision 2026-10-04) — `content/bonds/addon/battle.js` (不屈), `sim/professions.js installDollkeeper` (hook `dollSwap`)

- **Seen**: the triage of community report #24 (GitHub #33): 不屈 rolled on knock-outs only; a ground operator withdrawn by its own effect stayed down even at 210 layers (p = 1).
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约 下半/PRTS盟约记录, 不屈 — its 修正 (原因 6) replaces the text's "被击倒" by "被击倒、撤退、切换<替身>与<本体>": "地面干员…时，有(18+0.4×层数)%概率立刻重新部署"; "※“立刻重新部署”的实现方式为：令受益者下次部署的再部署时间和费用归零"; "※持有“复活”效果时，即使触发了不屈的“重新部署”效果，也还是会消耗一次“复活”次数". The tier-2 line "<在场3名不同【不屈】干员>地面干员被击倒时使场上所有干员技力+5" was not corrected. research 02 §3.19 already had the corrected trigger. **Owner's decision 2026-10-04**: follow PRTS.
- **Cause**: the `death` hook returned unless the reason was `'killed'`; nothing reported a 替身 switch (the garrisons poll `unit.trait.doll`).
- **Now**: a knock-out (`'killed'`) or a 撤退 rolls: the reasons `'retreat'` (an operator withdrawn by its own effect — 史尔特尔's 余烬, 耀骑士临光 S2, 伊内丝 S3, the 骑士戒律 combo) and `'merchant'` (行商 that cannot pay). A hit redeploys it at once, free, where it lies (the engine rest tile, §22.15) — once per unit and instant, as before. No roll on the 突袭 retreat (`'raid'`: it redeploys at once and free on its landing tile anyway) or on the 联防 forced exit (`FORCED_EXIT`: the battle's setup, not a 撤退 — §19.3; the operator keeps its full timer). A 傀儡师 switch rolls too — to the 替身 and back (new hook `dollSwap { unit, form }`, fired when a switch starts, not when she is knocked out as the 替身): she stays on the field, so a hit only zeroes her **next** deployment (`unit.mem.indomFreeDeploy`): the next time she leaves the field (knocked out as the 替身, withdrawn) she is back at once and free, whatever that exit's own roll gives; any deployment spends it (this redeploy, the 阿戈尔 5-tier revive, a 突袭 jump). Tier 2's +5 SP stays a knock-out effect. The 不屈 roll still needs a ground operator (`unit.ground`, §22.6) and the player's 不屈 bond; membership is not needed (unchanged).
- **Players notice**: with 不屈 lit, 耀骑士临光 coming back from her S2 withdrawal, 史尔特尔 after her 余烬, a 行商 short of DP and 伊内丝 after placing her 影哨 may redeploy at once (at 205+ layers always — 耀骑士临光 then re-runs S2 every 22 s, a 行商 that cannot pay keeps coming back); 归溟幽灵鲨 / 风丸 roll on each switch and a hit brings them straight back after their next knock-out.
- [ASSUMED]: no roll on the 突袭 retreat (it would change nothing — its redeploy is already immediate and free); one zeroed deployment at a time (a second hit while one is banked adds nothing), spent by the next deployment of any kind; the switch roll is silent (no fx — nothing is redeployed then). Open: the 阿戈尔 5-tier revive's implementation note ("受益者因移动之外的原因退场时…归零") may cover retreats too; it still fires on first knock-outs only.
- Docs: docs/SIM.md §1 (the 退场 paragraph) and the hook table (`dollSwap`).
- Tests: `test/content/feedback3-indom-exits.test.js` (p = 1: a `'retreat'` and a `'merchant'` withdrawal redeploy at once, free, on the tile; tier 2's +5 SP comes with a knock-out, not with a retreat; the 联防 forced exit and the 突袭 retreat roll nothing; p = 0.18: a missed roll leaves the operator down on its tile; 归溟幽灵鲨: the switch to the 替身 banks a free deployment — the knock-out of the 替身 brings her back at once and free, the bank spent; the switch back rolls on its own; with a bank, a knock-out brings any ground operator back at once whatever the roll), `test/content/bonds_addon.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback2-doll.test.js`, `test/sim/professions.test.js` unchanged and passing.

### 23.4 A stunned operator keeps its natural SP recovery (community report #18, 洛洛 S2) — `sim/skills.js tick`

- **Seen** (community report #18): "洛洛二技能结束眩晕好像不会自然恢复技力，而且好像持续时间也不太对，洛洛二技能是有过载的".
- **Official**: PRTS 技能 — natural recovery is a 1 s SP cooldown slot, and "在阻回状态或技力条已满时，保留剩余冷却时间，计时暂停": only 阻回 (SP_RECOVER_STOPPED, "停止技力自然回复…※通常情况下，处于技能期间的单位皆持有阻回") or a full bar pause it. PRTS 异常效果 STUNNED "状态机尝试切换至STUN（无法攻击、释放技能、阻挡敌人类单位；敌人类单位无法移动）" and gamedata_const ba.stun "无法移动、阻挡、攻击及使用技能" name no SP effect; ba.frozen likewise. So a stun does not stop natural recovery. 洛洛 S2 自负此轭 (skill_table `skchr_rockr_2`; the chess status skillLevel 4 / elite 7): duration 40, SP cost 45 / 40, initial SP 15 / 20, ATK +40 % / +50 %, ASPD +45 / +60, funnel cap ×1.6 / ×1.7; ba.overdrive "技能进行到一半时触发额外效果"; "技能结束后晕眩过载持续时间的等长时间".
- **Duration — correct, unchanged**: the overload starts half-way (20 s) and the stun lasts as long as the overload ran (20 s after a full skill; an earlier end stuns for the part already overloaded — the triage measured 5.03 s for a stop 5 s past half-way, 0 before it); the data match skill_table at both levels.
- **Cause**: `SkillRuntime.tick` returned when the unit could not act (`!canAct`: stunned, frozen, levitated) before the natural recovery, so a stunned operator's time SP stood still — written as a rule in skills.js and docs/SIM.md ("no SP gain … while stunned").
- **Now**: the time SP recovers first (unless 阻回 `noSp` or a running timed skill, both unchanged), then a unit that cannot act skips its trigger checks and casts. A general rule — every stunned / frozen / levitated operator with time SP. 洛洛 after a full S2: +20 SP during the 20 s stun (was 0), recast 45 s after the skill end (elite 40) instead of 65 (60). Others that change: self-inflicted — 幽灵鲨 S2 肉斩骨断 (10 s stun after the skill: +10 SP; recast 45 / 43 s after the end instead of 55 / 53), 圣聆初雪's 圣山的祝福 (4 s self-freeze after the saved knock-out: +4 SP unless a skill runs — S2 holds 阻回 anyway); 雷蛇 S2 反击电弧 is hurt-SP (受击回复, which already counted hits while stunned) — no change. Enemy stuns / freezes on operators: 粉碎攻坚手 / 组长 and 码头水手 / 水手长 (every Nth attack), 流泪小子 (鸭爵, 3.5 s), “巨大的丑东西” (its knock-out blast), 杰斯顿·威廉姆斯, 重弩突袭者 (直击), 乌顶巨角卢鲁 (角力对决) and the leaders' stuns (胄's 剑 / 锤 blasts, tentacles 5 s), the 神经损伤 burst on operators (10 s), cold-on-cold freezes (3 s) and 浮空 — the stunned operator's SP keeps filling, its casts wait until it can act. Attack SP (no attacks while stunned) and hurt SP are unchanged; units that are not deployed recover nothing.
- [ASSUMED]: none (the rule is PRTS's; frozen / levitated follow the same SP-cooldown text).
- Docs: skills.js header, docs/SIM.md §7.1.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback3-stunsp.test.js` (洛洛 S2's data against skill_table for both pieces; a full S2: a 20 s stun, +10 SP after 10 s of it, 20.5 after the stun, recast 45 s after the skill end; frozen: +1/s; 阻回: nothing; an operator that fills its bar while stunned casts only after the stun), `test/sim/combat.test.js` (the stunned operator neither attacks nor casts, SP +1.9 over 1.9 s — the old assertion was "no SP").

### 23.5 碎骨's grenade: 26 % ATK on the target and its 8 surrounding tiles, DEF −50 % for 5 s (community report #14) — `content/enemies.js` (`splashAttack`, `noAirTargets`, `kitLeaderMisc` enemy_1500_skulsr, `SKULSR_GRENADE_SCALE` / `SKULSR_DEFDOWN_DUR`)

- **Seen** (community report #14): 「敌人碎骨的伤害有人反馈过高，复核有没有异常」. **Real**: its combat stats are right (level 0 in data/enemies.json = PRTS: HP 10500, ATK 1000, DEF 150, RES 30, range 2.6, interval 3.0; the 50 % ATK-up below half HP is in the blackboard and was already modelled) — the unblocked grenade was not. §22.12 had listed it under "Found, not changed".
- **Official**: PRTS 碎骨 天赋 "未被阻挡时发射榴弹对目标及其周围八格的我方单位造成相当于攻击力26%的物理伤害，并令其在5秒内防御力下降50%（直接乘算）", "※榴弹对主目标造成物理普通伤害，对溅射目标造成物理溅射伤害，可溅射飞行单位", and "不会攻击飞行单位". The enemy_database blackboard has only `atkup.atk` 0.5, `atkup.hp_ratio` 0.5 and `defdown.def` −0.5: the 26 %, the 5 s and the 3×3 are PRTS's numbers (no table carries them); the −50 % is the blackboard's.
- **Cause**: the kit let the engine deal a full attack on the target, then hit everything within radius 1 (the 4 orthogonal neighbours, no diagonal) for another 100 % ATK, with a 3 s DEF-down [ASSUMED "短时间"].
- **Now**: a shared helper `splashAttack` fixes the attack's mode when it starts (`before`: unblocked ⇒ grenade). The target takes the engine's normal attack at 26 % ATK (`profile.atkScale`, so on-hit effects still apply); when it lands, every unit on the target's tile and the 8 around it (`areaAlliesInTiles` 'box' 1, 格子判定) takes 26 % ATK physical as a splash (tag `splash`); everyone hit gets DEF −50 % for 5 s. A blocked attack is its plain melee hit (100 %, no splash, no DEF-down). The splash keeps 0.1.2's area rule (§22.12): a 隐匿 operator next to the target that does not block 碎骨 is spared, a 迷彩 one is not; a flying ally (the 炎佑 dragon) is splashed ("可溅射飞行单位") but never targeted (`noAirTargets`); an airborne 起飞 operator stays out (对地规避, as every ground enemy's area). fx `explode` kind `grenade` (box) on each landing.
- [ASSUMED]: the splash goes off on the grenade's landing even when the target dodges it (the main hit's `hit` event, before its dodge); a target that died or turned invulnerable mid-flight gives no splash.
- **Measured** (real bot rounds — coop NORMAL, 4 bots, matches seed 3 and 7, the first bot's own field at rounds 4 / 6 / 8, one 碎骨 bounty added as `bountySpawns` would, 3 sim seeds each, 18 runs; before = v0.1.2 code): leaks 73 → 5, 碎骨 itself leaked 9 → 0 times (killed 9 → 18), operator knock-outs 81 → 55, damage taken by operators 383 k → 337 k.
- Tests: `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (碎骨: 26 % on the target as its attack, 26 % splash on a diagonal neighbour, nothing two tiles away, DEF-down 5 s / 0.5 on both; blocked: one 100 % hit, no splash, no DEF-down; ATK up below half), `test/sim/feedback2-stealth-splash.test.js` (the 隐匿 / 迷彩 / 伪装服 neighbours, unchanged).

### 23.6 Enemy numbers from the 0.1.2 review checked against PRTS and this mode's data (§22.12 "Found, not changed") — `content/enemies.js` (`splashAttack`, `noAirTargets`, `pathKeysAhead`, `kitWolfLord`, `kitUglyThing`, `kitTeapot`, `kitInvisShield`, `kitCrossbow`, `kitLeaderMisc` “庞贝” / “复仇者” / “墓碑”, 集团军重型火炮)

- **Seen**: §22.12 listed enemy numbers that differ from their PRTS pages; each was checked again on PRTS (the enemy pages, fetched 2026-10-04) and against this mode's enemy data (data/enemies.json = enemy_database level 0, the level every act2autochess enemy uses — activity_table act2autochess `enemyKey … level 0`; the act2autochess level files carry none of these enemies). Only what both confirm, or what PRTS states where the data has no number, changed.
- **Official → now** (until 0.1.3 in brackets):
  - **扎罗，“狼之主”** — 天赋 "【远古威慑】：自身1.5半径范围内的我方单位攻击速度-50(指定状况下生效)", "重生期间【远古威慑】生效", second form "【远古威慑】生效 … 进行远程攻击，普通攻击为2连击，攻击范围半径1.25": aura radius 1.5, ASPD −50, during the 40 s 重生 and in the second form; second-form attack radius 1.25 [2.5 / −30 only while reborning / 2.5, all ASSUMED]. 【溶血骇惧】 (技能 "逐渐流失生命（从0/秒开始线性递增，在40秒后达到最大流失速度30%最大生命值/秒），持续时间无限 … 场上存在被此技能影响的单位时狼之主获得静默，每结束1个技能效果狼之主回复5SP，场上技能效果全部结束时狼之主获得7s静默 … 累计损失20%生命值后解除") = the FearCage blackboard: the loss rate rises to `hp_ratio` 30 % max HP/s over `duration_bleed` 40 s and stays there, no end; cured once 扎罗's HP ratio is `hp_ratio_offset` 20 % below its ratio at the cast (an offset from that ratio, so healing 扎罗 delays the cure); an effect whose 扎罗 left the field without a 重生 (a leak, a removal) ends at its next tick with no loss [ASSUMED — no official text; the 重生's onKo cure covers its knock-out]; its SP (the spCost 55 s cooldown) stands still while a unit is caught, each ended effect (cure, knock-out) gives back `sp` 5, and the last one starts `duration_wait` 7 s more of 静默 [5 % peak ASSUMED, 40 s buff, `hp_ratio` 30 % read as the cure, a recast every 55 s whatever was caught].
  - **“复仇者”** — 冲锋 "仅自身未被阻挡且存在符合条件的可选目标时可触发：选择3.0半径内位于自身下个检查点前的后续路径上(包含自身当前所在地块)的距离自身最近的我方单位…": a target it may select (`targetsNear`: no 隐匿 / 迷彩 / 起飞 / untargetable operator) within 3.0 on its own path to its next checkpoint (`pathKeysAhead`: the tiles its current move leg crosses, its own tile included) [any unit within the blackboard's `range_radius` 1.5, 隐匿 / 迷彩 ones included, off its path too]. The blackboard keeps 1.5; PRTS's 3.0 is used. It walks to that unit's tile along that path anyway, so PRTS's re-aimed checkpoint is not a separate move.
  - **“庞贝”** — "被阻挡时，每10秒（受晕眩/无法行动/沉睡/冻结/浮空影响时暂停计时，解除阻挡时重置计时）对<固定半径>半径1.4范围内的所有我方单位造成1000预计算的无途径法术溅射伤害（不可对空）": radius 1.4; the clock runs only while it is blocked and can act and restarts at every new block; flying allies skipped [radius 1, a fixed 10 s clock from its spawn].
  - **“巨大的丑东西”** — "未被阻挡时，可对半径2.5范围内的1名非飞行的我方单位进行远程攻击，对目标及其周围8格内的所有我方单位造成攻击力100%的物理伤害（※…可溅射飞行单位）", "被阻挡时进行近战攻击，造成攻击力300%的物理伤害", "重生开始的2.17s后进行自爆，对半径3.0范围内所有我方单位造成攻击力150%的物理伤害和16s晕眩": the data's attack radius is 0, so it never attacked unblocked — now a 2.5-radius ranged attack on a ground unit with the 3×3 splash (`splashAttack`), the blocked hit at `mcmstr_rage_attack.atk_scale`; the self-destruct 2.17 s into the 重生 (a telegraph first), radius 3.0 [no ranged attack; the blast at once, radius 2.5 ASSUMED].
  - **“墓碑”** — "自身造成的远程途径伤害的攻击倍率降低至40%" (the blackboard's `atkdown.atk_scale` 0.4, unused until now), "未被阻挡时会进行远程攻击，对目标及其周围八格内的所有我方单位造成物理伤害，不会攻击飞行单位": unblocked, 40 % ATK on the target and its 3×3; blocked, its melee hit at 100 % [one target at 100 % either way].
  - **烹泉 / 沏虹** — "普通攻击对目标对及目标周围半径1.0范围内的所有我方单位造成法术普通伤害（无视迷彩，不可对空）": every attack also hits the units within 1.0 of its target; "死亡爆炸（爆炸半径1.25，造成攻击力100%法术溅射伤害并施加15s【烹泉减益】…不可对空）", 【烹泉减益】 "攻击速度-40（沏虹 −50），…可叠加，每层持续时间和效果独立计算": the blast reaches 1.25 and gives each unit hit one 15 s layer of its own [no attack splash; the blast took the attack radius 2 and left a 15 s steam zone re-applying the cut to whoever stood in it, ASSUMED]. The attack splash, a 法术普通伤害, can be dodged like the attack (`splashAttack` `dodge`); the blast (法术溅射伤害) and every other enemy splash here cannot.
  - **集团军重型火炮** — "不会攻击飞行单位", "普通攻击向目标发射一枚炮弹，对目标半径1.0范围内的所有我方单位造成攻击力100%的物理伤害（此弹道会强制击中主目标，碰撞无视迷彩，不可对空）", 【燃烧区域】 "<1.5倍可变半径>碰撞半径1.5": the shell splashes 1.0 around its target; the zone's radius is `projectile_range` × 1.5 and it skips a flying ally (`dmgZone` `noAir`: the 炎佑 dragon) [no splash, zone radius 1, the dragon burned].
  - **清明 / 堂皇** — 辉光照耀 "使【范围隐匿】生效3s" = the talent's `InvisibleShield.duration` 3 (both) [清明 took its skill blackboard's `duration` 5].
  - **重弩突袭者** — 直击 "蓄力1.4s后向目标方向发射1支弩箭，对击中的首个目标造成攻击力100%的法术伤害与5s晕眩 ※技能持续2.5s", 天赋 "隐匿（被阻挡，主动攻击期间均可解除）": revealed for the skill's 2.5 s, the bolt 1.4 s in at the nearest unit still in line in the aimed direction [revealed for the blackboard's `duration` 2 s, the bolt at once].
  - Every area added or changed picks its victims with `areaAllies` / `areaAlliesInTiles` (§22.12): no 隐匿 operator that does not block the enemy, no airborne 起飞 one, 迷彩 not checked; "不可对空" sites also skip a flying ally (the 炎佑 dragon), "可溅射飞行单位" ones take it.
- **Cause**: the kits predate these PRTS readings (the numbers were [ASSUMED] or read off the wrong blackboard key); 巨大的丑东西's and 墓碑's ranged attacks and the splash of 烹泉 / the 火炮 were never written.
- [ASSUMED]: a 溶血骇惧 ends when its 扎罗 leaks or is removed; 重弩突袭者 stands still and cannot attack for the 2.5 s, and a stun / silence or its death before the release cancels the bolt; 扎罗's SP is +1/s (its cast every spCost s, as before); a splash goes off on landing even if the target dodges (the main hit's `hit` event).
- **Found, not changed**: 【烹泉减益】 "可被抵抗" (a plain buff here, no 抵抗); 清明's 【范围隐匿】 is an aura during its 3 s (units entering the 2.0 radius are veiled, leaving ones not) — here the units inside at the cast get 3 s; “复仇者” "※重生后：此技能释放的动画动作期间免疫晕眩" (no cast animation in the sim); 扎罗's 无法撤退, 血债账款 / 【狂暴怒嗥】; enemy areas measure centre distances, so “庞贝”'s 1.4 misses a diagonal neighbour (√2); the rest of §22.12's list (碎铳之簧, 锏's 速杀, W's C4, 管, “萨科塔昂首”) is outside this item.
- **Measured** (real bot rounds — coop NORMAL, 4 bots, matches seed 3 and 7, the first bot's own field, one bounty enemy added as `bountySpawns` would, 3 sim seeds each; before = v0.1.2 code; leaks / operator knock-outs): 巨大的丑东西 R9/11/13 (18 runs) 190 → 251 / 78 → 113; “庞贝” R8/10/12 (18) 164 → 164 / 110 → 113; “复仇者” R9/11/13 (18) 193 → 218 / 88 → 92; 扎罗 R11/13 (12) 147 → 158 / 74 → 86; “墓碑” R11/13 (12) 353 → 358 / 96 → 96; 烹泉 R5/7 (12) 43 → 31 / 21 → 20; 清明 R5/7 (12) 39 → 36 / 21 → 23; 重弩突袭者 R6/8 (12) 0 → 0 / 31 → 35; 集团军重型火炮 R9/11 (12) 27 → 25 / 40 → 43. 扎罗, “墓碑” and 重弩突袭者 bounties are not drafted (`draftExcluded` 'unseen'); 集团军重型火炮 is a DOT 特训 enemy of the hard / abyss modes (`inactiveEnemyKeys` of the funny and normal ones) — added here to normal rounds all the same.
- Tests: `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` — 扎罗 (loss rate, no end, no recast while caught, cure at 18 % no / 20 % yes, the 7 s 静默 then the recast, every effect ends at the 重生, the aura −50 within 1.5 only, in the second form too, range 1.25), “复仇者” (a unit on its path 3 tiles ahead; none off its path, 4 tiles ahead, 隐匿 or 迷彩), “庞贝” (radius 1.4, the paused and restarted clock), “巨大的丑东西” (the blocked ×3 hit, the bomb at 2.17 s; the unblocked 3×3 at 100 %, the 3.0 blast), “墓碑” (40 % 3×3 unblocked, one 100 % hit blocked), 烹泉 / 沏虹 (splash r 1.0, blast r 1.25, one 15 s layer), 集团军重型火炮 (splash r 1.0, zone r 1.5; the 炎佑 dragon inside the zone is neither burned nor hit), 清明 / 堂皇 (3 s), 重弩突袭者 (no bolt before 1.4 s, revealed and standing for 2.5 s, a stun cancels it); 扎罗 leaking in its first form (every 溶血骇惧 ends, no loss after the leak); 烹泉 (an arts-dodge neighbour dodges the splash, the blast still hits a dodger). The three last fail on the branch's first version (Grok review).

### 23.7 圣聆初雪 S2 freezes the protection point, and only it (community report #32) — `content/kits/tier6.js sbell2` (`addSnow`, `freezeTile`), `render/units.js` (`ICE_TOKENS`), `render/textures.js diamondTexture` (`ice`), docs/ASSETS.md

- **Seen** (community report #32): 「圣聆初雪天赋不能冻住蓝门（积雪能不能对怪物正常生效也查一下）」. **Real**, for her S2 霜涛覆岭: 5 layers of snow on the blue gate never froze it, and nothing showed — the kit gave up because no operator can stand on a gate. Her talent 无垠的雪景 freezes nothing officially (snow, arts on passing, slow per layer); its snow works on ground enemies (checked on a walker: the mode's talent_magic_scale 0.75 × ATK arts on stepping onto a snowy tile — 539 at ATK 718 —, move ×0.64 at 3 layers = 1 − 0.12 × 3).
- **Official**: skill text 「积雪在目标点积累至5层时，使目标点变为冻结状态」 (skill_table skchr_sbell2_2; blackboard `talent@token_key` token_10058_sbell2_icetgt, 保护目标（冻结状态）, 「能够阻挡三个敌人」). PRTS 圣聆初雪 S2 备注: 「目标点冻结的实际效果为令圣聆初雪在该地块上召唤一个保护目标（冻结状态）（无视部署属性），并去除相应地块上的积雪（且存在自身的该召唤物的地块不会积雪）」, 「可以被'变为冻结状态'的目标点包括常规的保护目标点与促融共竞的保护目标点」; PRTS 保护目标（冻结状态）: 使用条件 「技能发动后于保护目标叠加5层积雪」, 备注 「持有禁疗、无法撤退」, 「圣聆初雪退场时强制撤退」. So 目标点 is the protection point — the gate — not any tile.
- **Cause**: `freezeTile` froze any tile that reached max snow but only where a melee operator could stand (`grid.canStand`), which a gate (build NONE) never is; the reverse of the official rule. The client has no model or avatar for the token (data/assets.json: owner only; PRTS 无头像), so a token showed the token fallback — 圣聆初雪's own face in a diamond (assets.js `tokenAvatarUrl`).
- **Now**: a layer bringing a goal tile (glyph E, `special` 'end') to max snow while S2 runs summons her 保护目标（冻结状态） there, deploy attributes ignored (`spawnToken` does not check them); its snow is used up and no snow gathers under her token. Any other tile at max snow just stays at max (S2's spread goes on); a gate that reached max while her one token stood elsewhere freezes on its next layer. The token blocks 3 (enemies walking into the gate are held there instead of leaking until it falls) and keeps its 禁疗 (PRTS 备注 「持有禁疗」, already modelled). On screen the frozen gate is an ice diamond: frame 0x9fe6ff and a snowflake, no picture looked up (`ICE_TOKENS`, `diamondTexture({ ice })`) — there is no ice model to load; docs/ASSETS.md (Tokens) says so.
- [ASSUMED]: the token's 70 s 再部署时间 does not gate the next freeze (as before); one token at a time per 圣聆初雪 (maxDeployCount 1).
- **Found, not changed**: PRTS 无垠的雪景 备注 「积雪的基本效果无视可选性，但无法影响具有无敌/隐匿/不可选中的单位」 — the sim's snow also slows / hurts a 隐匿 or untargetable ground enemy; the slow's 「叠加时减速数值翻一倍（具体可参考显示算法）」 note is not modelled (−12 % per layer, linear).
- Tests: `test/content/kits_alt_t6.test.js` (S2: a free tile of her range reaching 5 layers does not freeze; the gate at 4 + one layer freezes into the token on (9,2), blocks 3, its snow used up, none gathers under it; a walker is held at the gate, no leak), `test/render/unitview.test.js` (the token's ice diamond and frame).

### 23.8 琳琅诗怀雅 S1 heals an injured neighbour without an attack — a deliberate deviation (community report #5, owner's decision 2026-10-04) — `content/kits/tier3.js chess_char_3_04_a installS1`

- **Seen** (community report #5): 「琳琅诗怀雅1技能不会主动奶身边受伤的干员」 — with no enemy to attack, an injured operator next to her was never healed.
- **Official**: S1 仗义疏财 (skill_table skchr_swire2_1; this mode's data/chess.json) 「消耗一枚金币，下一次攻击会为周围八格内血量不足70%的一名友方单位恢复相当于攻击力40%的生命」 (`attack@heal_scale`; 携带此技能时金币上限为2): the heal rides on her next attack, so without an enemy to attack there is none — the sim did exactly that. Not a bug against the official text.
- **Decision**: the owner wants S1 to heal whenever an ally in her 8 surrounding tiles is injured, attack or no attack (owner's decision 2026-10-04) — a deliberate deviation from 「下一次攻击会为…恢复」, like §21.29's six 重装 skills that cast with an enemy in range instead of the official TAKE_DAMAGE row.
- **Now**: the kit's rules stay — the target is the lowest HP ratio below 70 % (the kit's reading of 「一名」) among the units of the 8 surrounding tiles (no 禁疗 or 孤立 unit, no device), a coin per heal (none without a coin), heal_scale × ATK, fx `coin` with the healed id as before. What changes is when: while she attacks, the heal still comes with the attack; when her last attack attempt found no target (`trait.hadTarget` false — no enemy in her range), she heals on her own timer. Either way at most one heal per attack cycle (her attack interval, ASPD included: `nextAt` = the heal + `s.interval`), so an attack right after a timer heal does not heal again; the `tick` hook runs after the attacks, so an attack due in the same tick takes the heal. A stunned (cannot act) or undeployed 琳琅诗怀雅 does not heal.
- [ASSUMED]: the cadence — one heal per attack cycle, the first one as soon as a coin and an injured neighbour are there.
- Tests: `test/content/kits_alt_t3.test.js` — no enemy on the field: an ally at 80 % is left alone and keeps the coin, one at 50 % is healed at once (heal_scale × ATK, one coin spent, no attack); kept injured with coins to spare, one heal per attack interval; with an enemy in her range, one heal per attack, never a second in the cycle; the existing S1 test (attack-driven heal, 周围八格 only, cap 2, no bombs) unchanged.

### 23.9 芬's 信标 sends the original operator, and the gift survives its sender (community report #6, GitHub #86) — `match/builtinMeta.js` (`use_equip_recruit_new_char_and_give_char_to_player_most_bond`, `mostBondMate`, `effect:builtin_gift` `afterElimination`), `match/effectsMeta.js EffectDispatcher.dispatchEliminated`, `match/Match.js startRound`

- **Seen**: community report #6 「精锐干员信标送出去变成一阶的了」 — a 精锐 operator sent with the beacon arrived at the teammate as the normal card of the same tier. GitHub #86: when the sender was eliminated before the next round start, the gift never arrived.
- **Official** [DATA]: act2autochess eff_acarm109 / eff_acgarm109 「装备时，目标干员和本装备销毁并进行一次特殊刷新，出现两名与携带者同等阶的干员，免费获取其中一名 / 若在同盟模拟中且存在其他队友，下个休整期向相应盟约人数最多的队友发送1个原干员 / 相应盟约人数相同则随机发送」; 芬 band_fang 「原干员在下回合传递给对应盟约人数最多的队友」. The gift is the original operator; nothing makes it depend on the sender still being in the match.
- **Cause**: (a) the gift stored `baseIdOf(target)` — the normal id — so an elite came back as its normal card holding 1 pool copy. (b) the gift was an EffectRef on the sender resolved by the sender's own `onRoundStart`; `Match.startRound` dispatches only to living players, and `PlayerState.eliminate` keeps `effects`, so an eliminated sender's gift waited forever. It was also removed before the grant, so a grant that failed (no copy left in the shared pool) lost it.
- **Now**: the gift carries the carrier's own id (`chess_char_*_b` for an elite) and its bonds (as counted at equip time, 变形同构体 pairings included). Destroying the carrier still returns its pool copies at once (an elite's 3); the grant takes them again (`grantChess` → `acquireChess`: an elite takes up to `goldenCopies`, like every effect grant), so the teammate gets the elite with 3 copies. The handler is flagged `afterElimination`: after the living players' `onRoundStart`, `Match.startRound` calls `EffectDispatcher.dispatchEliminated` for each eliminated player, which runs only such EffectRefs (nothing else of an eliminated player dispatches). The receiver is still the one picked at equip time; the effect is removed only after the operator was granted, then the CHAR_GIFT ticker names the sender.
- **Players notice**: a beaconed 精锐 arrives as that 精锐 next round; a gift sent by a player who drops out (LP 0 at the settle, or quits) still arrives; the sender's own pick of two is unchanged (two operators of the carrier's tier).
- [ASSUMED]: a receiver eliminated before the round start is replaced by the living teammate with the most members of the operator's bonds (ties at random, the text's rule applied again); with no teammate left alive the gift is dropped; a grant that fails (no copy of the operator left in the pool — the carrier's copies may be bought between the beacon and the next round start —, or the receiver's 整备区 and temp full) keeps the gift and is retried at every following round start (the official text names no refund); an elite whose copies are partly taken meanwhile arrives holding fewer than 3 (the existing rule for effect grants, as 升华 / 博士投影).
- Tests: `test/match/feedback3-gift.test.js` (an elite arrives as the elite with 3 copies and the pool count is restored; a normal carrier still sends the normal card; the sender eliminated at the R1 settle — the gift and its CHAR_GIFT ticker arrive; an empty pool keeps the gift until copies return; a receiver eliminated meanwhile → the next teammate), `test/content/items.test.js` (信标, unchanged).

### 23.10 远见's price −1 has no floor but 0 (owner's decision 2026-10-04) — `content/bonds/addon/meta.js` (`visiShip.onPrice`), research 02 §3.15

- **Seen**: the owner: with 远见 at 150 layers 至简 still cost 1; the text promises −1 on every operator, so 至简 should be free.
- **Official** [DATA]: bond 远见 (visiShip) 「<首次达到80层>所有【远见】干员购买价格永久-1资金 / <首次达到150层>以上效果改为调度中心内所有干员的购买价格永久-1资金」 (buff `bond_multi_layer_char_goods_price_bond_discount` `discount` 1). No floor is named.
- **Cause**: `visiShip.onPrice` lowered a price only while it was above 1 (`Math.min(disc, price − 1)`), research 02 §3.15's [ASSUMED] "price never below 1"; DESIGN §19.9 then kept 至简 (购买价格为1, a 特质 run first on `onPrice`) at 1.
- **Now**: both tiers take the full `discount` off whatever price reaches the bond — `ctx.modifyPrice(−1)`, which never goes below 0. Dispatch order unchanged (§19.9): the priced chess's own 特质 first (至简 3 → 1), then globals, the strategy, the bonds (远见 1 → 0). `PlayerState.priceOf` already clamps at 0; buying at 0 needs no funds (`buy`: `funds < price` is false), the shop card draws a price of 0 as FREE (`shopBar.js`, `priceTone` 'discount'), the client's 资金不足 check (`shopBlockReason`) and the bot's affordability (`price > funds`) both accept 0. The bot's full-board "worth more than the refreshes its price would pay for" comparison keeps its `max(1, price)` (a value heuristic, not a price floor).
- **Players notice**: at 150 layers 至简 is free; at 80 layers a 远见 operator brought to 1 by another discount becomes free; nothing else changes (every other operator was ≥ 2 before the −1).
- Open: 休露丝 雪域礼赠 (「每回合购买的首名<谢拉格>干员消耗资金为1」) runs as the strategy step, before the bonds, so with 远见 150 that first 谢拉格 operator now costs 0 too; whether the official game applies 远见 after the strategy's fixed price is not known.
- Docs: DESIGN §19.9 (至简 now reaches 0), research 02 §3.15 / 02-bonds.json `assumed` (→ data/bonds.json `visiShip.spec.assumed`, regenerated offline; the only data change), code comments in `garrisons/meta.js`.
- Tests: `test/content/bonds_addon.test.js` (150: 3 → 2, 2 → 1, 1 → 0, 0 → 0; 80: a 远见 operator at 1 → 0, others unchanged), `test/content/garrisons_meta.test.js` (至简 sees 1 in a global modifier, then 0 with 远见 150; `m.private` slot price 0; bought with 0 funds).

### 23.11 准备 with funds left asks first (community report #4) — `ui/gameLogic.js readyFundsPrompt`, `screens/game.js toggleReady`

- **Seen**: community report #4 「点准备继续时候如果商店里还有钱的话不会像原版那样弹提示框」 — the 准备 button (and Space) readied at once with funds left; the official client warns.
- **Official**: act2autochess `autoChessData.constData.noMoneyTipsBand` = ["band_cannot"] [DATA] — the strategies that get no "funds left" tip (坎诺特 利滚利 「每回合剩余的资金可以继承」), so every other strategy gets one; data/config.json carries it as `economy.leftoverFundsKeptByBands` (research 01 rules: noMoneyTipsBand). PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 (休整期): 「本回合的剩余资金将清零，未被冻结的干员/装备会随之清空。进入作战阶段。」 The dialog's own text is in neither the tables nor PRTS.
- **Cause**: the client sent `g.ready` straight away; only the temp overflow row could block it (`shopBlockReason('ready')`).
- **Now**: `toggleReady` (the 准备 button and Space) asks `readyFundsPrompt(m.private, { keptBands, autoplay })` first; with a prompt it opens the usual `confirmDialog` (title 剩余资金, 「还有 N 资金未使用。休整期结束时，本回合的剩余资金将清零。确定准备就绪吗？」, buttons 准备就绪 / 继续整备) and readies only on 准备就绪, if the prep is still on and the player not ready yet; a prep that ends while the question is open closes it. No prompt when un-readying, with 0 funds, already ready or eliminated, for a strategy in the list (坎诺特), or under AI 托管 (`m.public players[].autoplay`); bots never use the client. The server rule is unchanged: `g.ready` is accepted with funds and `PlayerState.endPrep` wipes them (坎诺特 keeps them).
- **Players notice**: pressing 准备 with money left asks once; 继续整备 keeps shopping, 准备就绪 (or Enter) readies; 坎诺特 and an empty purse ready at once as before.
- [ASSUMED]: the dialog wording (the rule sentence follows PRTS 帮助).
- Docs: PLAYING.md (剩余资金 row).
- Tests: `test/ui/feedback3-ready.test.js` (the prompt and its text; none at 0 / negative funds, for 坎诺特 — also without config —, when un-readying, already ready, eliminated, under AI 托管, without state; data/config.json lists band_cannot; the server still accepts g.ready and endPrep wipes the funds, 坎诺特 keeps them; game.js asks before `actions.ready` for the button and Space).

### 23.12 The host removes a human before the match (community report #17, owner approved) — `server/lobby.js kick`, `shared/protocol.js` (`room.kick {seat, playerId}`), `screens/room.js SeatCard / kick`, `main.js CLOSE_REASON.kicked`

- **Seen**: community report #17 「多人在房间准备阶段时给真人添加跟人机一样的踢出按钮，不然有的人离线了但是不会退房」 — only AI seats had the host's remove button; a human who stopped answering kept the seat.
- **Official**: none — the official room (research 06 §3.2: 4 seats, creator, ready) has no kick for players, PRTS neither; a lobby convenience the owner approved (owner's decision 2026-10-04). Before this, a human who dropped in the room lost the seat only after `lobbyGraceMs` (60 s), and one still connected but idle kept it.
- **Now**: `room.kick {seat, playerId}` (C→S, host only; `playerId` = the player the host confirmed): refused when the seat changed hands while the confirm was open — that player left and someone else sat down (`BAD_TARGET` 'seat changed hands', the newcomer stays; integration review); refused outside the LOBBY (`ROOM_STARTED` — during a match the seat is the player's to reconnect to), for the host's own seat, an AI seat (`room.removeBot` stays its message), an empty seat (`BAD_TARGET`), a non-host (`NOT_HOST`). The seat is freed at once (`removeMember`: grace timer cleared, result replay dropped, room state broadcast), so an offline seat no longer blocks `room.start`. The player gets `room.closed {reason:'kicked'}` — at once when connected, else on the next resume (`session.notice`, plus the result replay as after a grace timeout); the client goes back to the lobby with 「你已被移出同盟」 (`CLOSE_REASON.kicked`, already there). The room screen shows the host the same ✕ button on every other human's seat (tooltip 将该博士移出同盟) behind a confirm (移出同盟 「确定将「名字」移出同盟吗？对方可以凭同盟密钥重新加入。」).
- **Reconnect**: net.js reconnect tokens belong to the player's session, not to the seat; after the kick the seat binding is gone, so resuming with the old token keeps the identity (same playerId, its loadout) but lands outside the room (`room.closed {kicked}`, no `room.state`).
- [ASSUMED]: no ban — a kicked player may join the same room again with its code; no kick during a match; the result replay of the last match still follows `room.closed` for an offline kicked player (as the grace timeout).
- Docs: DESIGN §8.1 (`room.kick`, `room.closed.reason` 'kicked'), README (和朋友一起玩 step 1), the server/lobby.js header.
- Tests: `test/lobby-kick.test.js` (the schema; a connected guest: room.closed {kicked}, the seat freed, no more room.state, `NOT_IN_ROOM`, joins again; an offline guest: freed at once, the host starts alone, the old token resumes the identity with room.closed {kicked} and no room.state; refusals: non-host, self, AI seat, empty seat, no playerId (BAD_MSG), outsider, during a match; a seat that changed hands — Alice confirmed, Alice leaves, Bob takes seat 1 — is refused and Bob keeps it, naming Bob works), `test/lobby.test.js` / `test/lobby-loadout.test.js` unchanged and passing.

### 23.13 A skill with its own idle clip stands in it between attacks (community report #23) — `render/spine.js SpineActor.setSkill / _skillIdle / update`

- **Seen**: community report #23 「折娅开了技能就会不停做跳起来打人的动作，哪怕没有接敌」 — 折桠 (chess_char_2_17_a, char_4207_branch) kept jumping and striking during her S2 生存决心 with no enemy engaged.
- **Official** [DATA]: her battle model has `Skill_2_Begin`, `Skill_2_Loop` (the jump strike, OnAttack at 0.8 s), `Skill_2_End` and `Skill_2_Idle` (4 s). `Skill_N_Idle` carries no OnAttack event — it is the skill's resting pose, `Skill_N_Loop` its attack (research 07 §5.4 roles; no source spells out the official state machine, the clip names and events are the evidence). S2 starts when she takes damage (rule TAKE_DAMAGE) and her range is her own tile, so she can be in her skill with nobody to hit — the sim makes no attack then.
- **Cause**: `setSkill(true)` queued the skill's loop after the begin clip and nothing left it while the skill ran, so the attack clip looped with no attack. The skill idle (`anims.skill.idle`) was only reached after the first spell of attacks ended, and then through the skill's End clip, which belongs to the end of the skill.
- **Now**: when the equipped skill has an idle clip of its own (`skill.idle` present and not the loop) and its loop is the attack clip, the begin clip is followed by that idle; an attack plays the loop (wind-up and strike frame as before); when the attacks stop the model goes straight back to the idle; the End clip plays only when the skill ends. The manifest has 92 such skills on 62 operators' Front models (e.g. 史尔特尔 S3, 耀骑士临光 S1, 荒芜拉普兰德 S1–S3), all handled the same way; the 6 without a begin clip (耀骑士临光 S3, 缄默德克萨斯 S2 / S3, 引星棘刺 S3, 灰毫) already started in the idle and are unchanged at the start. Unchanged: a skill whose loop *is* its idle (蕾缪安 S2 / S3, 缇缇 S2, 信仰搅拌机 S3 — a stance), skills without an idle clip (begin → loop held as the stance; their spell of attacks still ends through the End clip), attacks borrowed from the normal attack (`via: 'attack'`), enemies.
- **Players notice**: 折桠 (and every operator whose skill has its own idle pose) stands in that pose while the skill runs and nothing is in range, and only swings when the sim actually attacks.
- Tests: `test/render/feedback3-skill-idle.test.js` (折桠 S2: begin → Skill_2_Idle, the loop only on attacks, back to the idle without Skill_2_End, End on stop; 史尔特尔 S3 begin → Skill_3_Idle; 耀骑士临光 S3 unchanged; 蕾缪安 S3 and 宴 unchanged).

### 23.14 The ammo bar counts the extra bullets (community report #35) — `sim/skills.js Skill.ammoMax / activate / addAmmo / end / reset`, `sim/snapshot.js unitTuple`

- **Seen**: community report #35 「拉特兰盟约叠层之后蕾缪安3技能锁定敌人消耗的子弹不会实时让技力条一格一格减少，而是最后突然消耗（最终效果好像是正确的）」.
- **Official** [DATA]: 拉特兰 「拉特兰干员开启技能获得的弹药量提升」 (`base_ammo_percent` 0.05 + `ammo_percent_per_stack` 0.015 × layers); 蕾缪安's talent 逃犯引渡手续 adds bullets at the skill start too. The bullets themselves were right (the lock / bombard count followed `ammoLeft`); only the bar was wrong.
- **Cause**: `installLaterano` raises `ammoLeft` in skillStart while `skill.ammo` stays the base 5, and the snapshot drew `ammoLeft / skill.ammo`; the client clamps the bar at 1 (`render/units.js`). With 30 layers 蕾缪安 starts S3 with 9 bullets: the bar stayed full for the first four locks, then shrank by fifths — "consumed at the end".
- **Now**: the skill keeps `ammoMax`, the most bullets this activation has held: set at activation, raised after the skillStart handlers (any bullets they added — 拉特兰, 逃犯引渡手续, talents writing `ammoLeft` directly as SIM §7.5's example does) and by `addAmmo` above it (refills under it leave it), 0 when the skill ends. The snapshot's draining bar is `ammoLeft / ammoMax`, so it starts full and every bullet takes one share (9 bullets: 8/9, 7/9 …). `skill.ammo` (the base the next activation starts from) is unchanged — raising it would compound the bonus on the next cast. No sim number changes; only the wire `sp` of a running ammo skill.
- **Players notice**: with 拉特兰 layers the skill bar drops one segment per bullet from the first shot.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback3-ammo-bar.test.js` (蕾缪安 S3 with 30 拉特兰 layers: 9 bullets, the full mark 9, the bar k/9 after each lock; a refill under the mark keeps it, above it raises it; reset at the end), `test/content/bonds_core.test.js` (拉特兰 counts, unchanged).

### 23.15 Unit sounds keep their official play chance and volume (community report #30) — `tools/assets/audio.mjs bankMix / indexAudio().mixOf`, `tools/assets/plan.mjs unitSounds`, data/assets.json `audio.sfx.units[id].mix`, `public/js/audio.js unit / unitGain / unitSoundPlays`

- **Seen**: community report #30 「狗、飞机敌人音效声音过大」.
- **Official** [DATA] (excel/audio_data.json): `battle.ON_ABILITY_ON.enemy_1000_gopro(_2/_3).combat` = an empty asset (weight 80) + `e_atk_gopro_1` (10) + `e_atk_gopro_2` (10), volume 1; `enemy_1165_duhond(_2).combat` = `e_atk_gopro_1` (20) + empty (80) — the dogs bark on about one attack in five. `enemy_1005_yokai_2 / _3.attack` (妖怪 / 威龙) = one vehicle clip at volume 0.7. Across the manifest's unit sounds 29 units have a bank that is not "always, volume 1": the three dogs (chance 0.2) and 26 with another volume (0.45–0.9 quieter, e.g. 妖怪, 盾卫's impact 0.65, 大鲍勃 0.45 / 0.6; 1.5–2 louder, e.g. 缄默德克萨斯's impact).
- **Cause**: `indexAudio` kept only the files of a bank (empty assets, weights and volumes dropped), so 猎狗pro / 深池侦察犬 played `e_atk_gopro_1` on every attack, and `audio.js unit()` played every attack / impact at a flat 0.55.
- **Now**: the asset tool records each bank's mix — `p` = the weight of its sounds that have a file over all the weights, `vol` = the played file's volume (mean of min / max) — and `plan.mjs` writes it, when either differs from 1, as `sfx.units[id].mix[role]` for attack / hit / die / born. data/assets.json was patched offline from the cached audio_data.json with the tool's own functions (each role's URL checked against the bank's first file; the JSON compare shows only the 29 `mix` entries and the content `hash`); the next `fetch-assets` run writes the same. `audio.js` plays a unit's own sound with chance `p` (a silent roll still counts as the unit's own sound: no generic `enemyHit` instead) at its base gain (0.55 attack / hit, 0.8 die / born) × min(1, `vol`): 妖怪 / 威龙 0.385, the dogs 0.55 on 20 % of their attacks; a bank louder than 1 stays at the old gain (the report asked for quieter, never louder). The roll is `Math.random` — presentation only, no determinism needed. Skill-start banks (4 official ones are quieter, 0.6–0.85) keep the old gain.
- [ASSUMED]: 猎狗pro's bank names two barks (10 + 10); the manifest has only `e_atk_gopro_1`, so it barks that one on its full 20 %. The volume is the first file's (the one the manifest downloads); a fallback file, if ever used, keeps it.
- **Players notice**: dogs bark now and then instead of on every bite; 妖怪 / 威龙 and the other officially quieter units are softer; nothing is louder.
- Tests: `test/ui/feedback3-audio-mix.test.js` (bankMix of the official dog / 妖怪 banks and `mixOf` on a picked list; data/assets.json mix vs audio_data.json; a dog barks only on rolls < 0.2 and never falls back to `enemyHit`; 妖怪 0.385, a volume-2 bank stays 0.55, 余's born 0.6), `test/ui/audio.test.js`, `test/assets.test.js` (unchanged).

### 23.16 A model load left hanging by a hidden tab is started again (GitHub #68) — `render/units.js SPINE_STUCK_MS / retryAssets / update / _acquireSpine`, `assets.js RefLru.restart / _attempt / _withTimeout`, `forgetPendingSpine`, `loadSpineData(entry, { fresh })`, store `spine.restart`

- **Seen**: GitHub #68 — after switching back to the game's tab some units stayed the placeholder (the diamond with the glyph) instead of their model.
- **Cause**: `UnitView.retryAssets` (run when the tab is shown again, §21.27) only reloads a view with no model *and no load in flight*. A load begun while the tab was hidden can stay pending — a request the background tab left hanging —, and every later try joined it: `PIXI.Assets.load` of the same URL hands out the same pending promise, and the spine cache shares an entry that is still loading. Its 20 s timeout and the bounded retries (§21.27) then ran into the same pending request until they gave up.
- **Now**: a view remembers whether its load began while the tab was hidden. When the tab is shown and that load is still in flight, `retryAssets` gives it `SPINE_STUCK_MS` = 5 s [ASSUMED] (a load about to succeed is never doubled); if it has not settled by then, `update` calls `assets.spine.restart(entry)`. `RefLru.restart` starts a new attempt of the same cache entry: its references and the promise every holder waits on stay (no acquire / release changes), the hung attempt is abandoned at once (its concurrency slot freed — a hung load no longer blocks the queue — and a late result ignored: PIXI shares resources by URL, so nothing is unloaded), the first attempt to succeed settles the entry, only the newest one's failure fails it. A restart — and any load after a remembered failure (a timeout may have left PIXI's request pending) — is `fresh`: `loadSpineData` first drops PIXI's still-pending requests of that skeleton (`forgetPendingSpine`: the .skel's entry in the loader's promise cache and, unless the atlas finished, the atlas's and its pages'; pages another cached skeleton uses stay), so the new load asks again instead of joining them. A load begun while the tab was visible keeps the old path (20 s timeout, bounded retries).
- [ASSUMED]: the 5 s grace; if the abandoned request completes after the restart, its PIXI resources are left to the garbage collector (a one-model leak on that rare path, preferred to unloading textures a shown model may share).
- **Players notice**: units whose models were loading while the game sat in a background tab show their models a few seconds after coming back, instead of staying placeholders.
- Tests: `test/render/feedback3-stuck-load.test.js` (RefLru.restart: the waiting promise settles with the new fresh attempt, refs kept, a late old result ignored; the abandoned attempt frees its slot; only the newest failure counts; forgetPendingSpine drops only unfinished requests and keeps shared pages; a view whose load began hidden is restarted SPINE_STUCK_MS after the tab is shown and shows its model with one reference; a load finishing within the grace is not doubled; a load begun while visible is not armed), `test/render/issue8-placeholders.test.js`, `test/render/assets.test.js` (unchanged).

### 23.17 Shots leave the hands and meet the chest; no attack jolt on a shown model (GitHub #61) — `render/projection.js Camera.liftFor`, `render/fx.js SHOT_HEIGHT / bodyZ / _bodyPt` (shots, boomerang, mortar, beams, lock marks), `render/units.js update` (the jolt)

- **Seen**: GitHub #61 — shots looked as if they came out of the operator's crotch and hit the enemy's hips; on every attack the whole model jumped a little toward its target (a ranged operator shooting up the screen hopped upward).
- **Official**: no data gives the official muzzle points (weapon tips are in the prefabs, not in the tables we read); the official models do not move toward their target when they attack — the attack is the Spine clip.
- **Cause**: (a) `fx.js chestZ` = feet + 0.45 × the unit's head height, used as a WORLD height; the battle models are upright screen-space billboards and the camera is pitched 30° from straight down, so a vertical world height draws at about half its length: shots started and aimed at ≈ 18–23 % of the model. (b) `units.js` shifted the body 0.12 tiles toward the target on every attack (`lunge`) — the avatar diamond's stand-in for an attack clip — also while the Spine model played its own clip.
- **Now**: `SHOT_HEIGHT = { launch: 0.45, aim: 0.5 }` [ASSUMED: ≈ a battle chibi's hands / chest; enemies' torsos] are shares of the drawn model's height above its feet; `bodyZ(cam, view, share)` turns one into a world height with `Camera.liftFor(x, y, z, px)` (the exact lift whose projection is `px` higher on screen). Shots and boomerangs leave at `launch` (the boomerang returns to it) and fly to `aim`, shells still fall to the feet, the mortar starts at `launch`, lock reticles sit at `aim`; beams go from the shooter's `launch` (a chain bounce from the previous target's `aim`) to the target's `aim`. Other unit-anchored effects (`_chest`: buff sparkles, auras) are unchanged. The jolt runs only while no Spine model is shown (the avatar diamond, DESIGN §9's "bob / lunge" fallback).
- **Height used**: the unit's head height (`_headTiles`, the HP bar's): the uniform chibi headroom for operators and tokens, the setup-pose bounds for enemies (clamped as for the bar). Operators' setup-pose bounds are not their drawn body — they include weapons and effect sprites (median 1.34 tiles, 90th percentile 2.35, one skeleton 7.62) —, so shots from them would leave above the visible model; the review's tall-operator case (秋白, 史尔特尔 …) is such bounds, not a taller chibi. A tiny enemy whose bounds sit under the bar's 0.55-tile floor (青瓷 / 彩瓷茶器) fires from near its top.
- **Players notice**: arrows, bolts and beams come from about the hands and strike the upper body; a model no longer hops toward its target on each attack.
- Tests: `test/render/feedback3-shot-height.test.js` (liftFor exact on the normal / prep / boss / unite cameras; an arrow starts at 45 % and aims at 50 % of the drawn models — the old rule drew < 25 %; a raised / hovering unit measured from where it is drawn; the diamond still lunges, a shown Spine model stays put and plays its attack clip), `test/render/fxproj.test.js`, `test/render/projection.test.js` (unchanged).

### 23.18 The leader stands on the boss field in the prep; its hit tiles light red beside a range preview (community report #12) — `match/waves.js previewOf` (`start`), `render/prepfield.js leaderStand`, `render/pick.js hitTiles`, `render/app.js setLeader / leaderShown / syncLeaderHits / leaderAt / LEADER_HIT_STYLE`

- **Seen**: community report #12 「最终boss应该在准备时间就放到关卡中，点击干员拖动选面朝方向并且展示干员攻击范围时候也展示那样大体型boss实际受击地块，干员攻击范围目前用的橙色，boss受击范围可以像官方原版那样用红色」 — in the Final Assault / Hidden Core prep the camera shows the boss field, but the leader waited in the enemy pen off screen, and choosing a direction showed only the operator's orange range.
- **Official**: research 09 §2.2 [DATA + WIKI]: during a boss round's prep the leader previews on the boss field, standing by its spawn with its HP bar (the PRTS 最终攻势 intro image). Research 08 §4.1 decoded the client's preview row offset (+13 for leader rounds), which lands in the pen — the two disagree; the owner's decision 2026-10-04 (approving report #12) puts the leader on the field. The hit area is the sim's: data/enemies.json `hitArea` (PRTS 巨型单位 "长4.95、宽2.95的长方形，向上偏移1.0", DESIGN §19.4) — 胄 / 管 / 昆图斯 / 阿利斯泰尔 / 萨米的意志 occupy 5 × 3 tiles (rows 3–5, cols 8–12 from (3, 10)); 铳 and 卢西恩 are point units (their tile). No source shows the colour of such tiles: red is the players' request.
- **Cause**: `previewOf` dropped the leader's route start, so the client could only lay it out in the pen (`gateOf` + 13 → the pen's upper zone, `layoutPen`), which only the pen camera shows; the range preview (`highlightTiles` groups `facing` / `selRange`) had no notion of the leader's body.
- **Now**: the leader's preview entry carries its spawn tile `start` ([row, col] on the boss field; escorts and bounties do not). In the prep the field view takes the first such entry out of the pen layout (`leaderStand`) and stands its model there (`setLeader`: idle, its full HP bar, facing the player's half — left half → left [ASSUMED look] —, a tap opens its details like a pen enemy), shown only by the boss-field prep camera (`leaderShown`, also during a flight to / from it); a real battle clears it. Whenever a range preview is shown there — the direction wheel's (`facing`) or a selected piece's (`selRange`, any RANGE_GROUPS highlight) — its hit tiles (`hitTiles` = the sim's `body.js bodyKeys`: every tile its rectangle overlaps) are lit in red (`LEADER_HIT_STYLE` 0xff3b30, fill 0.3 [ASSUMED]) beside the orange range, and go with it. Display only: no sim or rule change. The escorts stay in the pen (research 09 says they preview on the boss field too — not part of the report; left open).
- **Players notice**: in the boss rounds' prep the leader stands where it will fight, with its HP bar; while choosing an operator's direction (or looking at a selected operator's range) the tiles where the leader can be hit show in red next to the orange range, so one can see at once whether the range reaches it.
- Tests: `test/render/feedback3-leader-prep.test.js` (previewOf carries `start` for the leader only; every boss template's leader preview = its route start; `leaderStand` + `hitTiles` = the sim's bodyKeys for every leader, 15 tiles for the huge ones, 1 for 铳; the leader view shows a full HP bar; app.js wiring checks), `test/render/fieldview.browser.test.js` "Final Assault prep (L / R): the leader stands on its spawn tile …" (RENDER_E2E: on (3, 10), facing the player's half, not in the pen, no red tiles until a `facing` preview, then the 15 hit tiles, cleared with it, hidden by the own-board camera; screenshots fa-leader-L / -R), `test/match/waves.test.js`, `test/render/pen.test.js` (unchanged).

### 23.19 Spectator seats: up to two per co-op room, watching like an eliminated player (community report #26, owner approved — a remake feature) — `server/lobby.js` (`Room.spectators / spectatorOf`, `spectate`, `removeSpectator`, `freeSpectatorSeat`, `roomOf`, `onHello / onDisconnect / runResync`, `routeGame`, `memberSessions / sendToPlayer`, `buildReplay`, `disposeRoom`), `server/match/Match.js` (`opts.spectators`, `addSpectator / removeSpectator / _spectator / _viewers / _resync / _spectatorSpec`, `handle`, `sendTo / _sendStart / _humansShowing`, `finish`), `server/net.js HEAVY_TYPES`, `shared/constants.js MAX_SPECTATORS / ERR.SPECTATOR`, `shared/protocol.js` (`room.spectate`, `room.removeSpectator`), `store.js isSpectating`, `main.js onRoomState`, `screens/lobby.js` (观战), `screens/room.js SpectatorBar / roomFacts`, `screens/game.js`, `ui/hud.js TopBar`, `ui/combatHud.js`, `ui/matchChrome.js ExitModal`, `screens/briefing.js`, `screens/bandDraft.js`

- **Seen**: community report #26 「添加一到两个观战席」.
- **Official**: none — **not in the official game**. The official room has the players' 4 seat cards and nothing else (research 06 §3.2); watching exists only for the players themselves: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 「阵亡的玩家可在模拟中观战」, in 联防 「所有玩家于该阵地观战」, and a teammate's field after the own battle (research 09 §3.1, §6.3; §14 Spectating). A remake feature the owner approved (owner's decision 2026-10-04); no rule of the match changes.
- **Now (the seats)**: `room.spectate {code}` (C→S) takes one of a co-op room's `MAX_SPECTATORS` = **2** spectator seats — in its lobby **or while its match runs** (a player seat can only be taken in the lobby). Refused: unknown code (`ROOM_NOT_FOUND`), a solo room or both seats taken (`ROOM_FULL`), a player of that room (`ALREADY` — nobody switches to spectating in place), a session whose own room is in a match (`ROOM_STARTED`, as `room.join`); repeated by the spectator it is idempotent, and from another lobby room it leaves that room first (as `room.join`). `room.state` carries `spectators: [{ playerId, name, connected }]` (always present, `[]` without). A spectator is **no player**: never in `seats`, never counted for the 1–4 players, the ready count or the `room.start` gate, never host (`migrateHost` reads `seats` only), and never keeps a room alive — when the last human leaves, the room closes and its spectators get `room.closed {empty}` (players who left themselves get nothing, as before). The host frees a spectator seat with `room.removeSpectator {playerId}` (any time; `NOT_HOST` / `BAD_TARGET` otherwise): the spectator gets `room.closed {reason:'kicked'}` at once, or on its next resume when offline (with the result replay, as `room.kick` §23.12 and the grace timeout). `room.leave` / `g.leave` leave a spectator seat; in the lobby a spectator takes a free player seat with `room.join` of the same code (UI 入座).
- **Now (what a spectator may do)**: `g.watch` (the per-socket heavy bucket, 2/s burst 6, like every watcher), `g.leave` / `room.leave`, and `room.loadout` (checked and stored on its session for a later match, never handed to the match). Everything else → the new `ERR.SPECTATOR` (「观战中无法进行该操作」): the lobby refuses every other `g.*` / `b.*` before the match sees it (`routeGame`), `room.ready` too; host-only messages stay `NOT_HOST`; `Match.handle` refuses anything but `g.watch` from a spectator as well. **Emotes are off for spectators** [ASSUMED] — they see the players' bubbles but cannot send any, so they cannot spam the match.
- **Now (what a spectator sees)** — what an eliminated player watching sees, never more: every broadcast of the room (`room.state`, `m.public`, `m.ticker`, `m.emote`, `b.pool`: the public data every player gets); the match registers the spectator (`opts.spectators` at the start, `addSpectator` when it joins mid-match and on every resume / resync) and shows it fields through `_viewers()` — the loop that already served eliminated humans: each 各自行动 the first field (`b.start watch: true`, a display replica; server-run mode `m.field` + `b.snap`), the 联防 spec as an observer, the first boss field of 最终攻势 / 隐秘核心, any field on `g.watch` (alive false: none of the fighting player's limits — own battle running, the other pair's boss field), a player's board in prep on `g.watch 'n:<pid>'` (the one-shot scouting `m.field {prep: true}`), `b.end` of the field it watches, and `m.result` at the end (the rows every player gets; `playerId` = the spectator, no row of its own). Never an `m.private` (shop, hand, 临时整备区, funds, effects, loadout), an `m.toast` or an `m.unitStats`; never a field's player or authority.
- **Private-data check**: the watch view's `b.start` spec carries `players[].contentInfo`, battle-start counters written by `global:contentb_info` (sim/content/support/meta.js): `funds`, `handUnits`, `handChess`, `roundStats` … — every watcher (a teammate's 前往查看, an eliminated player) receives them, no screen shows them. A spectator's copy leaves out **`contentInfo.funds`** (`Match._spectatorSpec`, cached per field): no battle effect reads it (only the prep-side meta reads `ctx.funds()`), so the replica still plays the same battle. The other counters stay: battle effects read them (`handUnits` — the prep-gated 机变 cards' fallback; `roundStats.gainedChess` — an item), and a replica with other inputs could show another fight. `m.public players[]` (LP, strategy, shop level, board count, bonds, status) is what every teammate already sees.
- **Reconnect**: a spectator seat is kept like a player's — in the lobby `connected: false`, freed after `lobbyGraceMs` (60 s; then `room.closed {timeout}` on resume); during a match kept for the reconnect window (10 min), its disconnect not told to the match (it plays no field). A resume (or a repeated hello, coalesced per `resyncMinGapMs` as for players) answers `room.state` + `Match.addSpectator`: `m.public` and the watched battle (first field by default), or the result once ended.
- **Client**: the lobby has **观战** beside 加入同盟 (tooltip: 不占博士席位，只能观看，每个同盟最多 2 名，模拟进行中也可进入). The room shows a **观战席 N/2** strip under the seats while it has spectators — names, offline marks, the host's ✕ (no confirm [ASSUMED]: a spectator can come back with the code), and **入座** for a spectator while a player seat is free; a spectator's ready button reads 观战中 (disabled) and its status line 「观战中 · 不占博士席位，模拟开始后可切换观看各位博士」. `main.js` keeps the room for a spectator (`onRoomState` checks `spectators` too). In the match a spectator takes the eliminated player's path (`alive` false): no shop, no ready toggle (`TopBar spectator`), no 交流 wheel, no 返回自己 / 返回战场; the briefing button reads 观战中, the draft sends no `g.bandFocus`; the pill reads 「观战中 · 点击左侧成员头像切换查看」 in the watching pill's amber and the combat HUD 「观战中…」 — never 你已被淘汰; in 休整期 / 机变 / round start it is shown the first player still in by itself (once per phase; a team row switches); the exit dialog offers only **离开观战** (no 暂离).
- **Protocol version**: `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays 1, as for every change since the first import (`room.loadout`, `g.pause`, `g.unitStats`, `m.unitStats`, the `b.*` frames): the change is additive — two new C→S types, a new `room.state` field, a new error code, an optional match option and two optional match methods. The server serves its own client, so only a stale tab could meet them, and the build guard reloads such a tab (ui/buildGuard.js).
- **Server load**: at most 2 per room; a spectator's frames are the room's broadcasts (encoded once, `broadcastRoom`) plus the `b.start` / `m.field` of what it watches; `g.watch` and `room.spectate` (a mid-match join resends the state) draw from the heavy bucket; the per-network socket cap is unchanged. `/healthz` counts `spectators`.
- [ASSUMED]: solo rooms have no spectator seat; spectators cannot emote; no ban after a removal (like `room.kick`); no confirm on the ✕; the host may also remove a spectator during a match (server; the UI offers it in the room only); the spec without `funds`; the auto-shown board in prep; no spectator count in the match HUD.
