- Docs: DESIGN §8.1 (spectator-seat paragraph), META §1.4, PLAYING §8, README (观战 row), the headers of `server/lobby.js`, `server/match/Match.js` (interface), `screens/room.js`, `screens/lobby.js`, `screens/game.js`.
- Tests: `test/lobby.test.js` (+3, StubMatch / RecordingMatch over sockets: join / idempotent / `ALREADY` / cap / never a player seat / `SPECTATOR` and `NOT_HOST` refusals / 入座 / leave / host removal online and offline (`room.closed {kicked}` on resume) / solo and unknown codes / a room's last human leaving → `room.closed {empty}`; during a match: broadcasts but no `m.private`, a mid-match join, refusals, `room.loadout` kept off the match, drop and resume give the seat back, staying through the result; lobby grace like a player, `opts.spectators`, only `g.watch` routed, `addSpectator` on join / resume, `removeSpectator` on `g.leave`, never `onDisconnect / onReconnect / onLeave`; the protocol fuzz now also takes / frees spectator seats and checks the cap, no spectator in solo rooms, never seated twice, never host, the session pointing at its seat), `test/match/spectator.test.js` (3: a whole client-combat match — 各自行动, 联防, 最终攻势, settlement — every `b.start` watched and never authoritative, the first field like an eliminated player, `b.end` of the watched boss field, the same `m.result` rows, no frame (unicast or broadcast) with a private key, every player intent refused; mid-battle `g.watch` of any field, the spec without `funds`, a spectator added mid-battle gets the state, a removed one gets nothing; server-run mode `m.field` + `b.snap`), `test/match/lobby-integration.test.js` (+1: the real Match over sockets — briefing / draft / prep refusals, the prep board, a watched `b.start` without funds, drop and resume, no private frame, the room closing for it when the last player leaves), `test/client-static.test.js` (+1: `isSpectating`, `roomFacts`), `test/ui/spectator.e2e.test.js` (opt-in browser, `SP_E2E=1`: lobby 观战 → room strip and 观战中 → the host's ✕ → briefing / prep / battle views, no `m.private`, a reload, 离开观战; screenshots `test/e2e/out/spectator-*.png`).

### 23.20 阿戈尔 5: the 3 revives belong to the first 3 members by position (community report #24) — `content/bonds/core.js installEgir` (`egirOrder`, shared with `devour`)

- **Seen** (community report #24, many players): 「阿戈尔盟约复活有bug，只能复活一个干员」「阿戈尔5人羁绊不生效」. The usual food chain puts the carries back-left and the food front-right; the battle-start devour knocks the food out first, and our rule ("the first 3 members knocked out", since 0.1.0) gave it the 2–3 revives at t ≈ 0 — seen only as a fall and a redeploy in the deploy frame. In the fight the carries fell and nobody came back. Measured (real co-op Match 险境, seeds 3 / 11, R1–R14 with 联防 and the boss, the 46 battles of the player holding 5 阿戈尔): 126 instant revives at t = 0; 4 of the 16 later knock-outs were revived.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约 下半/PRTS盟约记录, 阿戈尔: "<在场5名不同【阿戈尔】干员>前3名【阿戈尔】干员首次被击倒时立刻复活" (bonds.json `max_free_respawn_cnt` 3); the revived unit is a 受益者 ("“复活”的实现方式为：受益者因移动之外的原因退场时…"); the devour note starts "从最先部署（更靠左和靠上的）的【阿戈尔】干员开始". The 2026-03-14 notice (ak.hypergryph.com news 5114) raises the 阿戈尔 revivers, a 特定数量, to 前3名 — worded unlike 埃芒加德's first N knocked out. Players' videos read it as by position: BV146QXB4EUK (2026-03-22, danmaku 207–284 s: by position, left to right and top to bottom, not by who is eaten first), BV11XwCzXEH9 (2026-03-19, 66 s: column by column from the left until the count runs out), BV17jSsBpEER (act 1: the uploader's pinned reply — by position, bottom / left in act 1, unrelated to the eating order). So the 3 are the first 3 阿戈尔 in the deployment / devour order (left column first, top to bottom; mirrored on the right-hand boss side), and each revives once on its own first knock-out, whatever the order of knock-outs.
- **Cause**: `installEgir`'s death hook revived any member on its first knock-out until 3 revives were spent.
- **Now**: at `battleStart`, before `devour()`, the beneficiaries are fixed: the first `max_free_respawn_cnt` member operators on the field in the devour's order (`egirOrder`, now shared by both: board column — a mirrored player counts from the field's right —, then top first, then id). The death hook (priority 11, still ahead of 不屈's 10) revives only a beneficiary, once, on its first `'killed'`; the other members' knock-outs are left to 不屈 and the rest. A member the devour knocks out comes back only when it is a beneficiary; its pending marks are cancelled as in 0.1.2 (§22.3). §22.3's chain 浊心斯卡蒂 → 乌尔比安 → 幽灵鲨 → 海霓 → 深巡 → 隐现: 幽灵鲨 (third by position) falls and is back at t = 0, 海霓 and 深巡 stay down until their redeploy, 浊心斯卡蒂 and 乌尔比安 come back on their first knock-out in the fight. Every battle (normal, 联防, boss) fixes its own beneficiaries. Measured again (the same 46 battles): 47 instant revives at t = 0 (only food among the first 3 by position); 10 of 25 later knock-outs revived (the carries; the rest were second knock-outs or other members).
- [ASSUMED]: (1) A member entering 联防 down (forced out before `battleStart`, §19.3) is no beneficiary; the next members on the field are. A 调和 member counts like any other member. (2) A beneficiary that 埃芒加德 or M3茧甲 saves in place (the remake's stand-in for PRTS's 0-time redeploy, §21.21) has used its 阿戈尔 revive, so its first knock-out after that is final — by analogy with PRTS's 不屈 note "持有“复活”效果时，即使触发了不屈的“重新部署”效果，也还是会消耗一次“复活”次数" (a held 复活 is consumed even when another effect brings the unit back at once). Read when the beneficiary is knocked out: its in-place revive count (`mem.revives`, items `revivedInPlace`) is compared with the one at battle start.
- Not changed: the devour (§22.3) and its order; 埃芒加德's own rule (the battle's first 3 knock-downs, as the notice words it — the devour's food still takes its charges at t = 0, community report #37: not a bug since 0.1.2). No icon marks the 3 holders yet.
- **§22.3 edited**: the one sentence that said the 3 revives are spent at t = 0 "as officially" now says what 0.1.2 did and points here [integrator: renumber "§23.20" there]. research 02 §3.6 and its JSON spec say "by position" (data/bonds.json `egirShip.spec` regenerated with `build-data --offline`; a JSON compare shows only that string changed).
- Tests: `test/content/bonds_core.test.js` — '阿戈尔 5: the first 3 members by position …' (rewritten: the bottom of the first column and the second column, knocked out first, stay down; the three top-left revive in any order, at full HP on the same tile with SP reset; a second knock-out is final; the relocation case; 3 members: none), '阿戈尔 5 + the devour chain (GitHub #33) …' (rewritten for the new outcome, normal and 联防), new '阿戈尔 5 beneficiaries: fixed at battle start …' (a 联防 member entering down keeps the slot (the forced exit is not a 击倒; reviewed 2026-10-04), a 调和 member included, the mirrored boss side from its own left), new '阿戈尔 5: a beneficiary 埃芒加德 or M3茧甲 save in place …'. `test/content/feedback3-egir-down.test.js` passes unchanged; `test/content/feedback1f-hammer.test.js`'s devour case has 3 members (no 5-tier) and passes unchanged.

### 23.21 傀儡师: the switch ends at full HP (community report #2) — `sim/professions.js installDollkeeper` (`startSwitch`)

- **Seen** (community report #2): 「傀儡师归溟幽灵鲨死后（阿戈尔盟约吞噬）之后无法正常切换形态复活」. 0.1.2 (§22.11) gave her the 替身 on a lethal hit, and a single devour mark works (full-HP 替身, back to the 本体 at 21 s). With two or more marks on her — e.g. 乌尔比安 → 斯卡蒂 → 归溟幽灵鲨 → 深巡 → 幽灵鲨 → 隐现 — the first mark switches her and the next lands inside the 1-s switch: the switch is no knock-out, so the mark is not cancelled; 无敌 does not stop a 流失 and 不死 holds it at 1 HP. She then fought the 替身's 20 s at 1 HP (1/3875), fell to the first hit and was down for 70 s.
- **Official**: PRTS 分支特性信息 §傀儡师: "切换动画开始时会清除自身一切Buff；切换途中重设自身生命至最大值，随后再次清除自身一切Buff", and during the switch animation she holds 不死 and 无敌 (with 阻回, 禁疗, 孤立, 强制缴械 and stun / freeze / sleep immunity). The HP reset comes after the start of the switch and 不死 holds throughout, so she always leaves the switch at full HP.
- **Cause**: `enter()` / `leave()` set the HP to the max only at the switch's start; the `fatal` hook's 不死 then kept a later lethal 流失 at 1 HP, and nothing set the HP again.
- **Now**: the 1-s `trait:dollSwitching` buff also sets the HP to the form's max when it expires (both directions: to the 替身 — its own max, 风丸's 纸偶 HP — and back to the 本体), if she is still on the field. Its early removal (knocked out, withdrawn) only clears the flag, as before. The 2-mark case: 1 HP at t ≈ 0, full HP (3875) when the switch ends at 1 s; a lethal 流失 inside the switch back is held at 1 HP and the 本体 comes out at full HP.
- [ASSUMED] (kept from §22.11): the switch lasts `DOLL_SWITCH` (1 s); the reset is placed at its end — PRTS says "途中" without a time, and the end is the latest point that still makes every lethal 流失 inside the switch harmless. The second "清除自身一切Buff" after the reset is not modelled (statuses landing during the switch stay; she is 无敌 and immune to stun / freeze / sleep then).
- docs/SIM.md (Professions, dollkeeper) says it.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback2-doll.test.js` new 'E: a lethal 流失 inside the switch (a second 阿戈尔 devour mark) …' (the real chain with 5 阿戈尔: two devour marks on her, 1 HP inside the switch, full HP when it ends; then a lethal 流失 inside the switch back, held at 1 HP, the 本体 at full HP; never knocked out — fails on 0.1.2's code); cases A–D, the hammer and 风丸 cases, `test/sim/professions.test.js`, `test/render/feedback2-doll.test.js` and `test/content/feedback3-indom-exits.test.js` pass unchanged.

### 23.22 联防 fights with the layers the helpers' own combat reached (community report #24) — `match/PlayerState.js battleInput` (`reached`), `bondsView`, `match/unite.js uniteBattleOpts`

- **Seen** (community report #24): 「两百多层不屈盟约复活有时候依然不触发」. The 不屈 chance itself matches the official (0.18 + 0.004 × layers: 196 → 287/300 hits, 200 → 292/300, 204 → 299/300, ≥ 205 → 300/300). But the 联防 battle ran on the round-start layers while the strip showed the layers reached in the own combat: with +20 in the own combat, 联防 used 230 while the strip showed 250 (a real co-op Match, real sim). Near 200 layers that is the gap between a sure redeploy and a miss.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §联防阶段: "所有防卫成功的玩家（最多2名）将以其阵地当前的状态，迎接所有防卫失败玩家未击倒敌人的集合。该阶段不能叠加层数。"
- **Cause**: `uniteBattleOpts` → `PlayerState.battleInput` → `bonds: bondSnapshot(this.bonds)` — the persistent state; the round's in-battle gains sit in `pendingLayerGains` until SETTLE, and only the views (`bondsView`, §20.15) added them.
- **Now**: the 联防 input takes its bonds from `bondsView()` (`battleInput({ reached: true })`): the persistent layers plus the own combat's pending gains, floored and capped at 999 exactly as `settle()` adds them (`bondsWithGains` / `layerGainRoom`) — the count the strip shows. Nothing else changes: the 联防 still gains no layers (`layerGainsEnabled` false), its result's gains are never read, and SETTLE adds the own combat's gains to `ps.layers` once, as before (no double count: the next round starts from 250, not 270). The normal and boss inputs keep the persistent layers (a boss round has no own combat before it).
- [ASSUMED]: "当前的状态" includes the layers the own combat reached that round (PRTS lists no field of that state; the strip shows them and the official mode carries layers within the round). The helpers' order between two (unite.js `helperOrder`, "盟约层数最高(存疑)") uses those same reached layers — persistent plus the pending gains, capped like the battle — so the right-hand helper is the one who actually fights with more layers. [ASSUMED] the 存疑 tie-break follows the layers the 联防 battle uses; the official line does not say "persistent only".
- Exit paths for 不屈 re-checked on this head (no change here): knocked out on the ground, 撤退 / 商人 (0.1.3, workstream W3A), 流失 to 0: rolls; the 联防 forced exit: no (official); an operator on a 高台 tile: no (by tile, community report #33). Pith: no 不屈 effect of her own (调和 +1 is for core bonds; 不屈 is not core).
- docs: DESIGN §5.1 (`bonds`), §6.1 UNITE and §20.15, META (bond layers in the views), PLAYING (联防), research 01 §4.4 say it.
- Tests: `test/match/watch-bonds.test.js` new 'the 联防 field fights with the layers the helper's own combat reached …' (fake battles: p_0 perfect with +20 不屈 / +9 炎, p_1 leaks; the own combat's input 230 / 995; the 联防 input 250 and 999 (capped), equal to the strip; nothing persistent before SETTLE; after it 250 / 999, and the round-2 combat starts from 250 — fails on 0.1.2's code); the other `watch-bonds` cases and `feedback3-unitecarry`, `unitedown`, `combat`, `clientCombat`, `observe`, `results`, `hardening`, `teammate-loadout` match tests pass unchanged.

### 23.23 The initial deployment goes by column: the left column first, top to bottom (the report's side finding) — `sim/Battle.js start`, `match/board.js mergeTile` (+ `boardOrder`'s comment), `ui/gameLogic.js mergeTarget`

- **Seen** (re-verification of the 阿戈尔 reports): `Battle.start` deployed row-major — the top row first, left to right within a row (the mirrored right boss side: right to left in field columns) — while the 阿戈尔 devour, written from PRTS's own wording, already ran by column. Act-2 videos (BV1rVwjziEQt, BV1pGAPzrEpZ) show the operators coming in column by column from the left, and the aggro that follows the same order.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §作战阶段 "按从上到下>从左到右的顺序部署。优先部署干员，随后为召唤物（如果有）", with the note "自卫戍协议：盟约 下半（2026/3/14）起，部署顺序由“从左到右>从下到上”更改为“从上到下>从左到右”"; §多人 "分属于右侧场地的玩家，其与方向相关的盟约效果、部署顺序等都将左右镜像变化". Read as a scanning order — down each column, the columns from left to right (act 1: along each row, the rows from the bottom) — it agrees with PRTS's 阿戈尔 note "从最先部署（更靠左和靠上的）的【阿戈尔】干员开始", the bond text's devour priority ("优先更靠左和靠上"), act 1's (bottom, then left) and the videos. Read as sort keys (the top row first) it contradicts all of them.
- **Cause**: §5.5 / §19.1 took "从上到下>从左到右" as row-major ([ASSUMED] in §19.1).
- **Now**: per player the operators deploy by column — the left board column first, top to bottom within a column — then the summon pieces the same way; the right-hand boss player starts from its own left (the highest field column), as the devour does (`egirOrder`). The shared-field interleave (the i-th operators of every player together, then the summons) and every other rule are unchanged; unit ids keep the battle input's reading order (`board.js boardOrder`, now described as what it is: the input / dispatch / view order, not the deployment order). What follows from the order: the 仇恨 tie-break "最后部署的目标" (`aggroSeq`) — an unblocked ranged enemy at equal taunt now picks the bottom of the rightmost column —, 耀骑士临光's "上一名部署干员", the order of `deploy` effects. A merge's elite among several deployed copies takes the tile of the copy that deploys first [ASSUMED, §20.11]: `mergeTile` and the client's `mergeTarget` (the gold tile in prep) now sort col asc, then row desc (in board coordinates: the mirrored boss half deploys in the same board order).
- [ASSUMED]: the scanning reading of the PRTS sentence (the integrator's reading of the report; PRTS gives no example). Kept [ASSUMED] from §19.1: the shared-field interleave.
- **Measured** — every test that moved (the full sim, content, match, ui, render and root folders were run; details in the workstream notes): `test/sim/battle.test.js` (the rule itself: new expected order, two cases added to tell column-first from row-first, also on the mirrored boss side), `test/sim/playtest5-targeting.test.js` #5 (the latest deployed is now the bottom of the rightmost column: new expected order and target), `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` 烹泉 / 沏虹 and 碎骨 and `test/content/kits_t6.test.js` 余 S3 (their enemy's target was "the latest deployed", which moved to another unit; the layouts — or, for 余, a taunt on the operator behind the wall — now keep the same target, the assertions are unchanged), `test/ui/playtest6-elite.test.js` (mergeTarget: the left column wins over a higher row; a same-column case added), `test/match/merge.test.js` (the expected first deployed tile of the 突变细胞 SETTLE case is computed by column), `test/docs-consistency.test.js` (META's merge sentence). Bots, client-combat parity, 突袭, replays and every other match test pass unchanged.
- docs: DESIGN §5.5, §19.1 (#5) and §20.11, SIM (start), META (board.js, garrison dispatch order, merges), PLAYING (作战, 晋升精锐, 仇恨), research 00 / 01 (§4.3, A1 rows 4.3 and 7, the promotion addendum) / 03 / 09 say it.
- Tests: as listed under Measured; plus the comment fixes in `test/sim/combat.test.js`, `test/match/hardening.test.js`, `test/match/playtest6-elite-to-board.test.js`.

### 23.24 深溟巢涌者 do not attack; a revealed 节日爵士乐手 only hits what it blocks — `content/enemies.js`

- **Seen**: 深溟巢涌者 and 富营养的巢涌者 swung a normal attack. A 节日爵士乐手 that had been revealed and was not blocking still picked a target and attacked.
- **Official**: the nest-spawner pages walk and pulse an aura; they are not attackers. The jazz page's revealed unblocked form only attacks what it blocks.
- **Now**: both nest spawners have `noAttack`. While they walk, a 1 s aura pulse hits allies in range, and the pulse pauses while the enemy is hidden. A revealed unblocked 节日爵士乐手 is melee, so it only swings at its blocker.
- [ASSUMED]: the pulse radius and the 100 % 无途径 arts plus the 5 % 神经 on the enriched spawner follow the pages read for 0.1.3; the pause-while-hidden is the same hidden-enemy rule as the rest of the sim.
- Tests: `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback2-stealth-splash.test.js` (the nest pulse still hits 迷彩).

### 23.25 A split or summoned child does not carry the kill bounty (GitHub #67, #89 item 2) — `content/enemies.js spawnChildren`, `match/unite.js planUnite`

- **Seen**: a child spawned when an enemy split or summoned paid the parent's kill bounty again. In 联防 a leaked child could also pay the card bounty of a different enemy.
- **Now**: `spawnChildren` drops `bountyId` and `bountyCoins` on the child and leaves them on the parent. Killing the body pays once; killing a child pays nothing. `planUnite` attaches a card bounty only when the leaked enemy's key is that card's enemy key.
- [ASSUMED]: none. The parent's bounty is unchanged.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback3-bounty-children.test.js`.

### 23.26 A 隐匿 blocker is hit only by the blocked enemy's attacks — not its areas, zones or auras (GitHub #97) — `sim/targeting.js areaSelectable / auraSelectable`, `content/enemies.js areaAllies / auraAllies`, `content/bosses.js`

- **Seen**: GitHub #97. A 隐匿 operator that is blocking an enemy was still selected by that enemy's splash, blast, zone and buff aura. 0.1.2 (`targeting.js`, DESIGN §22.12) had made that the rule on purpose.
- **Official**: PRTS 作战机制 §隐匿 "隐匿效果使得获得该效果的单位无法被任何敌方的能力索敌选中", and "因为“阻挡优先级最高”的效果，敌人会无视一切可选性对该干员进行攻击" — the attack, not the area. §AOE伤害判定 "AOE的判定是对攻击范围内的每个可以被选中的敌人进行判定". §隐匿与Buff的关系 "隐匿状态下的单位一般无法被敌方的索敌机制和Buff选择器选中为目标". PRTS 异常效果 迷彩: splash, 中点判定 / 格子判定 and auras are not stopped by 迷彩 (ba.camou "无法躲避溅射类攻击"). Direct attacks still land.
- **Cause**: `auraSelectable` returned true for a 隐匿 ally when `src.alive && src.blockedBy === a`. `areaSelectable` delegates to it, so splash, blasts, zones and auras all kept the blocker. That reading of 阻挡优先级 for area abilities was marked [ASSUMED] in §22.12.
- **Now**: `auraSelectable` rejects every 隐匿 ally, including the one blocking `src`. Areas follow. `canTargetAlly` is unchanged: the blocked enemy's own attack still hits (ranged attacks from other enemies still do not). 迷彩 is still not checked. A dead `src` (a death blast; the body is unblocked before the `death` hook) never gets a blocker exception — there is no exception left to apply. Abilities marked 无视无法选择 / 无视目标可选性 still do not call this selector. Owner's decision 2026-10-04: the 0.1.2 [ASSUMED] is withdrawn.
- **Players notice**: a 隐匿 blocker (伪装服, 叙拉古, 伊内丝 S2, an 排气格栅) no longer takes the enemy it blocks' self-blast, grenade splash, zone or aura. It still takes that enemy's attacks. A 迷彩 neighbour still takes the splash.
- Docs: SIM.md (the area-selector paragraph, the zone line, the `stealth` row). DESIGN §22.12 stays as the 0.1.2 text; this draft replaces the blocker exception.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback2-stealth-splash.test.js` (9 — the selector skips a 隐匿 blocker for areas and auras and still takes 迷彩; “庞贝”'s blast misses its 隐匿 blocker and a 隐匿 bystander, hits a 迷彩 bystander, and its attacks still land on the blocker; a dead source does not select its former 隐匿 blocker).
- Interaction (script, not a separate file): a 高能源石虫 death blast (`enemy_1021_bslime`, radius 1.25) damaged the 迷彩 neighbour and not the 隐匿 blocker, including when `blockedBy` was forced back onto the dead body; 深池伙友卫队's force field did not stick to the 隐匿 blocker and did stick to the 迷彩 neighbour. No new [ASSUMED].

### 23.27 A 冻结 from a second 寒冷 lasts the longer of the two colds, not a fixed 3 s — `sim/Battle.js applyStatus`, `sim/constants.js COLD_FREEZE_DURATION`

- **Seen**: stacking 寒冷 always froze the target for 3 s (`COLD_FREEZE_DURATION`), whether the colds were 1 s or 10 s. A longer cold still on the target was cut short; a longer incoming cold did not lengthen the freeze.
- **Official**: gamedata_const ba.cold "攻击速度下降30，如果在持续时间内再次受到寒冷效果则会变为冻结" (no length). PRTS 术语释义 寒冷 (id ba.cold): two kinds of cold that do not mix. 敌方 cold applied to a unit that already has 敌方 cold or 敌方 冻结 becomes 敌方 冻结. 友方 cold (operators and our summons, unless a page says otherwise) "始终需要两两一对产生友方冻结，持续时间取双方之中最高". Frozen-immune units never turn a cold into a freeze.
- **Cause**: `applyStatus`, on `cold` when the target already has cold and is not immune to `frozen`, called `applyStatus(target, 'freeze', { duration: COLD_FREEZE_DURATION })` with that constant 3. The cold buff itself already keeps the longer time (`addBuff` refresh `'extend'` → `timeLeft = max(old.timeLeft, new duration)`), and the incoming duration at that point is already after 抵抗.
- **Now**: the freeze uses that same max(remaining cold `timeLeft`, incoming duration). `resistApplied` stops 抵抗 from halving it a second time. Immune-to-frozen still skips the freeze; the colds still take the longer duration. `COLD_FREEZE_DURATION` (3) remains only when neither side has a duration (a non-positive, non-infinite time). A shorter second cold does not shorten a longer remaining cold or its freeze.
- **[ASSUMED]**: the engine has one catalogue cold, so an enemy-applied second cold uses the 友方 max as well. PRTS states 「持续时间取双方之中最高」 on the 友方 line only. The 敌方 trigger "already frozen, even with no cold left" is unchanged (still only when cold is still on).
- **Players notice**: 灵知's charged cast, 谢拉格's second gust, 不融冰 on an already-cold enemy freeze for the longer cold. A 1 s cold on top of a 10 s cold no longer freezes for 3 s, and does not wipe the remaining cold.
- Docs: SIM.md `cold` row. DESIGN §5's "freeze 3 s" sentence is the 0.1.2 text; this draft replaces it.
- Tests: `test/sim/feedback3-cold-freeze.test.js` (6 — 10 then 1 → freeze ~10 s; 1 then 8 → ~8 s; a 1 s cold after 6 s of a 10 s cold freezes for the ~4 s left; 抵抗 0.5 turns an incoming 10 s into 5 s before the max, and that 5 s is not halved again; frozen-immune gets no freeze; 吉兆飞鳞), `test/sim/combat.test.js` (the old "freeze lasts 3 s" case on two 5 s colds now expects 5 s).
- Interaction: 吉兆飞鳞 【失温坠落】 still fires. Cold 1 s then 1 s: the 0.05 s stun lands when that ~1 s freeze ends, not at 3 s. Cold 10 s then 1 s: no stun at 3.2 s; the 0.05 s stun lands when the 10 s freeze ends. That 0.05 s stun still drops it into the existing 8 s grounded mode.

### 23.28 「地面干员」 is the melee position, not the tile — 不屈, 战栗维式重锤, 休谟斯 回收利用 (community report 「不屈盟约效果高台干员也错误的吃到了」) — `content/support/index.js isGroundOp`, `content/bonds/addon/battle.js` (不屈), `content/items/battle.js` (战栗), `content/bands/battle.js` (`act1autochess_band18_buff`)

- **Seen**: a community report: the 不屈 bond's effect reaches 高台干员 — a ranged operator standing on a melee tile came back at once when knocked out. Measured (act2 m01, 不屈 tier 2 at 210 layers, p = 1): 普罗旺斯 (狙击) and 阿罗玛 (术师) on the melee tile (9,4) were back at once and gave every operator +5 SP; 歌蕾蒂娅 (钩索师, melee) on the 高台 (10,4) stayed down.
- **Official**: 地面干员 / 高台干员 name the deploy class (position MELEE / RANGED), on any tile. Game data: this mode's map card 模拟战场演变·模式五 「场地右侧的射击台变为可以部署高台干员和地面干员的地面」 (a tile both classes may use), the 山脊 device 「可将高台干员部署于其上」, a module 「所有地面干员阻挡数+2」 (only the melee class blocks), 「地面位干员阻挡数之和」; training CH-2 「即使在地面作战时，高台干员也…」 (a ranged operator on a ground tile is still a 高台干员); PRTS corrected 琴柳's 地面干员 to 地面位干员. The three effects worded 地面干员 in this mode: 不屈 (both lines: 「地面干员被击倒时，有…概率立刻重新部署」 and 「<在场3名不同【不屈】干员>地面干员被击倒时使场上所有干员技力+5」), 战栗维式重锤 (「若携带者是地面干员则攻击时有10%概率使目标战栗2秒」), 休谟斯 回收利用 (「地面干员技能结束时使周围四格随机1名干员获得3点技力」).
- **Cause**: `isGroundOp` (support/index.js) read `unit.ground`, the engine's tile flag set at deployment (low tile, not elevated) — §22.6 marked that reading [ASSUMED].
- **Now**: `isGroundOp(u)` = an operator whose `def.position` is MELEE, whatever tile it stands on. Its only callers are the three effects above, so all of them follow: 歌蕾蒂娅 on a 高台 is a 地面干员 (不屈 brings her back, the 战栗 proc and 回收利用 work), a ranged operator on a melee tile (this mode lets any ranged operator stand there: PRTS 帮助 「所有行动内远程干员可部署在近战位」) never is. `unit.ground` keeps every tile reading: blocking, the enemies' 地面单位 targeting (the 只攻击地面单位 enemy, ground-only boss picks, 污染秽蚀's 高地 rate), 赫默's module 「治疗地面单位」 and 隐德来希's 血镰 「1名其他地面单位」 (worded 地面单位, left on the tile), 焰影苇草 S2 「部署在地面的干员」 and the 推击手 远程位 refund (tile wording). Other 地面干员 texts already read the position: 新约能天使 S3 「将一名再部署时间最长的地面干员部署至该处」 (`def.position !== 'RANGED'`); 陈's 以己之长 says 所有干员 since PRTS's 70→100-layer patch (it once said 高台干员).
- **Players notice**: a 狙击 / 术师 / 医疗 / 辅助 knocked out on a melee tile no longer comes back through 不屈 nor gives its +5 SP; 歌蕾蒂娅 on a 高台 does both, and her 战栗维式重锤 procs there.
- Docs: §22.6's last bullet (the tile reading) now points here; research 02 §3.19 (不屈: melee position, any tile) in .md and .json (→ data/bonds.json `indomShip.spec`, rebuilt offline; the only data change); code comments (support, 不屈, 战栗, 回收利用, 蒂比's 起飞 in kits/tier2.js).
- Tests: `test/content/feedback3-ground-ops.test.js` (real chess on act2 m01: 歌蕾蒂娅 on the 高台 back at once with +5 SP, 普罗旺斯 on the melee tile stays down with no SP, 歌蕾蒂娅 on the ground as before; 战栗 from a melee carrier on a 高台, none from a ranged carrier on a melee tile; 回收利用 likewise — all three fail on the old tile reading), `test/content/bonds_addon.test.js` (不屈: the old "elevated → no" case now redeploys; a ranged operator on a melee tile does not, no tier-2 SP).

### 23.29 叙拉古 6 rolls on every 普通伤害 hit, not only on normal attacks (community report 「叙拉古盟约真伤概率数值没有正确递增成长」, GitHub #79) — `content/bonds/core.js siracusaRolls / installSiracusa`, `kits/tier6.js whitw2` (S3 pulse tag), `kits/tier2.js` (拉普兰德 module), `kits/tier3.js` (忍冬 追凶)

- **Seen**: a community report: 叙拉古's true-damage chance does not grow as it should; GitHub #79 (closed, still true): 荒芜拉普兰德 S3 「咬了一整个技能也没出」. Measured on 0.1.3 before the fix: 966 of 2324 member hits never rolled (619 S3 pulses, 255 S3 drone attacks, 92 德克萨斯 剑雨 hits); a whole S3 rolled nothing, since she makes no attack of her own then.
- **Official**: PRTS 盟约记录 叙拉古: 「※仅在造成普通伤害时尝试造成来源为干员自身的真实附加伤害和恐惧」, 「每次造成普通伤害时尝试触发，基础概率0.139%…至多720次」 (the PRD rule, a counter shared by the members, unchanged). PRTS 伤害分类: 普通伤害 is the attack type NORMAL — 「站内省略伤害的攻击类型时，默认为此伤害类型」, and group damage stays 普通伤害 unless it is 溅射 (SPLASH); the other types are 持续伤害 (BUFF, 「（不依赖攻击行为的）每秒造成……」) and 附加伤害 (ADDITION). Per member: 荒芜拉普兰德 S3 备注 「该技能释放的浮游单元造成的伤害不属于普通攻击/技能直接伤害」 (no attack, no skill hit — still 普通伤害) and 「持续法术伤害…不可叠加」 for the per-second area hit; 缄默德克萨斯 S1 「伤害分类为法术持续伤害」; 拉普兰德's module 「造成预计算的法术附加伤害」; 忍冬 追凶 「伤害类型为法术附加伤害」; 德克萨斯 剑雨, 阿罗玛 S1 / S2, 忍冬 S1 / S2, 伺夜 S3 and 缄默德克萨斯 S3 carry no class note (→ 普通伤害).
- **Cause**: `installSiracusa` rolled only on `dmg.isAttack && !dmg.isSplash` — the main target of a normal attack.
- **Now**: `siracusaRolls(dmg)` — every damage instance a member deals to an enemy rolls unless it is 溅射 (`isSplash`), 持续 (tags `dot` / `periodic`), 附加 (tag `addition`), an item proc (tag `item`), a bond's own damage (tags `bond:*`: this proc, 卡西米尔's rider), element damage, 无来源 (`sourceless`: 坚守's thorns) or a 流失. So 荒芜拉普兰德's S3 drone attacks, 德克萨斯 剑雨 (both hits on every enemy), 缄默德克萨斯's deploy bursts and sword rain, 阿罗玛's and 忍冬's skill hits and 伺夜 S3's bonus roll too. Kits: the S3 area hit gets the tag `dot` (it is 持续伤害, so it does not roll — and 锡人's 凋敝魂灵, 「受到的持续伤害提高」, now raises it, as PRTS's wording implies); 拉普兰德's module rider and 忍冬's 追凶 get `addition`. The 隐匿 window, the PRD counter (shared, 0.139 % steps, sure by 720), 5000 + 50 × L and the 3 s fear are unchanged.
- **[ASSUMED]**: item procs (天马之枪, 铳骑之威, 海沟实验体, 耶拉冈德之泪, 家族徽章's strike) and the bonds' riders do not roll — PRTS gives them no class; we read them as 附加. The S3 area hit is not 普通伤害 by PRTS's own wording 「持续法术伤害」 (the 伤害分类 list of BUFF damage does not name it, but it says 「包括但不限于」). Other operators' 附加 / 持续 damage is only classified where a 叙拉古 member deals it (a 调和 operator at 叙拉古 6 rolls on everything not tagged).
- **Players notice**: with 6 叙拉古, procs come on skills too: 荒芜拉普兰德 S3 procs on her drone bites (one roll per bite; about 1 proc per 33 rolls on average), 德克萨斯 剑雨 can proc on each of its hits. A fight's proc count rises by the share of hits that used to be skipped.
- Open: PRTS 「※脱离隐匿10秒内若自身的异常效果/异常效果免疫发生变动将刷新加成倒计时」 (the 10 s window refreshes) is still not modelled. 卡西米尔 6's rider (「仅在造成普通伤害时附带此真实伤害」) has the same old filter (`isAttack`) — a separate fix.
- Docs: §22.9 (the drone damage now rolls; the area hit's `dot`), research 02 §3.7 in .md and .json (→ data/bonds.json `siracusaShip.spec`, rebuilt offline; the only data change), code comments (core.js header, whitw2).
- Tests: `test/content/feedback3-siracusa-rolls.test.js` (rng forced to 0 so every roll hits: the classification case by case — attack, skill hit, drone attack, unclassed talent hit roll; 溅射, 持续, 附加, item proc, another bond's rider, element, 无来源 do not; 荒芜拉普兰德 S3 over 10 s: procs = drone attacks, pulses tagged `dot` and never rolled; 德克萨斯 剑雨: 4 hits on 2 enemies → 4 procs — all three fail on the old filter); `bonds_core.test.js` 叙拉古 tests unchanged and passing.

### 23.30 Mode-off bonds the player has members of show as grey 本局禁用 discs; the 成员 header counts the hand (community reports 「投资人等在休整区就能生效的盟约不生效」 / 「…不会触发斯卡蒂与异德的突袭」) — `match/bondsMeta.js offBondCounts / bondList`, `PlayerState.privateView`, `Match` public view, `ui/bondStrip.js`, `ui/gameLogic.js memberHeadCount`

- **Seen**: in 标准模拟 the bond strip hid 投资人, 奇迹, 突袭, 独行 and the other bonds the mode switches off, even when the player had their members. A 投资人 count of 3 (the members sitting in the 整备区) read 在场 3/3（含整备区） in the popup while the 成员 header said 0/n, because that header counted only operators on the board.
- **Official**: activity_table `modeDataDict.mode_single_funny` (标准模拟, and the same list on `mode_multi_funny`) `inactiveBondIdList`: 拉特兰 阿戈尔 卡西米尔 灵巧 奥术 奇迹 投资人 突袭 独行 绝技. Those bonds never activate. 投资人 / 远见 / 奇迹 count `BOARD_AND_DECK` (the board and the hand, not the 5 temporary slots); 突袭 counts the board. The grey disc is this remake's mark for a bond the mode never activates (the briefing already drew one with ✕); the official mode simply leaves the bond off.
- **Cause**: `computeBonds` skips `modeInactiveBonds`, and the views listed only what `computeBonds` returned, so a bond with members and no effect was invisible. `BondPopup`'s 成员 numerator was `members.filter(onBoard)`.
- **Now**: `computeBonds` still omits them — the battle input (`bondSnapshot` of `ps.bonds`) never sees them, so they still cannot activate. `offBondCounts` counts the inactive bonds the player has members of the same way (`rawCount`: 投资人 / 奇迹 include the hand) and `bondList(..., { off })` appends `{ bondId, count, active: false, tier: 0, layers, off: true }` after the live bonds, on `m.private` and `m.public`. The strip draws that entry last as a grey disc with ✕ and 本局禁用 under it (no stack count). An inactive bond with no members is left out. A mode whose `inactiveBondIds` is empty (`offBondCounts` → null; 险境 and the rest) is unchanged. The 成员 header uses `memberHeadCount`: for a bond with `countsHand` (投资人 远见 奇迹) a row in the hand counts, a row only in the temporary slots does not, and one operator on the board and in the hand counts once.
- **[ASSUMED]**: 调和's +1 is not added to a 本局禁用 bond's count. The bond never activates, so the +1 would only inflate a count that does nothing; the tooltip's 「N 名成员」 is the raw member count.
- **Players notice**: in 标准模拟, holding a 投资人 (even only in the 整备区) or fielding 斯卡蒂 / 异德 shows that bond at the end of the strip, grey, marked 本局禁用, instead of it vanishing. Opening it, the 成员 number matches 在场, hand included. The fight is unchanged: those bonds still do not fire.
- Docs: DESIGN §8.3 (the `off` entries come last; the pinned bonds-array sentence is unchanged), DESIGN §20.15 (m.public passes `offBondCounts`), META.md (the same list), PLAYING.md (本局禁用 on the strip, and the 成员 header counts the 整备区).
- Tests: `test/match/feedback3-off-bonds.test.js` (标准: 投资人 in hand and 突袭 on the board are `off` entries and absent from `computeBonds` / `bondSnapshot`; a hand member counts for 投资人, not for 突袭, not from the temporary slots, and not twice; a mode with an empty inactive list returns null and those bonds stay in `computeBonds`), `test/ui/gameLogic.test.js` (`memberHeadCount`, `bondMembers` `inHand`, `sortBonds` puts `off` last), `test/ui/feedback1b-bonds.test.js` (three 奥术 on the board: still absent from `ps.bonds`, present on `m.private` as `off: true`, the chip still says 本局禁用).

### 23.31 An item gained as 休整期结束 waits in hand and merges at the next prep start (维多利亚's 战栗维式重锤; owner's decision 2026-10-04) — `match/PlayerState.js acquireItem`, `match/effectsMeta.js` (`_deferItemMerge`), `match/Match.js enterPrep`, `content/bonds/core.js payHammers`

- **Seen**: 维多利亚 pays a 战栗维式重锤 when a 25-layer milestone is crossed, including at 休整期结束. A player who already had one equipped went into the fight without it: the new copy had merged at once, the equipped copy was detached, and the golden hammer sat in the hand through the combat.
- **Official**: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 (the in-game tip, also `autoChessData.gameTipsList`): 「两件同名装备可以合成一件更强力的装备」 and 「已拥有2件同一初始装备时…自动合并」. 维多利亚 (bonds.json `layer` 25, `count` 1, pool `pool_equip_vict`): 「每叠加25层获得1件带有随机效果的维式重锤」. Nothing there says the merge has to finish in the same moment the prep ends, or that an equipped copy comes off for a fight that has not started. The item is `chess_item_2_03_e_a` 战栗维式重锤, golden `chess_item_2_03_e_b`, `upgradeNum` 2.
- **Cause**: `payHammers` runs on `onPrepEnd` (`HAMMER_HOOKS`). `grantItem` → `acquireItem` calls `completesItemMerge` / `_mergeItem` immediately, and `_mergeItem` detaches an equipped copy of the same item. `Match.endPrep` dispatches `onPrepEnd` and only then starts the fight, so the detach landed before combat.
- **Now**: while `onPrepEnd` is on the dispatch stack (the hook itself, and a grant nested under it — the next owed milestone is paid from the grant's `onGain`), `acquireItem` stows the item (hand, else temp, the existing stow rules) and does not merge, even if an identical normal copy is already in the hand, the temp slots or equipped. `enterPrep` runs `checkItemMerges` once before `onPrepStart`, so the pair merges before the player acts in the next prep, not in `endPrep` of the prep that granted it. A buy, an `onPrepStart` grant, an `onLayers` grant during combat and every other path still merge at once. Hand and temp both full keeps 「整备区已满，获得的装备已销毁」: the new item is destroyed and the equipped copy is not replaced by a golden one in its slot. At the next prep there is then nothing waiting to merge, so the equipped normal hammer is still there.
- **[ASSUMED]**: the deferral is every item granted during that `onPrepEnd` dispatch, not only 维多利亚's hammers. The owner's sentence is 「an item that arrives at 休整期结束」. Two milestones caught up at that moment both wait: the nested `onGain` payment is inside the same stack, so it does not merge the first copy early.
- **Players notice**: the hammer already on an operator stays on for the fight that is about to start; the new one sits in the 整备区 (or the temporary slots, if the hand was full). The golden hammer appears when the next prep starts. If the hand and the temporary slots were both full, the new hammer is destroyed and the equipped one is untouched, including at the next prep. Two hammers paid at the same prep end both wait; the next prep merges those two with each other and leaves the equipped one on the operator.
- Docs: META.md §2.5 (the auto-merge sentence now names this exception), PLAYING.md (同名装备: 休整期结束 waits, other gains still merge at once).
- Tests: `test/match/feedback3-prep-end-item.test.js` (one equipped 战栗维式重锤, 维多利亚 pays another at prep end: still equipped in the battle input, second copy in hand, no golden; the next prep has the golden and no normal. Two milestones: three normals through the fight, then one golden plus the equipped copy. Hand and temp full: two 「整备区已满，获得的装备已销毁」, equipped copy unchanged after the next prep. A buy during prep merges at once. `onLayers` during combat merges at once). `test/content/bonds_core.test.js` (the catch-up payment on `onPrepStart` still merges) and `test/match/feedback1b-items.test.js` (every other gain path) still pass.

### 23.32 乌尔比安 S3 【移动】 and 【返回】 are free redeploys (community report 「乌尔比安三技能结束后不属于再部署」) — `Battle.moveRedeploy`, `kits/tier5.js`, `tokens.js` 从不混淆的方向, `devices.js`, `render/interp.js`, `render/spine.js`

- **Seen**: his S3 only changed his tile (`Battle.relocate`). Deploy effects never saw the 【移动】 or the 【返回】 — 迅捷作战粮, 突袭手雷, 卡西米尔 / 叙拉古 / a garrison `<部署时>` — and the return did not empty his SP. A knock-out, 不屈 and 阿戈尔's revive must not see either move.
- **Official**: PRTS 乌尔比安 S3 备注 — both steps are 【移动】; "【移动】后仅继承下列效果：技能进度、第二天赋叠加层数"; the marker is the 【返回】 flag; "【返回】时将清空技力，但仍可以享受后续由其他效果提供的技力". PRTS 术语释义 【移动】 (ba.addash): "不退场，以当前血量在目标位置部署", "本质上为一次特殊的撤退-再部署行为", "因移动撤退时不会积累再部署惩罚", "可以通过移动行为多次触发部署时触发的效果", "部署时将清空技力". His skill is the special case: the outbound move keeps 技能进度, and only the 【返回】 names the clear. While he blocks, the anchor stays on his own tile (§22.3, unchanged): no marker, no new deployment.
- **Cause**: `relocate` keeps the same `deploySeq` / `deployedAt` and emits nothing.
- **Now**: `moveRedeploy` does `relocate`'s checks, then a new deployment (`deploySeq` / `aggroSeq` / `deployedAt`) and `deploy { initial:false, move:true }`. No `die` / `death`, so no knock-out, no redeploy timer, no cost. `clearSp` empties SP before the deploy handlers (the 【返回】, and the marker's fallback). The outbound move does not. HP ratio and buffs stay, including the 阿戈尔 devour gains (owner's deviation, DESIGN §22.3 — officially only 技能进度 and the second talent's stacks carry). A 【移动】 does not zero terrain memory (`ctx.move`), so the old tile's 气流 / 烟尘 is not stranded. The client snaps a unit whose deploy animation starts between snapshots, and a skill that ends on that deploy lets the Start clip finish.
- **[ASSUMED]**: a refused tile changes nothing (no 强制撤退, and a 【返回】 that cannot land does not 击倒 him). His own S3 never takes that branch — the note says the check can always fall back to his tile — so only a direct `moveRedeploy` onto an illegal tile hits it. The glossary's "部署完成后立刻流失一次生命值以匹配移动前的生命比例" and the return's 0.333 s 无敌 / 不可阻挡 are not separate events: the HP ratio is kept on the same body. The return runs in `onEnd`, while the skill's mods are still applied ("技能结束后" would be the other reading); nothing that reaches him on that deploy reads those stats. 大帝 加急调派's permanent redeploy stack grows once per deployment, so a move adds one stack ("每次部署后"); that was already [ASSUMED] cumulative.
- **Players notice**: after the skill he is back on his tile with empty SP plus whatever a deploy effect just gave (迅捷作战粮), the 突袭手雷 window counting from the landing, and his devour / 血脉 stacks still on him. 不屈 and an 阿戈尔 revive are not spent. A 傀儡师's 替身 standing nearby is not knocked out.
- **Docs**: `docs/SIM.md` (`deploy` ctx `move`, `moveRedeploy`, the client's `deploy` event). Not DESIGN §23.
- **Tests**: `test/sim/feedback3-ulpia-move.test.js` (both moves bump `deploySeq` and emit `deploy { move:true }`; no death / die / FORCED_EXIT; knock-out count stays 0; SP cleared on the return only, then the ration's gift; devour buffs stay; 突袭手雷 re-arms; blocking still does not move; illegal tiles change nothing; out and back leaves 风丸's 替身 up and does not spend 不屈 or the 阿戈尔 charge; 卡西米尔 +20 % once per move, 叙拉古 refreshes without a second stack, 大帝 one stack per deployment). `test/render/feedback3-move-deploy.test.js` (headless, no browser). `test/docs-consistency.test.js`.

### 23.33 波登可's spore cloud radius is 0.9 (PRTS 备注) — `kits/tier1.js` `SPORE_RADIUS`

- **Seen**: the cloud used radius 1.2, commented as "no blackboard key; ≈ the 3×3". An enemy on the next tile (centre distance 1) was inside it.
- **Official**: PRTS 波登可 技能2 孢子扩散 备注 "※孢子群范围半径为0.9，可对空". No blackboard key carries the radius.
- **Cause**: the constant was a guess at the 3×3 around the vial.
- **Now**: `SPORE_RADIUS = 0.9`. The zone fx and the pulse both use it. Air units stay in range (可对空; `foesInRadius` does not drop them). An enemy whose centre is 1 tile from the vial is outside.
- **[ASSUMED]**: none. The centre is still the current target's position (the skill text "在周围", no landing note).
- **Players notice**: the cloud no longer reaches the tiles beside the enemy it landed on. Damage, 停顿 and silence on the enemy under the vial are unchanged.
- **Docs**: the kit comment cites the PRTS note. No SIM / DESIGN sentence named 1.2 for this cloud (the 1.2s elsewhere are 孽罪奇美拉, `batPct` and 沸血燎原).
- **Tests**: `test/content/kits_t1t2.test.js` 「1_13 波登可」 — the zone event's radius is 0.9; one pulse per second and the silence / 停顿 only on the enemy the vial landed on; the enemy one tile away and the far one take none.

### 23.34 琳琅诗怀雅 S3 shoots her coins when the purse reaches 10 (owner 2026-10-04) — `kits/tier3.js` 千金一掷

- **Seen**: with the purse full and a ground enemy in the coin range, `onTick` called `skill.end('manual')`. GitHub #89-1 reported that auto-close. Removing it left no way to shoot the coins, because a fight never calls a manual close.
- **Official**: the skill text 「携带此技能时金币上限为10」 and 「主动关闭技能时消耗所有金币」. PRTS 帮助 「通常不会自动关闭技能」 is the general line #89-1 cited.
- **Owner 2026-10-04**: the written cap is the close. At 10 the coins are shot. No separate 关闭技能 button.
- **Now**: while S3 is active and `coins >=` the S3 blackboard `sp` (10), `onTick` ends it with `manual` once a ground enemy is in the skill attack range (the record's range, one tile ahead — the same range as her attack). `onEnd` is unchanged: that reason spends every coin on the ground enemies of range 2-4 and the ones she blocks, then pushes. Below the cap, or with nobody in the attack range, it stays open and the coins stay. `skill.stop()` (`reason: 'stopped'`) still does not spend.
- **Owner 2026-10-04** (second call): do not shoot into an empty attack range. An enemy only in the coin-mark 2-4 grid does not trigger it.
- **Players notice**: she double-attacks and collects coins until the tenth, then waits until an enemy walks into the tile in front of her. That is when the burst fires.
- **Docs**: the kit comment. No pinned SIM / DESIGN sentence described the auto-close.
- **Tests**: `test/sim/feedback3-swire-s3.test.js` (9 coins and an enemy, 6 s, still open; 10 coins, the enemy is hit and the purse is empty; 10 coins and no enemy, ends, spends, no hit, no error; a manual close at 4 still spends). `test/sim/feedback1d-push.test.js` 「琳琅诗怀雅 S3」 (the radial push, one coin).

### 23.35 高台 only for elite 歌蕾蒂娅 carrying HOK-Y (owner's decision 2026-10-04) — `shared/highGround.js`, `match/board.js placeClass`, `PlayerState._placementOf`, `ui/gameLogic.js piecePosition`, `match/bot.js planLayoutSteps`, `tools/build-data.mjs`

- **Seen**: 0.1.2 §22.6 set `chess.json placement: 'all'` on every melee record whose branch trait contains 「可以放置于远程位」 — 歌蕾蒂娅, 崖心 and 见行者, normal and elite — so `positionClass` let all of them stand on a 高台. The owner (2026-10-04) says that reading is wrong.
- **Official**: character_table still writes that line on the 钩索师 (歌蕾蒂娅 `char_474_glady`, 崖心 `char_173_slchan`) and the 推击手 (见行者 `char_4036_forcer`). PRTS 新人入门 says both branches deploy on 高台 and ground. PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §战斗部署 switches off 部署效果 ("携带Y模组的教官仍无法部署至高台位"). No table row names HOK-Y as the prep exception. The module id is the data: `chess_char_4_12_b.modules` lists `uniequip_003_glady` typeName `HOK-Y` name 淡金坠饰 (not the default; the default is `uniequip_002_glady` HOK-X 执政官手镜). 崖心's only module is HOK-X `uniequip_002_slchan`. The golden record is the elite (`chess_char_4_12_b`, `isGolden`); there is no further copy.
- **Cause**: §22.6 trusted the trait line. It is copied onto every variant, including ones the owner refuses.
- **Now**: build-data does not emit `placement`. At place time, `meleeOnHighGround(rec, moduleId)` is true only when the record is golden, `charId` is `char_474_glady`, and the equipped module (the player's loadout, defaults included) is `uniequip_003_glady`. That widens `placeClass` / the client's `piecePosition` to every deployable tile. A normal record, 崖心, 见行者, HOK-X, `none`, and a missing module stay ground-only. The same check is what `g.move`, swaps, `_evictIllegal`, the merge-tile legality, the audit and the invariants use. The client lights the same tiles. The bot plans with `placeClass`. Owner 2026-10-04: when a 高台 tile's range covers an enemy-road tile, HOK-Y 歌蕾蒂娅 is planned there even if the ground is free; otherwise she stays on the ground. Everyone else may not use a 高台. She is still a melee blocker in the lineup (`basePositionClass`). The battle is unchanged: position stays MELEE, so on a 高台 she attacks and blocks nothing. The loadout is locked after 干员调配, so the module does not change under a piece already on a tile.
- [ASSUMED]: the sim's automatic searches (突袭 landing, summon / device tiles, `grid.canStand` without `ranged`) still treat every MELEE unit, including HOK-Y 歌蕾蒂娅, as ground-only. A prep placement on a 高台 is the board tile the battle deploys and redeploys on; those searches do not hunt a 高台 for her. There is no ground-only tile that refuses a ranged operator (ranged chess may use melee tiles); a non-deployable tile still refuses them.
- **Tests**: `test/match/board.test.js` (the class table), `test/match/feedback1-placement.test.js` (the operator × module × tile table, client highlights, the swap, the battle input on (10,4), the bot plan), `test/ui/gameLogic.test.js` (the drag mirror). `node tools/build-data.mjs --offline` drops `placement` and nothing else in `data/`.
- docs: DESIGN §3, DATA (`placement`), PLAYING §4, META (`board.js`), research 03 C2. §22.6 itself is left for the integrator to replace with this section.

### 23.36 Watching another board during prep follows its moves (GitHub #87) — `Match.watch`, `Match._notifyPrepScouts`, `ui/screens/game.js`

- **Seen**: GitHub #87 — in the deploy phase, the board you opened with 前往查看 does not follow the other player's moves. Leaving and entering again shows the new layout.
- **Official**: research 09 §3.1 — a teammate's board in prep is read-only, including their pen. PRTS was not re-fetched for this report; the live update is what the report asks of the client, and the owner filed it as a bug.
- **Cause**: `g.watch 'n:<pid>'` while no battle field is up deleted the viewer from `watchers` and sent one `prepFieldMeta` (`m.field`, `prep: true`). Later `g.move` only refreshed `m.private` for the player who moved. The client places that one `m.field` and had nothing further to enter.
- **Now**: the scout stays in `watchers` under `n:<pid>`. When that player's board changes (a move, a facing, equipment on a deployed piece), `flush` sends `prepFieldMeta` again to everyone scouting it, including a spectator seat. A shop or funds change does not. Opening the watch still sends the current board at once. Combat start still clears `watchers` and assigns live fields, so a spectator's battle watch is the same path as before. A prep `m.field` with no units still places an empty board, so a withdrawn lineup does not leave the old pieces up. [ASSUMED] the signature is the deployed pieces only (uid, id, tile, facing, equipped items); a hand-only change is not pushed, which matches a board view.
- **Tests**: `test/match/prep-watch.test.js` (seat B moves in prep, seat A's `m.field` and a spectator's gain the piece before combat; freezing the shop does not). `test/match/spectator.test.js` kept.

### 23.37 折射 reads as off while the enemy is silenced — `ui/abilityLines.js`, `ui/detailPanel.js` EnemyDetail, `render/style.js statusIconKey`, `render/units.js`, `shared/protocol.js unitStatsEntry`

- **Seen**: silencing a 深池 enemy turns the 折射 RES bonus off (the sim already removes `ab:refraction`). The enemy card still prints the 折射 line in the active colour, and the status icon does not show that the ability is off.
- **Official**: the handbook line is `<$ba.refraction>折射</>` with `format: "SILENCE"` (`data/enemies.json`, 深池侦察兵 `enemy_1166_dusbr` and the other 折射 enemies). PRTS 异常效果 沉默: the unit cannot use abilities flagged that way. The RES rule is unchanged.
- **Cause**: `EnemyDetail` always drew `abilities[]`. `statusIconKey` had no entry for `ab:refraction`, and nothing looked at silence when choosing icons.
- **Now**: `unitStatsEntry` carries `silenced` from the aggregated flags. A SILENCE-format line (折射, and any other line with that format) gets `is-off` — grey text, the term underline dropped — while `live.silenced` is set. The 折射 buff maps to a status icon and `statusIconSuppressed` leaves it out of the row while a `silence` status is up. The sim's RES rule is untouched. A card with no live stats (the prep preview, a handbook line) stays fully lit: nobody is silenced yet.
- [ASSUMED]: every `format: "SILENCE"` line greys the same way, not only the word 折射. The icon is dropped rather than drawn grey.
- **Tests**: `test/ui/refraction-view.test.js` (the line and the icon rule; no browser). `test/match/unitStats.test.js` (the new `silenced` field).

### 23.38 A granted chess is named, and the new card flashes — `effectsMeta.js grantChess`, `render/app.js setPrep`

- **Seen**: 歌蕾蒂娅's row trait grants 斯卡蒂 / 幽灵鲨 / 深巡 with no notice. 余's SERVER_MOST_BOND is the same. The card appears in the hand with no flash.
- **Official**: garrison text 「获得1个斯卡蒂、幽灵鲨或深巡」 / 「随机获得1名当前人数最多盟约的干员」. No official toast wording was fetched; the line is the owner's.
- **Cause**: `grantChess` acquired the piece and did not toast. `setPrep` flashed `fx.deploy` only for a new board piece.
- **Now**: a successful `grantChess` toasts 「名字：获得X」 — the garrison's operator (歌蕾蒂娅, 余), the item's name, the band's effect name, or the choice card's name. `opts.toast === false` skips it, so a caller that already says the same thing is not doubled (nothing in the tree did). A full hand still only gets the existing 整备区已满 warning. A new hand or temp card, after the first prep layout, uses the existing `fx.deploy` flash — the same one a new board piece already gets. Not a new animation.
- [ASSUMED]: the toast names the piece actually owned, so a grant that immediately promotes names the elite. A shop buy is not a `grantChess` and does not toast; a bought card is still a new hand uid, so it gets the same flash.
- **Tests**: `test/content/garrisons_meta.test.js` (歌蕾蒂娅 and 余, the toast text equals a chess now in hand).

### 23.39 An emote manifest that times out shows the glyph — `data.js` `local` / `assets`, `ui/emotes.js` EmoteArt

- **Seen** (GitHub #99): the 交流 panel's cells are empty, including the stand-in icon. The screenshot is the size of the emote plate.
- **Official**: none. The 36 pictures and the glyph are already the §22.5 art rule. The timeout length is not from a table.
- **Cause**: `EmoteArt` draws an empty box while `local` or `assets` is `idle` or `loading`, and the glyph only once both have settled to `ready` or `missing`. `createDataStore` had no clock, so a fetch that never returned left every cell blank for the session. This is the data-store fetch (`useData('local','assets')`), not the separate image cache in `assets.js`.
- **Now**: one attempt at `local` or `assets` is abandoned after `ART_MANIFEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. A timeout or any other failure sets that file `missing` and notifies immediately, which is the existing glyph path. The status stays `missing` through the retry wait (going back to `loading` would blank the button again). A later attempt that succeeds notifies `ready` and the picture replaces the glyph. A 404 or bad JSON is still not retried. Chess and the other text files are not on this clock and still stay `loading` across retries.
- [ASSUMED]: 8 seconds. A response that arrives after that attempt's clock is ignored; the retry is a new request, and the in-flight fetch is not aborted. After the two existing retries the glyph stays until the next `invalidate` or page load.
- **Tests**: `test/ui/emotes.test.js` (stub timer, no network: hung `local` is `missing` before the retry wait, then `ready`; a thrown fetch is the same; a timeout with no retry and a 404 stay `missing`; `chess` arms no timer). `test/ui/playtest3.test.js` data-store retry and `test/client-static.test.js` data.js still pass. No browser render: `EmoteArt` already picks the glyph from `missing`.

---

## 24. Community reports after 0.1.3

Reports after the 0.1.3 release. Each was checked against the official data and PRTS; anything with no source is marked [ASSUMED]. Where each is handled: a skill clip with no Begin and no own Idle plays once (德克萨斯 S2 剑雨) → §24.1.

### 24.1 A skill clip with no Begin and no own Idle plays once — 德克萨斯 S2 剑雨 (player report) — `render/spine.js SpineActor.setSkill / update` (`SKILL_CLIP_MIN`, `skillClipOnce`, `skillEndPending`)

- **Seen** (player report after 0.1.3): 德克萨斯's S2 剑雨 casts with no skill animation — she stays in her idle through the whole cast.
- **Official** [DATA]: her Front model has a single skill clip, `Skill` (2.167 s) — no `Skill_Begin`, no `Skill_Idle`, no `Skill_End`, so the manifest roles are `anims.skill {begin: null, loop: 'Skill', end: null}` and `anims.skills["0"] / ["1"]` are the same clip. Both her skills are instant (`durationType NONE`, duration 0; S1 冲锋号令·γ型, S2 剑雨). The official plays that `Skill` clip on the cast.
- **Cause**: `setSkill(true)` played a clip only when the skill had a Begin clip; with `begin === null` it fell through to `else if (this.mode === 'base') this._play(this._baseName())` — the idle. The loop clip was reachable only through an attack (`_attackClip`), and an instant skill makes no attack inside its activation window: sim `skills.js` fires `['skill', id, 1]` and `['skill', id, 0]` in the same tick (`activate` → `end('instant')`) and only holds the anim code `SKILL` for 0.5 s (`snapshot.js animOf`), so the actor was back on its idle before a frame passed.
- **Now**: a skill clip with neither a Begin nor its own Idle (its loop is not its idle, and it is not borrowed from the attack clip: `via !== 'attack'`) plays that clip once for its own length — `max(SKILL_CLIP_MIN 0.2 s, dur(loop))` — as the skill's own window; a `setSkill(false)` inside that window only marks the transition, so when the window ends the actor plays the skill's End clip (if it has one) or the idle. An instant skill therefore shows its animation instead of a one-frame flash, and a longer skill of the same shape still rests in its idle between attacks while its attacks use the loop, exactly as before. Untouched: a Begin clip with its queued idle / loop, a skill with an idle clip of its own (§23.13), `via: 'attack'` clips, `loop === idle` stances and enemies.
- **Scale**: 90 such clips on 61 Front models (96 clips have no Begin at all — the 6 with an own Idle keep the §23.13 path); 57 of the 90 belong to instant skills.
- **A PASSIVE skill reaches none of this through `activate()`**: `Skill.activate()` returns early for `kind === 'passive'` (`sim/skills.js`); a passive goes through `_startPassive()` (active + mods + `onStart`). Three of the 57 instant clips are passives in the data — 琳琅诗怀雅 S1 仗义疏财 / S2 “见面礼” and 凯瑟琳 S1; 凯瑟琳 S1 turned out to be `kind: instant` in the sim (so it already casts), and the two 琳琅诗怀雅 ones now fire the same window from `_startPassive` — on at the deployment, off after `SKILL_ANIM_WINDOW` (0.5 s) while the passive itself stays active. So all 57 are live.
- **Deploy-time passives with a duration are left alone** (缄默德克萨斯 S1–S3 8–12 s, 野鬃 S1, 伊内丝 S3, 耀骑士临光 S2): the sim keeps a passive active until death, so how long its stance should show is a separate question. A clip that IS the normal attack clip (`anims.skill.loop === anims.attack.loop`: 缄默德克萨斯 S1, 砾, 普罗旺斯, 斯卡蒂 S2) is never played as a skill animation — the new branch requires a distinct clip, so a deploy-time passive on those shows no attack twitch.
- **Tests**: `test/render/feedback4-texas-skill-clip.test.js` (德克萨斯 S2: the clip plays, holds through the same-tick off, then the idle, with no base flash in between; two more real instant entries — `char_140_whitew` S1 日晷, `char_206_gnosis` S2 零度爆发; 银灰 S3 真银斩 — the same shape but long — rests without freezing on the clip's last frame and still attacks with the loop; the deploy-time passives 琳琅诗怀雅 S1/S2 hold their 1 s clip through the sim's 0.5 s window and then idle; a clip that is the attack clip falls through to the base clip; unchanged: 折桠 S2 begin → idle, 耀骑士临光 S3 own idle, 宴 begin → loop). `test/sim/skills.test.js` covers the sim side (the passive window opens at the deployment and closes 0.5 s later, the passive staying active; a timed passive fires nothing). `test/render/feedback3-skill-idle.test.js`, `feedback3-move-deploy.test.js`, `loadout-skill.test.js` and the full suite stay green.
